- entry[000] 0x002c-0x0072: Orphen | Ah, I'll never get any sleep in this storm!
- entry[001] 0x0072-0x00b2: Orphen | I've got a bad feeling | about this ship...
- entry[002] 0x00b2-0x00e6: Dortin | The weather is getting worse.
- entry[003] 0x00e6-0x017f: Volcan | Ha ha ha! | Do your worst, Ocean! | Raging tempests don't scare me. | I'm Lord Volcano Volcan, | the Masmaturian Bulldog! | Ohh...
- entry[004] 0x017f-0x019d: [vomits] Ughh...
- entry[005] 0x019d-0x0216: Dortin | Who would have guessed that | Volcan the invulnerable could be | laid low by a little sea sickness?
- entry[006] 0x0216-0x02ce: Volcan | Shaddup! | You know I've always | suffered from a weak constitution. | I'm too delicate for this\ufffdc | Ugh, | when is this old tub going | to get to Arvanrama?
- entry[007] 0x02ce-0x0309: Dortin | Not for at least | another three days.
- entry[008] 0x0309-0x034d: Volcan | What!? You gotta be kidding! | I want a refund!
- entry[009] 0x034d-0x0395: Dortin | Refund? | For what? | Orphen paid for our tickets.
- entry[010] 0x0395-0x03e3: Volcan | Of course he did! | He's lucky we let him in on the deal!
- entry[011] 0x03e3-0x0496: Dortin | Yeah, Orphen must have been pretty | desperate to buy that crazy story. | Easy money working as a bodyguard | for a rich merchant in Arvanrama? | Yeah, right!
- entry[012] 0x0496-0x0558: Hey... | Volcan... | You notice anything peculiar? | I don't think we're sailing | in the right direction... | We shouldn't have to go this far | out to sea just to go to Arvanrama.
- entry[013] 0x0558-0x05a4: Volcan | Ugh, stop the ship! | I'm getting off right here!
- entry[014] 0x05a4-0x05b8: [vomits]
- entry[015] 0x05b8-0x05f3: Dortin | Are we... | Are we on the right ship?
- entry[016] 0x05f3-0x0630: Sephy | Chaos Island... | Finally, I'm on my way.
- entry[017] 0x0630-0x0680: Will my plan work? | If it does work, | will the gods forgive me?
- entry[018] 0x0680-0x06dc: Rufus... | What should I do? | Dearest Rufus... | My one and only | true love.
- entry[019] 0x06dc-0x0737: Cleo | Okay, | Magnus... | You cross this line, | and I slug you. | And I mean it!
- entry[020] 0x0737-0x07a4: Magnus | Yes, Ma'am. | Don't worry, | I won't cross your precious line. | I'm not crazy, you know.
- entry[021] 0x07a4-0x07f5: Cleo | This is so unfair! Why do I | have to share a room with a boy!?
- entry[022] 0x07f5-0x08a4: Magnus | Well, the Master said | he needed to have a room to himself. | And we only booked two cabins... | Master said he didn't want to | share a room with a kid.
- entry[023] 0x08a4-0x08d2: Cleo | Who are you calling 'kid'!?
- entry[024] 0x08d2-0x094b: Magnus | Not me... | Uh, I mean, I didn't... | It was the Master... | Hey! | What was that? | I thought I...
- entry[025] 0x094b-0x097d: Cleo | What? | I don't see anything.
- entry[026] 0x097d-0x09b6: Magnus | Just now... | I could | have sworn...
- entry[027] 0x09b6-0x09ec: Cleo | That's just rain hitting the window.
- entry[028] 0x09ec-0x0a0f: Magnus | I don't know...
- entry[030] 0x0a1b-0x0a41: Magnus | Ohhhhh... Master!!
- entry[031] 0x0a41-0x0a62: Cleo | Ohhhhh Orphen!!
- entry[032] 0x0a62-0x0a83: Orphen | What's wrong?
- entry[033] 0x0a83-0x0aa4: Magnus | M-m-monsters!
- entry[034] 0x0aa4-0x0ad9: Cleo | S-s-s-snakes! | Big, flying snakes!
- entry[035] 0x0ad9-0x0af6: Orphen | Monsters?
- entry[036] 0x0af6-0x0b63: Magnus | Big ones! They're all over the place! | Do something! | Before they tear the ship apart!
- entry[037] 0x0b63-0x0b76: Orphen | Damn!
- entry[038] 0x0b76-0x0b9c: Argh! | Take that!
- entry[039] 0x0b9c-0x0bd0: Magnus | Master! | Somebody's in trouble!
- entry[040] 0x0bd0-0x0be9: Magnus | Whoa!
- entry[041] 0x0be9-0x0c1c: Orphen | What in the world | are those!?
- entry[042] 0x0c1c-0x0c7d: Zeus | I know not what they are, | but they aren't friendly! | Get out of here, now!
- entry[043] 0x0c7d-0x0ca1: Cleo | We'll help you!
- entry[044] 0x0ca1-0x0cba: Magnus | Whoa!
- entry[045] 0x0cba-0x0cdc: Cleo | Where did he go?
- entry[046] 0x0cdc-0x0d25: Orphen | I don't know, | but now we | need to find another way out.
- entry[047] 0x0d25-0x0d63: Magnus | Hey! | Somebody ransacked our room!
- entry[048] 0x0d63-0x0dc2: Cleo | Yiiaa! | My luggage... It's gone! | Oooh! | My favorite outfit was in there!
- entry[049] 0x0dc2-0x0e27: Magnus | Cleo! The ship is about to sink, | and you're worried | about your dumb clothes?
- entry[050] 0x0e27-0x0ed0: Cleo | Magnus! | You're supposed | to be taking care of my baggage, | and now look what's happened! | Can't you do anything right? | This is all your fault!
- entry[051] 0x0ed0-0x0f04: Magnus | My fault!? Why is it my fault?
- entry[052] 0x0f04-0x0f2d: Orphen | Knock it off! | Let's go.
- entry[053] 0x0f2d-0x0f9a: Magnus | The minute I stepped on board | I just knew something | bad was going to happen.
- entry[054] 0x0f9a-0x0fcf: Cleo | You should of told us that earlier!
- entry[055] 0x0fcf-0x1011: Magnus | I did tell you earlier! | But you ignored me.
- entry[056] 0x1011-0x1089: Orphen | Shut up already! | There are monsters running everywhere, | and the ship could sink at any moment.
- entry[057] 0x1089-0x10ce: Cleo | Well, duh! | What are we | supposed to do about it?
- entry[058] 0x10ce-0x1116: Orphen | For now, | let's just get to the deck. | Look for an exit!
- entry[059] 0x1116-0x1137: Magnus | Uh-oh.
- entry[060] 0x1137-0x114e: Cleo | Yaaa!
- entry[061] 0x114e-0x1169: Orphen | Phhhht.
- entry[062] 0x1169-0x1197: Cleo | Look! | The crates are gone!
- entry[063] 0x1197-0x11b4: Orphen | Okay, Let's go!
- entry[064] 0x11b4-0x11e6: Magnus | Hey! That's dangerous!
- entry[065] 0x11e6-0x1242: Sephy | I'll be alright. You people should | get out as soon as possible too.
- entry[066] 0x1242-0x1258: Cleo | Wow!
- entry[067] 0x1258-0x12b3: Orphen | Shut up. | We got to get out! I don't | want to die here with you guys.
- entry[068] 0x12b3-0x12dc: Cleo | Yeah, the feeling is mutual!
- entry[069] 0x12dc-0x1327: Cleo | That guy who was here earlier, | I hope he's okay.
- entry[070] 0x1327-0x1344: Orphen | Never mind him!
- entry[071] 0x1344-0x138e: Magnus | Think that woman | made it up to the deck okay?
- entry[072] 0x138e-0x13e6: Orphen | Who knows? | Maybe we'll find her | if we ever make it out of here.
- entry[073] 0x13e6-0x140f: Cleo | Come on... | Let's get going!
- entry[074] 0x140f-0x1451: Cleo | Oh, no! | The stairs are | totally blocked off!
- entry[075] 0x1451-0x1486: Magnus | Whoever was here | is gone now.
- entry[076] 0x1486-0x14dd: Orphen | Hey! | You two! | Just what the hell | is going on here!?
- entry[077] 0x14dd-0x151f: Dortin | Uh, well, | we don't really know actually...
- entry[078] 0x151f-0x15b4: Volcan | Hey! | Dortin! | Hurry up | and get that food in the sack! | Try to get it all in there. | We're going to need all of this.
- entry[079] 0x15b4-0x1607: Orphen | Hey! | You oversized raccoons! | It's no time for stealing!
- entry[080] 0x1607-0x1622: Dortin | Ahhhhh!
- entry[081] 0x1622-0x1647: Volcan | Wha-what's that!?
- entry[082] 0x1647-0x167f: Orphen | Uh-oh! | Looks like we're in for a fight.

## scr/scr3.out

Header: pointer_table=0x2cf4-0x2fbc, footer=0xca14, records=177, text_records=176

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x005e: Allow Sephy to join your group? | choices: Yes / No
- entry[001] 0x005e-0x008f: Allow Zeus to join your group? | choices: Yes / No
- entry[002] 0x008f-0x00e8: Orphen | Magnus! | Cleo! | Damn! | What the hell | is going on around here?
- entry[003] 0x00e8-0x010c: Orphen | Magnus!
- entry[004] 0x010c-0x0128: Magnus | Master!
- entry[005] 0x0128-0x0142: Orphen | Ah...
- entry[006] 0x0142-0x01d0: Magnus | This is Sephy. | She was being attacked | by some monsters, | and I helped her escape. | Sephy, | this is my teacher.
- entry[007] 0x01d0-0x022c: Sephy | What in the world is going on? | I've never seen such horrible beasts!
- entry[008] 0x022c-0x02a2: Orphen | Okay, calm down. | I don't | really know | what's going on myself, but | I intend to find out.
- entry[009] 0x02a2-0x02ee: Sephy | Oh. I'm... | I'm sorry for being rude. | I'm | Sephy.
- entry[010] 0x02ee-0x0343: Orphen | Orphen. Nice to meet you. | Looks like | this ship has had it.
- entry[011] 0x0343-0x0375: Sephy | No! | Not when I'm so close...
- entry[012] 0x0375-0x0396: Sephy | That amulet!
- entry[013] 0x0396-0x03f8: Magnus | Master. We can't leave | Sephy here all by herself. | I will stay and protect her.
- entry[014] 0x03f8-0x0441: Orphen | Hmm. | Looks like you got | the hots for her.
- entry[015] 0x0441-0x04b6: Magnus | What? No, of course not! | What a silly thing to say! | This is no time for jokes, | Master!
- entry[016] 0x04b6-0x0537: Orphen | Whatever. | Anyway, she's going to need | more protection than you think. | You two better come with me.
- entry[017] 0x0537-0x0565: Magnus | All right! | Come on, | Sephy!
- entry[018] 0x0565-0x05c5: Orphen | You two | better wait here. | I'm gonna go | check on the others.
- entry[019] 0x05c5-0x05de: Magnus | Be careful!
- entry[020] 0x05de-0x061f: Cleo | Hey! | Orphen! | Where have you been?
- entry[021] 0x061f-0x0659: Orphen | Ah, Cleo. | You made it out safely?
- entry[022] 0x0659-0x0688: Cleo | Thanks to | this gentleman.
- entry[023] 0x0688-0x06a8: Orphen | Mercenary?
- entry[024] 0x06a8-0x0712: Zeus | That I am. | They call me | Zeus. | And unless I miss my mark, | you'd be a sorcerer.
- entry[025] 0x0712-0x0746: Orphen | That's right. | The name | is Orphen.
- entry[026] 0x0746-0x07df: Orphen | Well, Zeus, | Here's the deal: | We're in a bad | situation here. | We'll have a much better chance | with you on the team.
- entry[027] 0x07df-0x083d: Zeus | Aptly put. | I've always wanted to go into a fight | with a sorcerer by my side.
- entry[028] 0x083d-0x089b: Orphen | Look, | do me a favor | and watch this | brat for a little while.
- entry[029] 0x089b-0x08ca: Cleo | Who are you calling 'brat'!?
- entry[030] 0x08ca-0x0900: Orphen | I want to go check on the others.
- entry[031] 0x0900-0x0917: Zeus | Very well.
- entry[032] 0x0917-0x098f: Orphen | Let's go and check | the cabin area again. | Someone might still be | trapped down there.
- entry[033] 0x098f-0x09d5: Cleo | Hey, that's right... | Where's Volcan and Dortin?
- entry[034] 0x09d5-0x0a06: Orphen | Never mind about those two.
- entry[035] 0x0a06-0x0a1f: Cleo | What?
- entry[036] 0x0a1f-0x0a56: Orphen | Just ignore them if you see them.
- entry[037] 0x0a56-0x0aab: Magnus | Uh, okay. | Well, they probably | don't want our help anyway.
- entry[038] 0x0aab-0x0af5: Orphen | Look, | the ship could sink any time. | Let's get going!
- entry[039] 0x0af5-0x0b23: Magnus | Master, | look!!
- entry[040] 0x0b23-0x0b4d: Magnus | The mast is on fire!
- entry[041] 0x0b4d-0x0b98: Orphen | You two, find someplace safe. | I'll handle this one myself.
- entry[042] 0x0b98-0x0bc4: Cleo | What's going on!?
- entry[043] 0x0bc4-0x0bf5: Cleo | Oh, no! | The ship's sinking!
- entry[044] 0x0bf5-0x0c47: Orphen | You two, go find a safe hiding spot. | I'll handle this one myself.
- entry[045] 0x0c47-0x0c74: Magnus | \u201a\xa8\u017dt\u2014l | \u201a \u201a\xea\u201a\xf0\ufffdI
- entry[046] 0x0c74-0x0c8d: Cleo | \u2030\xbd\u201a\xe6\ufffdI
- entry[047] 0x0c8d-0x0cb8: Magnus | \u0192}\u0192X\u0192g\u201a\xaa\u201dR\u201a\xa6\u201a\xc4\u201a\xa2\u201a\xdc\u201a\xb7\ufffdI
- entry[048] 0x0cb8-0x0ce2: Cleo | \u2018\xe5\u2022\xcf\u201a\xe6\ufffdI | \u2018D\u201a\xaa\u2019\xbe\u201a\xde\u201a\xed\ufffdI
- entry[049] 0x0ce2-0x0d1b: Orphen | \u201a\xb1\u201a\xb1\u201a\xcd\u2030\xb4\u02c6\xea\ufffdl\u201a\xc5\ufffd\u2022\xaa\u201a\xbe\ufffdI" | \u02c6\xc0\u2018S\u201a\xc8\ufffd\u0160\u201a\xc9\u2030B\u201a\xea\u201a\xc4\u201a\xeb
- entry[050] 0x0d1b-0x0d5b: Orphen | Hey, | Kid! | Come down from there!
- entry[051] 0x0d5b-0x0d91: Orphen | What's | the matter with that dumb kid?
- entry[052] 0x0d91-0x0dab: Orphen | Huh?
- entry[053] 0x0dab-0x0e02: Sephy | Orphen... | don't you want to | help out a lady in need?
- entry[054] 0x0e02-0x0e65: Magnus | Master, | We can't leave Sephy here all by herself. | I will stay and protect her.
- entry[055] 0x0e65-0x0e8f: Sephy | Oh... | I see.
- entry[056] 0x0e8f-0x0ec0: Orphen | Sorry lady, maybe next time.
- entry[057] 0x0ec0-0x0ecf: Sephy | ...
- entry[058] 0x0ecf-0x0f1c: Orphen | Wait | here until I get back. | I'm gonna take a look around.
- entry[059] 0x0f1c-0x0f47: Sephy | Not interested?
- entry[060] 0x0f47-0x0f62: Orphen | No, | sorry.
- entry[061] 0x0f62-0x0faa: Sephy | By the way... Have you | seen that little boy?
- entry[062] 0x0faa-0x0fd0: Orphen | What little boy?
- entry[063] 0x0fd0-0x101f: Sephy | There was a small child earlier. | About 7 or 8 years old.
- entry[064] 0x101f-0x1059: Orphen | Sorry, | didn't see anyone like that.
- entry[065] 0x1059-0x108b: Sephy | Oh, well... | I hope he's all right.
- entry[066] 0x108b-0x10d0: Magnus | Master, | Don't you think we should help Sephy?
- entry[067] 0x10d0-0x1106: Orphen | Sorry, | not right | now.
- entry[068] 0x1106-0x1168: Magnus | Oh, well. | Master, what do you suppose | happened to the other passengers?
- entry[069] 0x1168-0x11cc: Orphen | There's not a single lifeboat left. | Looks like they jumped ship without us.
- entry[070] 0x11cc-0x121f: Magnus | You mean | we're trapped on the ship... | All by ourselves!?
- entry[071] 0x121f-0x12b3: Orphen | Don't feel too bad... | On a night like this, | we're safer here on the ship, | than in a tiny little boat | out there in the storm.
- entry[072] 0x12b3-0x12e8: Magnus | Why won't you help her!?
- entry[073] 0x12e8-0x1309: Orphen | Don't ask...
- entry[074] 0x1309-0x132f: Magnus | You're just mean!
- entry[075] 0x132f-0x1357: Orphen | You watch | your tongue!
- entry[076] 0x1357-0x139c: Magnus | By the way, | do you hear that, Master?
- entry[077] 0x139c-0x13b6: Orphen | No...
- entry[078] 0x13b6-0x13d7: Magnus | Oh. I thought I...
- entry[079] 0x13d7-0x1444: Zeus | Sometimes two men may succeed | where one will fail. | Will you join me, Master Orphen?
- entry[080] 0x1444-0x14cf: Zeus | Very well, | Master Orphen. | So be it. | Fear not for this young woman, | I shall guard her life with my own.
- entry[081] 0x14cf-0x14f0: Orphen | Thank you, | Zeus.
- entry[082] 0x14f0-0x154f: Zeus | Master Orphen. Do you think the | crew members have all been slain?
- entry[083] 0x154f-0x15d1: Orphen | Hmm. | I haven't seen any in a while. | But I haven't | seen any dead bodies either... | They must have escaped.
- entry[084] 0x15d1-0x1637: Zeus | Master Orphen, | there was a child playing a flute... | Have you seen him?
- entry[085] 0x1637-0x1665: Orphen | Nope, | haven't seen him.
- entry[086] 0x1665-0x16d8: Zeus | That is not good news... | I'll make those monsters pay | if they have murdered | an innocent child!
- entry[087] 0x16d8-0x17af: Cleo | Orphen, | The ship could sink at any time! | We have to do something! | Are you sure you won't | change your mind about Zeus? | I bet we could find a solution if we all worked together.
- entry[088] 0x17af-0x1807: Orphen | Sorry, not right now. | I'm going to go take a look around.
- entry[089] 0x1807-0x183a: Cleo | Ughh! | You're as stubborn as a mule!!
- entry[090] 0x183a-0x1871: Cleo | Did you change your mind, | Orphen?
- entry[091] 0x1871-0x18be: Orphen | No! I don't need | more people slowing me down.
- entry[092] 0x18be-0x18f6: Cleo | What!? You'd be the one | slowing him down!
- entry[093] 0x18f6-0x1946: Cleo | Hey, | Orphen... | Have you seen a kid with a flute?
- entry[094] 0x1946-0x1991: Orphen | Nope\ufffdc The only kiddie | I've seen around here is you.
- entry[095] 0x1991-0x19d6: Cleo | Oh, stop it! I hope he | wasn't eaten by the monsters...
- entry[096] 0x19d6-0x1a33: Orphen | Where am I? | I think I've been here before... | Am I dreaming?
- entry[097] 0x1a33-0x1a8a: Orphen | Ouch! | Okay, so it's not a dream. | So what the hell's going on?
- entry[098] 0x1a8a-0x1ad1: Orphen | Magnus! | Cleo! | Where are you!? | Answer me, damn it!
- entry[099] 0x1ad1-0x1b29: Orphen | What the hell? | Deja vu, again? | What is going on around here?
- entry[100] 0x1b29-0x1b54: Orphen | Hey, | Magnus!
- entry[101] 0x1b54-0x1b6d: Magnus | Ah!
- entry[102] 0x1b6d-0x1ba1: Orphen | Relax. | It's just me. Orphen.
- entry[103] 0x1ba1-0x1c0d: Magnus | Oh, | Master... | [sigh] | I'm glad you're here. | I keep hearing this spooky flute.
- entry[104] 0x1c0d-0x1c67: Orphen | What flute? I don't hear anything. | Something's fishy around here.
- entry[105] 0x1c67-0x1cd1: Magnus | I think so too! I mean... | There's no one else left on the ship! | Where did they all go?
- entry[106] 0x1cd1-0x1cfb: Orphen | What's that?
- entry[107] 0x1cfb-0x1d5f: Magnus | See, see! | That's it! | That's the flute! | Maybe it is a ghost or something.
- entry[108] 0x1d5f-0x1db1: Orphen | Hmm... | I better check it out. | Wait here. | I'll be right back.
- entry[109] 0x1db1-0x1ddc: Orphen | Hey! | Magnus!
- entry[110] 0x1ddc-0x1e18: Magnus | Oh, | it's you! | Did you find anyone?
- entry[111] 0x1e18-0x1e69: Orphen | No, | there was no one there... | Hey, have you seen Cleo?
- entry[112] 0x1e69-0x1eb8: Magnus | Yeah. She's making a raft... | Up near the bow somewhere.
- entry[113] 0x1eb8-0x1ee1: Orphen | Okay. | Don't go anywhere!
- entry[114] 0x1ee1-0x1f0c: Orphen | Hey! | Magnus!
- entry[115] 0x1f0c-0x1f46: Magnus | Oh, | It's you. | Did you find Cleo?
- entry[116] 0x1f46-0x1fb4: Orphen | Yeah, she's okay, | but her raft is in trouble! | Building is definitely not her forte.
- entry[117] 0x1fb4-0x200b: Magnus | Master, | we have to do something fast, | before the ship sinks!
- entry[118] 0x200b-0x202b: Orphen | Wait here!
- entry[119] 0x202b-0x2041: Magnus | Master!
- entry[120] 0x2041-0x2063: Orphen | Cleo!
- entry[121] 0x2063-0x20b5: Cleo | Oh, Orphen. | It's about time. | Where have you been, anyway?
- entry[122] 0x20b5-0x20e2: Orphen | Where's everybody else?
- entry[123] 0x20e2-0x212c: Cleo | How should I know? | Maybe they already left the ship.
- entry[124] 0x212c-0x2146: Orphen | Huh?
- entry[125] 0x2146-0x2197: Cleo | Come on and help me. | I'm making a raft so we can get away.
- entry[126] 0x2197-0x220b: Orphen | Always thinking about yourself, huh? | Are you gonna make rafts for all of us? (sarcastically)
- entry[127] 0x220b-0x226b: Cleo | You're one to talk! | Fine, be that way! | Don't expect to ride on MY raft!
- entry[128] 0x226b-0x2291: Orphen | Yeah, whatever...
- entry[129] 0x2291-0x22b0: Cleo | You big... | Jerk!
- entry[130] 0x22b0-0x22d8: Cleo | Ah, | Orphen.
- entry[131] 0x22d8-0x2308: Orphen | What're you doing, | Cleo?
- entry[132] 0x2308-0x2351: Cleo | What's it look like I'm doing? | I'm building a raft!
- entry[133] 0x2351-0x237c: Orphen | You call that a raft?
- entry[134] 0x237c-0x23d3: Cleo | Come on, Orphen. Help me. | Don't just stand there catching flies!
- entry[135] 0x23d3-0x23f0: Orphen | See ya.
- entry[136] 0x23f0-0x242e: Cleo | Orphen! | Hold on! | Where are you going?
- entry[137] 0x242e-0x245a: Orphen | Anywhere but here. | Bye bye.
- entry[138] 0x245a-0x249d: Cleo | Orphen, | stop goofing off and help out!
- entry[139] 0x249d-0x24d0: Orphen | Oh, of course, your highness.
- entry[140] 0x24d0-0x2538: Cleo | Magnus was bragging about how | he was protecting that woman. | He's so immature...
- entry[141] 0x2538-0x2567: Orphen | A little jealous, are we?
- entry[142] 0x2567-0x2625: Cleo | Over a twerp like Magnus? | Don't be stupid! All I'm saying is | that woman is strange... | She ran up that rope like a monkey. | And you saw that flimsy little costume!
- entry[143] 0x2625-0x263f: Orphen | Hmm.
- entry[144] 0x263f-0x2675: Cleo | Hey! | did you | hear what I said?
- entry[145] 0x2675-0x26b9: Orphen | Nope, | I wasn't listening. | Catch ya later.
- entry[146] 0x26b9-0x26dd: Cleo | Orphen, wait! | Hey...
- entry[147] 0x26dd-0x2706: Orphen | Hey! | Cleo!
- entry[148] 0x2706-0x2764: Cleo | Orphen, | We're in the middle of a crisis! | Where did you wander off to?
- entry[149] 0x2764-0x278e: Orphen | Oh, nowhere special.
- entry[150] 0x278e-0x27d3: Cleo | So anyway, which way did you go? | Left or right?
- entry[151] 0x27d3-0x27ef: Orphen | What??
- entry[152] 0x27ef-0x2819: Cleo | The exit. | Which one?
- entry[153] 0x2819-0x2833: Orphen | Oh, | that.
- entry[154] 0x2833-0x2866: Magnus | Master, | you're back!
- entry[155] 0x2866-0x288e: Orphen | Everything | okay?
- entry[156] 0x288e-0x28ff: Magnus | Yeah, | we're both fine... | So Master, which way did you go? | Did you go left, or right?
- entry[157] 0x28ff-0x2933: Orphen | What difference does it make!?
- entry[158] 0x2933-0x2983: Magnus | A big one! Even little decisions | can be very important later on.
- entry[159] 0x2983-0x29ae: Orphen | Right I guess.
- entry[160] 0x29ae-0x29c9: Magnus | Hehe!
- entry[161] 0x29c9-0x2a06: Cleo | Traitor! | You took his advice over mine?
- entry[162] 0x2a06-0x2a71: Orphen | Yeah, and I walked right into a pack | of flying monsters. Man, | that wasn't pretty.
- entry[163] 0x2a71-0x2a9a: Cleo | Ha! Serves you right!
- entry[164] 0x2a9a-0x2ac6: Orphen | Left I guess...
- entry[165] 0x2ac6-0x2afe: Cleo | I knew it! | Of course he went left.
- entry[166] 0x2afe-0x2b52: Magnus | Oh, | Master... | You listened to that tomboy? (sarcastically)
- entry[167] 0x2b52-0x2b77: Cleo | I'm not a tomboy!
- entry[168] 0x2b77-0x2bc1: Orphen | And I walked right into a | pack of flying monsters!
- entry[169] 0x2bc1-0x2be9: Magnus | Ha! I told you so!
- entry[170] 0x2be9-0x2c1e: Cleo | Aw, scared by a little dragonfly?
- entry[171] 0x2c1e-0x2c61: Orphen | Argh! You two are impossible! | I'm leaving.
- entry[172] 0x2c61-0x2c8a: Cleo | Wait! | Wait for us!
- entry[173] 0x2c8a-0x2cac: Magnus | Master, | wait up!
- entry[174] 0x2cac-0x2cde: Orphen | What's going on here?
- entry[175] 0x2cde-0x2cf3: A Dragon Eye...

## scr/scr4.out

Header: pointer_table=0x0288-0x02c0, footer=0x28bc, records=12, text_records=12

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x005e: Magnus | Phtooey. | It's salty!
- entry[001] 0x005e-0x009f: Cleo | Oh, no! | We're sinking! | We have to get out!
- entry[002] 0x009f-0x00c2: Magnus | Whoa!
- entry[003] 0x00c2-0x0113: Magnus | Look... Which way do we go? | I say we go right.
- entry[004] 0x0113-0x0157: Cleo | No way. We can't go right. | We have to go left!
- entry[005] 0x0157-0x019a: Magnus | And how do you know | the left way is better?
- entry[006] 0x019a-0x01b6: Cleo | Instinct!
- entry[007] 0x01b6-0x01cf: Magnus | Huh?
- entry[008] 0x01cf-0x0220: Orphen | Knock it off you two! | We have to get out of here, fast!
- entry[009] 0x0220-0x0241: Magnus | Hey! Smoke!
- entry[010] 0x0241-0x025b: Cleo | Orphen!
- entry[011] 0x025b-0x0288: Orphen | Get to the deck! Fast!

## scr/scr5.out

Header: pointer_table=0x0300-0x0334, footer=0x1974, records=12, text_records=10

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x007c: Cleo | This place is super spooky... | Look! What's that?
- entry[001] 0x007c-0x00d0: Orphen | A whole fleet of ancient ships? | Is this a ship graveyard?
- entry[002] 0x00d0-0x012f: Zeus | I don't understand it... | There's no sign that anyone lives here at all.
- entry[003] 0x012f-0x0170: Orphen | Are you sure this is the right direction?
- entry[004] 0x0170-0x01b6: Zeus | Yes, quite sure. | We have to go through there.
- entry[005] 0x01b6-0x020d: Cleo | What!? Isn't there another way? | Something a little less scary?
- entry[006] 0x020d-0x0268: Zeus | We can't get to the center of the island without going through there.
- entry[007] 0x0268-0x02b0: Cleo | Look! The drawbridge! | Now we can't get back in!
- entry[008] 0x02b0-0x02e1: Orphen | Enough whining. Let's go!
- entry[009] 0x02e1-0x02fc: Cleo | Okay, okay!

## scr/scr6.out

Header: pointer_table=0x01b8-0x01dc, footer=0x16e4, records=8, text_records=7

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x0083: Cleo | Orphen... | I think this place is haunted. | I'm scared!
- entry[001] 0x0083-0x00c7: Orphen | Ha! Trust me, you're scarier than any ghost.
- entry[002] 0x00c7-0x010d: Cleo | And just what | is that supposed to mean, Mr. Orphen?
- entry[003] 0x010d-0x012f: Cleo | Oh, wow!
- entry[004] 0x012f-0x0160: Orphen | Whoa! Look at all this loot!
- entry[005] 0x0160-0x018b: Cleo | Orphen, you're drooling!
- entry[006] 0x018b-0x01b7: Orphen | Stop! Something's not right...

## scr/scr7.out

Header: pointer_table=0x04a0-0x04f4, footer=0x266c, records=20, text_records=19

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x0066: Cleo | It's too far to the next ship.
- entry[001] 0x0066-0x009a: Zeus | Wait here, I'll be right back.
- entry[002] 0x009a-0x00cb: Orphen | What are you going to do?
- entry[003] 0x00cb-0x0115: Zeus | Knock down a mast and make a bridge to the next ship.
- entry[004] 0x0115-0x0130: Cleo | What?
- entry[005] 0x0130-0x0150: Orphen | Are you crazy?
- entry[006] 0x0150-0x01c9: Cleo | All these old shipwrecks... | A lot of people must have been | shipwrecked here besides us.
- entry[007] 0x01c9-0x0211: Orphen | That's strange... | This ship... I know I've...
- entry[008] 0x0211-0x0265: Orphen | That figurehead! | It was on the bow of the ship we came on!
- entry[009] 0x0265-0x0286: Cleo | What... Oh!
- entry[010] 0x0286-0x02c4: Orphen | This is the same ship! I'm sure of it.
- entry[011] 0x02c4-0x0317: Zeus | But how can that be? | This ship looks hundreds of years old.
- entry[012] 0x0317-0x037e: Orphen | The ship didn't age like that overnight. | And that leaves just one explanation.
- entry[013] 0x037e-0x03a0: Cleo | What? What!?
- entry[014] 0x03a0-0x03f7: Orphen | It means that somehow, we've been | transported into the future!
- entry[015] 0x03f7-0x0418: Cleo | The future!?
- entry[016] 0x0418-0x0439: Zeus | Impossible!
- entry[017] 0x0439-0x0480: Cleo | Wh-wh-what do you mean, Orphen? | What's going on?
- entry[018] 0x0480-0x049f: Orphen | I wish I knew!

## scr/scr8.out

Header: pointer_table=0x0238-0x0264, footer=0x2cc8, records=9, text_records=9

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x0078: Cleo | Brrr! | Whatever happened to the Volcan and Dortin?
- entry[001] 0x0078-0x00df: Orphen | Bah. | Who knows? At least it's finally | winter. | Ahh... One big ice skating rink.
- entry[002] 0x00df-0x0101: Cleo | Aiee! | Ouch...
- entry[003] 0x0101-0x0138: Orphen | And Cleo is entertaining the fans.
- entry[004] 0x0138-0x017f: Cleo | Orphen! Don't just stand there, | help me! | Aiihhh!
- entry[005] 0x017f-0x01d2: Orphen | Just don't go through the ice, okay? It's too cold for a swim.
- entry[006] 0x01d2-0x01ec: Cleo | Ooohh!
- entry[007] 0x01ec-0x0208: Mar | Hahahaha!
- entry[008] 0x0208-0x0238: Cleo | What are you doing? Let's go!

## scr/scr9.out

Header: pointer_table=0x05bc-0x061c, footer=0x24bc, records=22, text_records=22

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x006a: Cleo | Awesome! Mar, that flute is amazing!
- entry[001] 0x006a-0x0092: Orphen | How do you do that?
- entry[002] 0x0092-0x0100: Mar | I don't really know. | Whenever I get in trouble, I just play the | music my mother taught me.
- entry[003] 0x0100-0x0134: Cleo | Was your mother a great musician?
- entry[004] 0x0134-0x0190: Mar | I don't know... | All I remember is this | song. She used to hum it to me.
- entry[005] 0x0190-0x01f6: Cleo | I bet there's something in that tower | that will help us find your mother! | Come on!
- entry[006] 0x01f6-0x0211: Mar | Let's go!
- entry[007] 0x0211-0x025a: Cleo | Hey, | I can hear music coming from this tower.
- entry[008] 0x025a-0x02b4: Mar | Yeah... | But this isn't the right tower. | And it's not the same music.
- entry[009] 0x02b4-0x02f6: Cleo | Let's take a look anyway. We might find a clue.
- entry[010] 0x02f6-0x034f: Orphen | How are we supposed to get in there? | The drawbridge is frozen solid.
- entry[011] 0x034f-0x03c0: Cleo | I don't know... Wait! | If we go back to our normal time, | the ice will all be melted, right?
- entry[012] 0x03c0-0x041a: Orphen | Hmm... You might be right. | Well, I guess | we need to go back anyway.
- entry[013] 0x041a-0x0442: Orphen | So how do we do it?
- entry[014] 0x0442-0x04a3: Cleo | Hmm... | Maybe it's like making a wish. | You think about when you want to be...
- entry[015] 0x04a3-0x04ce: Orphen | That'll never work...
- entry[016] 0x04ce-0x04e9: Orphen | Woah!
- entry[017] 0x04e9-0x0511: Cleo | Cool! We jumped back!
- entry[018] 0x0511-0x053b: Orphen | That actually worked?
- entry[019] 0x053b-0x0568: Mar | I can still hear the music.
- entry[020] 0x0568-0x0584: Cleo | Let's go!
- entry[021] 0x0584-0x05bc: Orphen | Who knows what we'll find...

## scr/scr10.out

Header: pointer_table=0x01c0-0x01ec, footer=0x40f8, records=10, text_records=8

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x007a: Orphen | The crust isn't hard enough to support our weight.
- entry[001] 0x007a-0x00a4: Cleo | What do we do, Orphen!?
- entry[002] 0x00a4-0x00cf: Orphen | Let me think, damn it!
- entry[004] 0x00d1-0x0125: Mar | I can get us out of here. | I don't weigh enough to fall through.
- entry[005] 0x0125-0x0150: Cleo | Mar, it's too dangerous!
- entry[006] 0x0150-0x016f: Mar | I'll be okay.
- entry[007] 0x016f-0x019b: Cleo | Don't do anything stupid.
- entry[008] 0x019b-0x01bd: Orphen | What's wrong?

## scr/scr11.out

Header: pointer_table=0x1054-0x1150, footer=0x5f2c, records=62, text_records=61

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x0058: Orphen | Where am I?
- entry[001] 0x0058-0x0078: Magnus | Master!
- entry[002] 0x0078-0x00a6: Orphen | Magnus! | Where's Cleo?
- entry[003] 0x00a6-0x0120: Magnus | She must have washed ashore somewhere on this island. | But we'll never find her | in this storm.
- entry[004] 0x0120-0x014d: Orphen | Where are we, anyway?
- entry[005] 0x014d-0x018f: Magnus | Apparently, some place called Chaos Island.
- entry[006] 0x018f-0x01e0: Orphen | What?? | Chaos Island? | Shouldn't we be near Arvanrama?
- entry[007] 0x01e0-0x025a: Sephy | The ship we were on was headed for Chaos Island all along. | Arvanrama is in the opposite direction.
- entry[008] 0x025a-0x029f: Magnus | It looks like Volcan put us on the wrong ship.
- entry[009] 0x029f-0x02d9: Orphen | Damn! | And we even paid their way!
- entry[010] 0x02d9-0x0331: Magnus | Maybe it's our own fault | for trusting them in the first place.
- entry[011] 0x0331-0x0355: Orphen | Oh, shut up!
- entry[012] 0x0355-0x03ba: Sephy | I wonder if we shouldn't try to find some shelter. | It's cold out here in the rain.
- entry[013] 0x03ba-0x0423: Magnus | I can't believe it sank. | It was such a big ship. | I guess we're stuck here.
- entry[014] 0x0423-0x0458: Orphen | We better start looking for Cleo.
- entry[015] 0x0458-0x0483: Magnus | I hope she's all right.
- entry[016] 0x0483-0x04a0: Orphen | Yeah, me too...
- entry[017] 0x04a0-0x04e7: Orphen | It's a dead end. We can't go any further.
- entry[018] 0x04e7-0x0521: Magnus | There must be another path, Master.
- entry[019] 0x0521-0x0552: Orphen | Damn. This is not my lucky day.
- entry[020] 0x0552-0x05a3: Magnus | Look at all those trees! | I can't even see the path.
- entry[021] 0x05a3-0x0611: Orphen | This island is starting to get on my nerves! | Magnus, it's time for a little forest fire!
- entry[022] 0x0611-0x0625: Magnus | Okay!
- entry[023] 0x0625-0x0658: Magnus | Hey, look! | A building!
- entry[024] 0x0658-0x0695: Sephy | We could wait out the storm in that tower.
- entry[025] 0x0695-0x06e7: Orphen | Hmm... | I don't like it. | That tower must be centuries old.
- entry[026] 0x06e7-0x073b: Magnus | It's better than nothing. | At least we'll be out of the storm.
- entry[027] 0x073b-0x07a6: Orphen | I realize that! | But we can't get in anyway. | Not with the drawbridge up like that.
- entry[028] 0x07a6-0x0817: Sephy | Orphen, I think I may be able to help. | I could climb up the | ivy to that hole in the wall.
- entry[029] 0x0817-0x0860: Magnus | No, Sephy! | It's too dangerous! | Master, I'll go.
- entry[030] 0x0860-0x08f2: Sephy | I'll be fine. | I can use those floating logs over there | to get to the base of the tower. | Orphen, Magnus, | I'll be right back.
- entry[031] 0x08f2-0x0929: Magnus | Be careful Sephy!
- entry[032] 0x0929-0x0950: Orphen | Yell if you need help!
- entry[033] 0x0950-0x0973: Sephy | Hah!
- entry[034] 0x0973-0x09a7: Magnus | Wow, that was great, Sephy!
- entry[035] 0x09a7-0x09c9: Orphen | You okay?
- entry[036] 0x09c9-0x09e5: Sephy | Yes.
- entry[037] 0x09e5-0x0a1d: Orphen | Pretty impressive footwork...
- entry[038] 0x0a1d-0x0aa8: Sephy | I'm a little embarrassed to admit it... | But I'm a professional acrobat. I'm just | doing what I'm trained to do.
- entry[039] 0x0aa8-0x0b19: Sephy | I never thought | my training in the Royal Palace | would be of use in a place like this.
- entry[040] 0x0b19-0x0b71: Orphen | Hmm... | So tell me, | Why did you want to come to this island?
- entry[041] 0x0b71-0x0baf: Sephy | I came to this island to honor someone.
- entry[042] 0x0baf-0x0bd8: Orphen | To honor someone?
- entry[043] 0x0bd8-0x0c0b: Sephy | Yes... | My fiance. He died.
- entry[044] 0x0c0b-0x0c31: Magnus | ! | Fiance...
- entry[045] 0x0c31-0x0c6f: Sephy | Orphen, | may I ask a big favor of you?
- entry[046] 0x0c6f-0x0c8d: Orphen | What?
- entry[047] 0x0c8d-0x0d13: Sephy | I know it's very | selfish of me to ask | at a time like this, but... | Would you accompany me while I'm here?
- entry[048] 0x0d13-0x0d33: Orphen | Why me?
- entry[049] 0x0d33-0x0d9b: Sephy | I'm just a girl. What would I do | if I ran into one of those horrible monsters?
- entry[050] 0x0d9b-0x0ddf: Orphen | Sorry, but I have better things to be doing.
- entry[051] 0x0ddf-0x0e16: Sephy | I can make it worth your while.
- entry[052] 0x0e16-0x0e33: Orphen | Huh?
- entry[053] 0x0e33-0x0ebf: Sephy | I used to perform in the Royal Palace. I was the star of our acrobatics troupe. | My benefactors were quite generous.
- entry[054] 0x0ebf-0x0ef6: Magnus | Oh Sephy, you don't have to ...
- entry[055] 0x0ef6-0x0f14: Magnus | Ouch!
- entry[056] 0x0f14-0x0f50: Orphen | Well, now, that's a different story!
- entry[057] 0x0f50-0x0f76: Sephy | Oh, thank you!
- entry[058] 0x0f76-0x0fa3: Orphen | Shall we get started?
- entry[059] 0x0fa3-0x0ff0: Magnus | But Master... | It's not right to take her money...
- entry[060] 0x0ff0-0x1052: Orphen | Look at this place. What a dump! | Looks like it hasn't been used for centuries.

## scr/scr12.out

Header: pointer_table=0x033c-0x036c, footer=0x242c, records=11, text_records=10

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x0076: Magnus | Hey, Master, | Do you smell something?
- entry[001] 0x0076-0x0097: Orphen | Like what?
- entry[002] 0x0097-0x0102: Magnus | Hmm... | It's a sweet smell... | Ah... I think it's coming from those tree blossums.
- entry[003] 0x0102-0x0145: Sephy | Blossums? Tree? Is that the Thousand Year Tree?
- entry[004] 0x0145-0x0173: Orphen | The 'Thousand Year Tree'?
- entry[005] 0x0173-0x0195: Magnus | What's that?
- entry[006] 0x0195-0x022d: Sephy | Somewhere on this island, | there is supposed to be a tree, the Thousand Year Tree, | that blossoms only once in a thousand years.
- entry[007] 0x022d-0x02ae: Magnus | Once in a thousand years? | So, we happened to come just at the right time? | Master, isn't that amazing?
- entry[008] 0x02ae-0x02fc: Orphen | I couldn't care less about blossoming trees. Let's get moving!
- entry[009] 0x02fc-0x0339: Magnus | What's wrong? Shouldn't we keep moving?

## scr/scr13.out

Header: pointer_table=0x05f0-0x0650, footer=0x5cdc, records=23, text_records=22

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x0080: Magnus | This time we jumped into the middle of the night.
- entry[001] 0x0080-0x00b5: Sephy | I can't see anything in this fog.
- entry[002] 0x00b5-0x0108: Orphen | Stay alert. | You never know what could be hiding in that fog.
- entry[003] 0x0108-0x013d: Magnus | Come on, stop trying to scare us...
- entry[004] 0x013d-0x019d: Magnus | Master, | Weren't we just on this path a little while ago?
- entry[005] 0x019d-0x01f6: Orphen | I realize that! | How are we supposed to get out of this dumb maze?
- entry[006] 0x01f6-0x0223: Sephy | Orphen,
- entry[007] 0x0223-0x0245: Orphen | What?
- entry[008] 0x0245-0x02ff: Sephy | I can smell the blossoms of the Thousand Year Tree. | It must be above us somewhere. | I'll climb up and take a look around. | I may be able to find a way out of here.
- entry[009] 0x02ff-0x0325: Orphen | Good idea. | Hurry back.
- entry[010] 0x0325-0x034f: Orphen | See anything?
- entry[011] 0x034f-0x037e: Magnus | Be careful, Sephy.
- entry[012] 0x037e-0x03c9: Sephy | Ahh... | What truly magnificent blossoms.
- entry[013] 0x03c9-0x03f7: Sephy | Aha! A new pathway.
- entry[014] 0x03f7-0x044f: Sephy | Orphen, | I found a new pathway. | It might be the way out.
- entry[015] 0x044f-0x0476: Orphen | Excellent!
- entry[016] 0x0476-0x04fe: Sephy | There's more... The Thousand Year Tree was in blossom. | I think that means we have returned to our own time.
- entry[017] 0x04fe-0x0537: Magnus | What? Really? | All right!
- entry[018] 0x0537-0x0561: Orphen | Okay, let's move out!
- entry[019] 0x0561-0x058d: Sephy | Orphen...
- entry[020] 0x058d-0x05b2: Sephy | This is the path.
- entry[021] 0x05b2-0x05ef: Magnus | I don't remember seeing anything here before...

## scr/scr14.out

Header: pointer_table=0x06e4-0x0754, footer=0x68c8, records=27, text_records=26

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x007b: Magnus | So Rani, | why were you in that place back there?
- entry[001] 0x007b-0x00c9: Rani | There are tombs underneath there. | I take care of them.
- entry[002] 0x00c9-0x010d: Orphen | Aren't you a little young for a grave keeper?
- entry[003] 0x010d-0x0168: Rani | My mother and father are both there. | I like to keep it looking nice.
- entry[004] 0x0168-0x0191: Orphen | Oh... | I'm sorry.
- entry[005] 0x0191-0x0202: Rani | No, don't be. | Not many people live here anymore. | Someone has to take care of the graves.
- entry[006] 0x0202-0x0232: Zeus | So you, too, are alone...
- entry[007] 0x0232-0x026b: Rani | Ah! | Where-where did you find that?
- entry[008] 0x026b-0x02d3: Zeus | Eh? | Oh, this? | It belongs to my daughter... | Her name is Azel. Do you know her?
- entry[009] 0x02d3-0x02ed: Rani | Azel!
- entry[010] 0x02ed-0x0315: Zeus | So you do know her?
- entry[011] 0x0315-0x035f: Rani | No, | I, ah, | I just asked because it's so pretty.
- entry[012] 0x035f-0x037e: Zeus | Oh, I see.
- entry[013] 0x037e-0x039b: Rani | Sorry.
- entry[014] 0x039b-0x0407: Orphen | What's wrong with this gate? | It's not opening! | Rani, that lever will open it, right?
- entry[015] 0x0407-0x043c: Orphen | Wait in the boat. | I'll be right back.
- entry[016] 0x043c-0x049f: Magnus | Uh-oh, this gate isn't going to open. | It's rusted to pieces.
- entry[017] 0x049f-0x04c6: Zeus | I'll get that gate open.
- entry[018] 0x04c6-0x04e9: Zeus | Ungghh!
- entry[019] 0x04e9-0x0508: Magnus | Wow! He did it!
- entry[020] 0x0508-0x053c: Zeus | I see you! Come out and fight!
- entry[021] 0x053c-0x0560: Zeus | \u0152\xfb\u201a\xd9\u201a\xc7\u201a\xc9\u201a\xe0\u201a\xc8\u201a\xa2
- entry[022] 0x0560-0x0599: Magnus | \u201a\xa8\u017dt\u2014l\ufffdA\u2039C\u2022\xaa\u201a\xaa\u02c6\xab\u201a\xa2\u201a\xc5\u201a\xb7\ufffdI | \u2018D\ufffd\u0152\u201a\xa2\u201a\xc5\u201a\xb7
- entry[023] 0x0599-0x0657: Orphen | Zeus! | It's your turn this time. | Use your strength to move that stone! | That should block the canal and | raise the water level enough for us to | get past the gate!
- entry[024] 0x0657-0x06a0: Orphen | Not again! | What's up with these gates, Rani?
- entry[025] 0x06a0-0x06e1: Orphen | Okay, let's see... | There's the switch over there.

## scr/scr15.out

Header: pointer_table=0x0408-0x0454, footer=0x210c, records=18, text_records=14

- entry[001] 0x0035-0x00b1: Orphen | Well, let's go. | The sooner we finish our task, | the sooner we can get off this dumb island.
- entry[002] 0x00b1-0x010d: Magnus | What beautiful weather! | It's hard to believe there ever was a storm.
- entry[003] 0x010d-0x0177: Orphen | So... | This temple, where you're going to pray | for your fiance... Where is it?
- entry[004] 0x0177-0x01c3: Sephy | It's in the middle of the island, | over there, over the water.
- entry[006] 0x01cb-0x01f9: Magnus | Master! | The drawbridge!
- entry[008] 0x0202-0x0258: Magnus | Look, Master! The water is lower now, and there's a path!
- entry[009] 0x0258-0x0289: Orphen | Good. Magnus, you go first.
- entry[010] 0x0289-0x02bd: Magnus | Me? Why do I have to go first?
- entry[011] 0x02bd-0x02fc: Orphen | Because there might be monsters up ahead.
- entry[012] 0x02fc-0x0358: Magnus | That-that's not very nice, Master! | Don't you care what happens to me?
- entry[013] 0x0358-0x0396: Orphen | Of course I do! Think of it as training.
- entry[014] 0x0396-0x03b4: Magnus | Oh, man.
- entry[015] 0x03b4-0x03df: Magnus | I always get the worst jobs.
- entry[016] 0x03df-0x0406: Sephy | Let's keep moving.

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x007d: Orphen | Look at all this sand. | Magnus could have | been swept away.
- entry[001] 0x007d-0x009d: Orphen | Ah!
- entry[002] 0x009d-0x00c0: Sephy | Oh! | Magnus!
- entry[003] 0x00c0-0x00de: Sephy | Magnus...
- entry[004] 0x00de-0x010f: Magnus | Ugh... | Ahh! Where? What?
- entry[005] 0x010f-0x0141: Sephy | Oh! | You're alive, | Magnus!
- entry[006] 0x0141-0x0180: Magnus | Uh, | Sephy, | Master? | What's going on?
- entry[007] 0x0180-0x01d5: Orphen | Dummy! | When you didn't come back, | we came looking for you.
- entry[008] 0x01d5-0x024f: Magnus | What? | Come back? | The switches... | Oh, yeah. | I got caught in the sand and fell from up there.
- entry[009] 0x024f-0x0275: Sephy | Thank goodness.
- entry[010] 0x0275-0x02a2: Magnus | \u201a\xed\ufffdI\ufffdH\ufffd@\u0192Z\u0192t\u0192B\u201a\xb3\u201a\xf1\ufffdE\ufffdE\ufffdE
- entry[011] 0x02a2-0x02f8: Orphen | Right, now that you remember your name... | Let's get going!
- entry[012] 0x02f8-0x0317: Magnus | Yes, sir!
- entry[013] 0x0317-0x0333: Sephy | Orphen!
- entry[014] 0x0333-0x0368: Orphen | Damn! | Persistant, aren't they?
- entry[015] 0x0368-0x0389: Sephy | Let's hurry.
- entry[016] 0x0389-0x03ba: Sephy | Magnus! | Where could he be?

## scr/scr22.out

Header: pointer_table=0x0558-0x05c4, footer=0x679c, records=26, text_records=25

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x0053: Magnus | Master!!
- entry[001] 0x0053-0x008c: Magnus | This building must be really old.
- entry[002] 0x008c-0x00d5: Orphen | Yeah. | Well, at least we're out of the rain now.
- entry[003] 0x00d5-0x0126: Magnus | Still, | something about this place gives me the creeps.
- entry[004] 0x0126-0x014d: Orphen | Are you scared?
- entry[005] 0x014d-0x01a4: Magnus | Scared? | Who, me? | I'm not scared! | I'm just... Concerned.
- entry[006] 0x01a4-0x01c4: Sephy | Orphen...
- entry[007] 0x01c4-0x01fe: Sephy | Do you see that hole in the ceiling?
- entry[008] 0x01fe-0x0252: Sephy | I wonder if this platform could take us up to the next floor.
- entry[009] 0x0252-0x026b: Magnus | Up we go!
- entry[010] 0x026b-0x02a8: Orphen | Okay, this could be tricky...
- entry[011] 0x02a8-0x02fd: Orphen | Let's split up. | I'll go on ahead. | You guys | catch up later.
- entry[012] 0x02fd-0x034d: Magnus | Master, | let's wait inside until the storm dies down.
- entry[013] 0x034d-0x03aa: Magnus | Master! | Please... This is no time to go out and play in the rain!
- entry[014] 0x03aa-0x03cb: Magnus | Master!!
- entry[015] 0x03cb-0x0407: Orphen | I wonder | if those two are okay...
- entry[016] 0x0407-0x0432: Magnus | Is anything wrong?
- entry[017] 0x0432-0x0465: Magnus | Be careful, Master Orphen.
- entry[018] 0x0465-0x04a0: Magnus | Master? | Shouldn't we keep going?
- entry[019] 0x04a0-0x04c5: Orphen | Sephy.
- entry[020] 0x04c5-0x04e3: Sephy | Orphen.
- entry[021] 0x04e3-0x0510: Orphen | Keep your eyes open!
- entry[022] 0x0510-0x0527: Sephy | I will.
- entry[023] 0x0527-0x0545: Sephy | Orphen.
- entry[024] 0x0545-0x0556: Sephy | . . .

## scr/scr23.out

Header: pointer_table=0x0878-0x08f4, footer=0x67ac, records=29, text_records=29

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x005f: Magnus | This looks like the top floor.
- entry[001] 0x005f-0x007d: Sephy | Ah!
- entry[002] 0x007d-0x0098: Orphen | What!?
- entry[003] 0x0098-0x00c3: Magnus | Sephy, | what's wrong?
- entry[004] 0x00c3-0x00f4: Magnus | What in the world | is that?
- entry[005] 0x00f4-0x0132: Sephy | This... | Is a fragment of the Crystal Egg.
- entry[006] 0x0132-0x0156: Magnus | The Crystal Egg?
- entry[007] 0x0156-0x02f5: Sephy | I must apologize... | I wasn't trying to keep it a secret, but | my fiance Rufus did tell me that there was something | on this island called the Crystal Egg, which could manipulate time itself. | The strange part is | from what he told me, | the Crystal Egg | should not have been in this tower at all. | Orphen, | somehow, I think we've been transported to a different time.
- entry[008] 0x02f5-0x0310: Magnus | What!?
- entry[009] 0x0310-0x034c: Orphen | What do you mean, | a 'different time'?
- entry[010] 0x034c-0x0460: Sephy | I suspect | that something has caused | the very space-time continuum of this island | to become warped. | When I first entered this tower, | I wondered why everything | looked so old and decrepit. | I suspect that we've | jumped into the future somehow.
- entry[011] 0x0460-0x049b: Magnus | ! | Future? | How far into the future?
- entry[012] 0x049b-0x0525: Sephy | I don't know... | But, | don't worry... | As long as we have the Crystal Egg, | we should be able to go back in time.
- entry[013] 0x0525-0x0566: Orphen | Yeah, but | that egg is in a hundred pieces.
- entry[014] 0x0566-0x05f5: Sephy | True, | but all we have to do is touch the inside | of this egg fragment, and we can | jump around in time as we please.
- entry[015] 0x05f5-0x0635: Magnus | Master! | Look! The storm is gone! | Cool!
- entry[016] 0x0635-0x0688: Orphen | What the hell is going on? | Are we back to our own time now?
- entry[017] 0x0688-0x06a0: Sephy | Yes!
- entry[018] 0x06a0-0x06be: Magnus | All right!
- entry[019] 0x06be-0x06f5: Sephy | That | is the power of the Crystal Egg...
- entry[020] 0x06f5-0x072a: Sephy | Orphen, | Shall I proceed?
- entry[021] 0x072a-0x0763: Orphen | Yeah. | Oh, and no mistakes, please.
- entry[022] 0x0763-0x0781: Magnus | Mistakes?
- entry[023] 0x0781-0x079c: Sephy | Magnus?
- entry[024] 0x079c-0x07bd: Magnus | Uh, | ready!
- entry[025] 0x07bd-0x0815: Orphen | Okay... | Let's hope it works as planned. | We're counting on you.
- entry[026] 0x0815-0x082e: Sephy | Okay.
- entry[027] 0x082e-0x0857: Orphen | Well, | let's do it!
- entry[028] 0x0857-0x0878: Magnus | Go ahead, Sephy!

## scr/scr24.out

Header: pointer_table=0x01c4-0x01e4, footer=0x36cc, records=7, text_records=6

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x00a5: Magnus | It doesn't look like anyone lives here. | What do you think this building is for, anyway?
- entry[001] 0x00a5-0x011b: Orphen | Who knows? | In any case, it makes a nice shelter from the storm. | I think I'll do some exploring.
- entry[002] 0x011b-0x0143: Magnus | Is anything wrong?
- entry[003] 0x0143-0x016c: Magnus | Be careful, Master.
- entry[004] 0x016c-0x01a3: Magnus | Master? | Shouldn't we keep going?
- entry[005] 0x01a3-0x01c1: Sephy | Orphen...

## scr/scr25.out

## scr/scr26.out

Header: pointer_table=0x025c-0x028c, footer=0x0cfc, records=11, text_records=10

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x0088: Zeus | You've taken us far enough, Rani. | Be careful on your way home.
- entry[001] 0x0088-0x0104: Rani | Don't worry, I will. | Go along the wall until you get to the | Tower of Wisdom. The entrance is there.
- entry[002] 0x0104-0x0128: Orphen | Thanks, Rani.
- entry[003] 0x0128-0x0161: Rani | Good luck to you, too, Mr. Magician.
- entry[004] 0x0161-0x0197: Orphen | I told you, I'm not a magician!
- entry[005] 0x0197-0x01cc: Magnus | Okay, okay. | Thank you, Rani.
- entry[006] 0x01cc-0x01e8: Rani | Zeus...
- entry[007] 0x01e8-0x0201: Zeus | Yes?
- entry[008] 0x0201-0x0240: Rani | I hope... | I hope you find your daughter.
- entry[009] 0x0240-0x0259: Zeus | Thank you.

## scr/scr27.out

Header: pointer_table=0x0a2c-0x0ab8, footer=0x31bc, records=34, text_records=33

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x005a: Orphen | This is the place.
- entry[001] 0x005a-0x0091: Mar | This is it! This is my mother's song!
- entry[002] 0x0091-0x00b4: Cleo | Who's that girl?
- entry[003] 0x00b4-0x00e2: Quaris | You there! | Who are you?
- entry[004] 0x00e2-0x0180: Cleo | Well who are you? | You're supposed to | give your own name first! | Didn't they teach you how to make | a proper introduction at school?
- entry[005] 0x0180-0x01f4: Orphen | Easy, Cleo. | We heard the music | and followed it. | We talked to some old lady named Jado.
- entry[006] 0x01f4-0x021b: Quaris | So you met Mother?
- entry[007] 0x021b-0x026c: Orphen | You're her daughter? | So just what is going on around here?
- entry[008] 0x026c-0x03bb: Quaris | Mother probably told you already, but | the evil spirit on this island | is only kept in check by this song. | But now the music machine is broken, | and the demon is starting to escape. | Already, there are monsters roaming the | island. | When the demon fully awakens, | this island will sink beneath the waves.
- entry[009] 0x03bb-0x0412: Cleo | The island's going to sink? | Then why | aren't you trying to leave?
- entry[010] 0x0412-0x0492: Quaris | Rani, my daughter, has been playing | the Song nonstop, but she's | exhausted. | I don't know what to do.
- entry[011] 0x0492-0x04f0: Orphen | Demons? Magic songs? Music machines? | Just who | exactly are you people?
- entry[012] 0x04f0-0x0565: Quaris | My family | and the Song | have been the | guardians of this island | for countless generations.
- entry[013] 0x0565-0x0580: Mar | M-mother?
- entry[014] 0x0580-0x059a: Quaris | What?
- entry[015] 0x059a-0x05b4: Quaris | Ahh!
- entry[016] 0x05b4-0x0601: Quaris | No, | I... I'm not | your mother! | I don't have a son!
- entry[017] 0x0601-0x0622: Mar | But... | Why?
- entry[018] 0x0622-0x063c: Quaris | Rani!
- entry[019] 0x063c-0x0654: Cleo | Eeya!
- entry[020] 0x0654-0x066f: Orphen | Uh-oh.
- entry[021] 0x066f-0x06c9: Quaris | Leave this island, save yourselves! | No one will think badly of you.
- entry[022] 0x06c9-0x06df: Mar | Ah!
- entry[023] 0x06df-0x0720: Cleo | Mar, | so that woman's not your mother?
- entry[024] 0x0720-0x075a: Mar | I don't know. | Her face... I thought...
- entry[025] 0x075a-0x07bc: Cleo | But she says she doesn't have any sons. | What if the island really is sinking?
- entry[026] 0x07bc-0x07fd: Cleo | Orphen, don't just stand there, say something!
- entry[027] 0x07fd-0x08c0: Orphen | Ah... | It's just that, well, | if she really | was his mother, | why would she deny it? | Either way | there's nothing we can do about it. | I think it's time we went home.
- entry[028] 0x08c0-0x08f8: Cleo | Orphen! | Think about Mar's feelings!
- entry[029] 0x08f8-0x0954: Mar | I want to... | I want to help that girl. | I have to help Rani. I have to!
- entry[030] 0x0954-0x098d: Cleo | Huh? | Wait a minute. | Why that girl?
- entry[031] 0x098d-0x09f2: Mar | I don't know... | Cleo! | Orphen! | Please! | Help me, just a little while longer!
- entry[032] 0x09f2-0x0a2b: Cleo | What are you doing? Come on, let's go!

Header: pointer_table=0x075c-0x07ec, footer=0x53c4, records=35, text_records=34

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x005f: Orphen | . . . | Did it work?
- entry[001] 0x005f-0x0087: Magnus | Wow! | It's hot!
- entry[002] 0x0087-0x00c8: Sephy | What... What happened? | Why is it so hot?
- entry[003] 0x00c8-0x0103: Magnus | Master, look! | It's all desert out there!
- entry[004] 0x0103-0x0181: Orphen | So in this time period, | the island has become a desert? | The heat | is almost unbelievable.
- entry[005] 0x0181-0x01ab: Sephy | Oh, Orphen! | Look!
- entry[006] 0x01ab-0x01db: Magnus | We did it! | It's whole!
- entry[007] 0x01db-0x01fc: Sephy | The Egg...
- entry[008] 0x01fc-0x0213: Orphen | ?!
- entry[009] 0x0213-0x0237: Orphen | Sephy, no!
- entry[010] 0x0237-0x0253: Sephy | What?
- entry[011] 0x0253-0x026d: Sephy | Oh!
- entry[012] 0x026d-0x028a: Orphen | Damn!
- entry[013] 0x028a-0x02a9: Magnus | Master!
- entry[014] 0x02a9-0x02c5: Magnus | Ugh!
- entry[015] 0x02c5-0x02e4: Orphen | Magnus!
- entry[016] 0x02e4-0x0302: Sephy | Magnus!
- entry[017] 0x0302-0x033f: Magnus | Oh, Master. | What are we going to do?
- entry[018] 0x033f-0x03bb: Orphen | There has to be | a switch somewhere in this tower | to disarm the trap. | You'll have to find it.
- entry[019] 0x03bb-0x03de: Magnus | Me? Why me?
- entry[020] 0x03de-0x042b: Orphen | Numbskull!!! | Because you're the only one out there!
- entry[021] 0x042b-0x0462: Magnus | But I don't know where it is...
- entry[022] 0x0462-0x051e: Orphen | Pathetic. | And you're going to become a sorcerer? | Stop whining, and go find the damn switch! | And don't leave us here too long, | or we'll turn into mummies!
- entry[023] 0x051e-0x053f: Magnus | Yes, sir!
- entry[024] 0x053f-0x056b: Sephy | Magnus, | be careful.
- entry[025] 0x056b-0x05a1: Magnus | Sephy, | Don't worry, I'll save you.
- entry[026] 0x05a1-0x05ce: Orphen | Hmm... | Ah...
- entry[027] 0x05ce-0x05f3: Sephy | Magnus did it!
- entry[028] 0x05f3-0x0636: Orphen | Yeah. | Normally he's such a screw-up, too.
- entry[029] 0x0636-0x0665: Orphen | What's taking him so long?
- entry[030] 0x0665-0x068b: Sephy | Do you think...
- entry[031] 0x068b-0x06f5: Orphen | Damn! | Just when you think he's finally done a good job, | He goes and gets lost.
- entry[032] 0x06f5-0x0738: Orphen | I'm gonna go take a look. | Catch up when you can.
- entry[033] 0x0738-0x075b: Sephy | Brave Magnus...

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x0056: Cleo | It's so quiet...
- entry[001] 0x0056-0x0092: Mar | I can hear the music from somewhere above.
- entry[002] 0x0092-0x00d8: Orphen | I don't see any stairs. | What the hell is that?
- entry[003] 0x00d8-0x0112: Mar | That sounds like part of that last song.
- entry[004] 0x0112-0x014e: Mar | That sounds like part of that last song.
- entry[005] 0x014e-0x0189: Mar | Let's try playing the phrases in order.
- entry[006] 0x0189-0x01ab: Cleo | What is this?
- entry[007] 0x01ab-0x01c9: Mar | We did it!

## scr/scr40.out

Header: pointer_table=0x0178-0x0194, footer=0x25ec, records=6, text_records=5

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x0072: Orphen | What's the deal here? | The maze goes on forever.
- entry[001] 0x0072-0x009f: Cleo | Shh... | Do you hear that?
- entry[002] 0x009f-0x00bf: Orphen | Over there?
- entry[003] 0x00bf-0x015c: Mar | Wait... | The faster we chase it, the | farther away it goes. | Let's try walking really, really slow, | so our footsteps don't make any sound.
- entry[004] 0x015c-0x0176: Cleo | Stairs!

## scr/scr41.out

Header: pointer_table=0x0124-0x0140, footer=0x25bc, records=6, text_records=5

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x0061: Mar | Orphen, look! | What is that thing?
- entry[001] 0x0061-0x00b1: Cleo | It wants us to follow in that order. | You go first, Orphen.
- entry[002] 0x00b1-0x00d6: Mar | Be careful, Orphen!
- entry[003] 0x00d6-0x0102: Orphen | Why do I have to first?
- entry[004] 0x0102-0x0121: Cleo | Wait for us!

## scr/scr42.out

Header: pointer_table=0x079c-0x0824, footer=0x282c, records=33, text_records=32

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x0052: Jado | Who are you?
- entry[001] 0x0052-0x007c: Mar | Oh, | it's the old lady.
- entry[002] 0x007c-0x00b1: Jado | I've never seen you people before.
- entry[003] 0x00b1-0x00d7: Cleo | What are you doing?
- entry[004] 0x00d7-0x0122: Jado | I'm trying to dispel the curse, | and save this island.
- entry[005] 0x0122-0x0142: Orphen | What curse?
- entry[006] 0x0142-0x016c: Cleo | Ah! Another earthquake!
- entry[007] 0x016c-0x01ac: Jado | They're caused by the demon under the island.
- entry[008] 0x01ac-0x01c7: Orphen | Demon?
- entry[009] 0x01c7-0x0233: Jado | An evil spirit of the island. | As long as the Song is playing, | it will remain asleep.
- entry[010] 0x0233-0x025e: Cleo | So that music machine...
- entry[011] 0x025e-0x02c2: Jado | You saw my machine, eh? | It plays the Song automatically. | But now it's broken.
- entry[012] 0x02c2-0x031f: Jado | I have get it working again, fast. | I'm drawing up some plans for it now.
- entry[013] 0x031f-0x0368: Orphen | Very interesting. | And the music playing right now?
- entry[014] 0x0368-0x03cc: Jado | Is played by hand, at the other tower. | Haaaah. | If I don't fix that machine...
- entry[015] 0x03cc-0x03fb: Cleo | So the song Mar remembers...
- entry[016] 0x03fb-0x0412: Jado | Mar?
- entry[017] 0x0412-0x045d: Cleo | Yeah. This boy. He came all the way here for this music.
- entry[018] 0x045d-0x048e: Mar | I'm | trying to find my mother.
- entry[019] 0x048e-0x04ae: Jado | !? Oh, ohh...
- entry[020] 0x04ae-0x0507: Mar | My mother played it when I was a baby. | It's all I remember about her.
- entry[021] 0x0507-0x053c: Jado | Your mother? | But it can't be...
- entry[022] 0x053c-0x05a2: Mar | You know who I am? | Please tell me! Where is my mother? | Please! | I need to know!
- entry[023] 0x05a2-0x05d9: Jado | I, I don't know! | Why would I know?
- entry[024] 0x05d9-0x060a: Mar | Then | who's playing this song?
- entry[025] 0x060a-0x0653: Jado | I don't know. I don't know! | Now get off this island!
- entry[026] 0x0653-0x0677: Orphen | Come on now...
- entry[027] 0x0677-0x06bc: Jado | I'm busy! I have nothing else to say! | Go away!
- entry[028] 0x06bc-0x06e8: Orphen | She's hiding something.
- entry[029] 0x06e8-0x0726: Cleo | Maybe, but she's not going to tell us what.
- entry[030] 0x0726-0x0741: Mar | Mommy...
- entry[031] 0x0741-0x0799: Orphen | She mentioned the other tower... | Let's go check it out.

## scr/scr43.out

Header: pointer_table=0x0ed4-0x0f80, footer=0x3814, records=41, text_records=41

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x0086: Cleo | Orphen! | Come on, wake up! | Don't make me kick you...
- entry[001] 0x0086-0x00d4: Orphen | Damn... | Can't I even pass out in peace? | Where am I?
- entry[002] 0x00d4-0x0115: Cleo | Um, | Some place called, like Anarchy Island.
- entry[003] 0x0115-0x014a: Orphen | Hmm... | Anarchy Island, huh?
- entry[004] 0x014a-0x016d: Zeus | Chaos Island.
- entry[005] 0x016d-0x01f5: Cleo | Oh, yeah, | Chaos Island, | that was it. | Zeus here says that | that ship was | headed for Chaos Island all along.
- entry[006] 0x01f5-0x0211: Orphen | What!?
- entry[007] 0x0211-0x0247: Cleo | Volcan took us | on the wrong ship.
- entry[008] 0x0247-0x02b1: Orphen | Fantastic. (sarcastically) | We never should have trusted that | demented halfling!
- entry[009] 0x02b1-0x0326: Cleo | You were the one that wanted go with him! | I wonder what happened to | Magnus and the others...
- entry[010] 0x0326-0x036d: Orphen | If they're not dead, | then they're alive.
- entry[011] 0x036d-0x03d7: Cleo | Zeus, do you think anyone lives | around here? Someone might | have seen them.
- entry[012] 0x03d7-0x0455: Zeus | I don't know. | I've never been here before. | By the way, | what | shall I call you? | Miss Cleo?
- entry[013] 0x0455-0x04a5: Cleo | Yuck! | That sounds so old and stuffy. | Just call me Cleo!
- entry[014] 0x04a5-0x052e: Zeus | Actually, | You see I have a daughter. | I came here to meet her. | Perhaps I made a mistake | in coming, though.
- entry[015] 0x052e-0x0546: Cleo | Huh?
- entry[016] 0x0546-0x065d: Zeus | I'm ashamed to say it, | but my wife took my daughter | and left me ten years ago. | I spent too much time perfecting | my craft, and not enough with my family. | I have had news that | my wife has died, | and my daughter | is living alone on this island.
- entry[017] 0x065d-0x0708: Cleo | Oh... | So that's why | you wanted to come here? | Father and daughter, reunited after 10 years! | How exciting! | Ooh... | I love stories like this!
- entry[018] 0x0708-0x07a4: Zeus | And now I don't know how I will face her, | or what I should say to her. | I'm not good at these sort of things. | What would you do?
- entry[019] 0x07a4-0x080a: Cleo | I'm great at things like this! | Don't worry, I'll tell you | exactly what to do!
- entry[020] 0x080a-0x083f: Zeus | Ah, | that would be a great help.
- entry[021] 0x083f-0x086c: Orphen | Man, | she sure is nosy.
- entry[022] 0x086c-0x08ab: Cleo | So, | do you know where | your daughter is?
- entry[023] 0x08ab-0x0941: Zeus | No... | I thought this was going to be a normal little island. | I didn't realize it would have ancient | buildings like these.
- entry[024] 0x0941-0x098b: Cleo | Orphen, | I'm going to | help Zeus find his daughter.
- entry[025] 0x098b-0x09a6: Orphen | What?
- entry[026] 0x09a6-0x0a08: Cleo | Well, it's not like | sitting around here all day is going to do us any good.
- entry[027] 0x0a08-0x0a36: Orphen | Yeah, that's true but...
- entry[028] 0x0a36-0x0ac9: Cleo | Great! | It's a plan, then. Let's go! | 'A father and daughter meet again after years apart'... | Ooh... | It's so beautiful!
- entry[029] 0x0ac9-0x0b13: Orphen | Never mind that it's an | excuse for you to be nosy.
- entry[030] 0x0b13-0x0b47: Zeus | Are you agreed, | Master Orphen?
- entry[031] 0x0b47-0x0bca: Orphen | Why not? After all, | there could be more monsters out there, | and you look like you'd be good in a fight.
- entry[032] 0x0bca-0x0be5: Zeus | I thank you.
- entry[033] 0x0be5-0x0c2c: Cleo | What could that be? | Let's check it out!
- entry[034] 0x0c2c-0x0cce: Orphen | The only thing is that, well, you haven't seen your daughter | in ten years. Don't you think you're | asking a bit too much?
- entry[035] 0x0cce-0x0d1d: Cleo | Orphen! | Who are you to be lecturing about relationships!
- entry[036] 0x0d1d-0x0d71: Orphen | Watch it! | I may not be touchy-feely, but I do have a heart.
- entry[037] 0x0d71-0x0e3b: Zeus | Master Orphen is right. | Just as he said, | I have no right to expect anything after all these years. | But, if nothing else, I want to see my daughter and | tell her I'm sorry.
- entry[038] 0x0e3b-0x0e5a: Cleo | Oh, Zeus...
- entry[039] 0x0e5a-0x0eb8: Orphen | Enough already. This is getting depressing. | Let's just | get moving.
- entry[040] 0x0eb8-0x0ed4: Cleo | Yea! We're off!

## scr/scr44.out

Header: pointer_table=0x046c-0x04a0, footer=0x44ac, records=11, text_records=11

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x005c: Orphen | Not too bad for an old man.
- entry[001] 0x005c-0x0141: Cleo | There's something written here... | 'We are the Guardians of Wisdom. | If you we spy, here your bones will lie. | We are stones, possessed of life.' | Now that's weird... | Orphen, you go first!
- entry[002] 0x0141-0x0165: Orphen | Oh gee, thanks, Cleo.
- entry[003] 0x0165-0x01a0: Cleo | Did you see that! That stone just moved!
- entry[004] 0x01a0-0x01c8: Cleo | Look! It moved again!
- entry[005] 0x01c8-0x0240: Cleo | Orphen! They have eyes! If they see you, | you'll be trapped! | Be careful! Don't let them see you!
- entry[006] 0x0240-0x0276: Cleo | You big dummy! You let them see you!
- entry[007] 0x0276-0x02bd: Cleo | Hey, look... Somebody carved a message here!
- entry[008] 0x02bd-0x0378: Volcan | I leave this message to any fellow travelers that find | themselves trapped in the maze. | Go right, along the wall! Right! | - V. Volcano, the Masmaturian Bulldog
- entry[009] 0x0378-0x0433: Cleo | Hey, Volcan wrote it! | I bet he lied about going right. | That's just the kind of nasty thing he would do. | Orphen, go left! | The way out is to the left, along the wall!
- entry[010] 0x0433-0x046c: Zeus | We're counting on you, Master Orphen.

## scr/scr45.out

Header: pointer_table=0x00d8-0x00e8, footer=0x236c, records=2, text_records=2

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x0099: Cleo | Wow! It's like a giant roulette wheel! | That switch in the middle must make it spin!
- entry[001] 0x0099-0x00d8: Cleo | Line up the gates to get to the other side.

## scr/scr46.out

Header: pointer_table=0x03bc-0x03f4, footer=0x31c4, records=13, text_records=12

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x00cc: Orphen | There was supposed to be a library down here. | I was hoping to find out more | about this weird island. | But if this is it...
- entry[001] 0x00cc-0x0133: Zeus | This water also means that | we won't be able to use the underground passageway.
- entry[002] 0x0133-0x0165: Cleo | So, | like, | where are we?
- entry[003] 0x0165-0x01b3: Cleo | That's not far away at all. | Come on, let's go!
- entry[004] 0x01b3-0x01f8: Orphen | And how just how do you propose we get there?
- entry[005] 0x01f8-0x0244: Cleo | Leave that to me! | Lucky, be a good boy and wait here, okay?
- entry[006] 0x0244-0x02ac: Cleo | We're finally out! All thanks to me! | How about a word of thanks, Orphen?
- entry[007] 0x02ac-0x02db: Orphen | Yes, Ma'am. Thank you.
- entry[008] 0x02db-0x0306: Cleo | That wasn't so hard, was it?
- entry[009] 0x0306-0x0331: Cleo | That needs checking out.
- entry[010] 0x0331-0x037f: Cleo | Aha! A chain! | This should lower the drawbridge! | Ungh!
- entry[011] 0x037f-0x03ba: Cleo | I wonder what this device was used for?

## scr/scr47.out

Header: pointer_table=0x041c-0x046c, footer=0x27dc, records=19, text_records=18

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x005d: Orphen | What is this place?
- entry[001] 0x005d-0x009c: Magnus | It looks like a laboratory of some kind.
- entry[002] 0x009c-0x00d2: Zeus | A lab? | For Quaris' experiments?
- entry[003] 0x00d2-0x0120: Orphen | It seems that | some very cruel experiments took place.
- entry[004] 0x0120-0x015a: Zeus | What does it say!? | What about Azel?
- entry[005] 0x015a-0x01ea: Orphen | Calm down... Here's the deal: | Our friend Quaris | has been conducting experiments here, | to control the flow of time.
- entry[006] 0x01ea-0x020c: Zeus | For what end?
- entry[007] 0x020c-0x0294: Orphen | That I don't know. | This is what it says... | 'Captured two young girls to use as test | subjects. One escaped.'
- entry[008] 0x0294-0x02c4: Hahahaha! | Kellu Rahpat Gabar!
- entry[009] 0x02c4-0x02da: Stop!
- entry[010] 0x02da-0x02f0: Argh!
- entry[011] 0x02f0-0x0308: Aiyiee!
- entry[012] 0x0308-0x031d: Ahh!
- entry[013] 0x031d-0x034e: Run... | Never mind me, just go!
- entry[014] 0x034e-0x036b: I'm sorry.
- entry[015] 0x036b-0x039a: Zeus | Did... | Did Azel escape?
- entry[016] 0x039a-0x03f0: Orphen | I don't know. We have to | find that evil witch, and fast! | Let's go!
- entry[017] 0x03f0-0x041b: Orphen | Okay, here we go...

Header: pointer_table=0x0de4-0x0ebc, footer=0x4c34, records=53, text_records=52

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x0054: Magnus | \u201a\xa4\u201a\xed\ufffd[\u201a\xc1\ufffdI
- entry[001] 0x0054-0x0075: \u0192Z\u0192t\u0192B | \u0192L\u0192\u0192\ufffd[\u0192b\ufffdI
- entry[002] 0x0075-0x0090: Orphen | \u201a\xa4\u201a\xa8\ufffd[\u201a\xc1\ufffdI
- entry[003] 0x0090-0x00ec: Orphen | You've got nowhere | to run to now. | Prepare yourself.
- entry[004] 0x00ec-0x0105: Quaris | !!
- entry[005] 0x0105-0x0149: Zeus | Witch! | You hurt | my daughter! | Time to pay!
- entry[006] 0x0149-0x0187: Orphen | Don't even | think of trying something!
- entry[007] 0x0187-0x01ae: Rani | Stop! | You can't!
- entry[008] 0x01ae-0x01c9: Zeus | What!?
- entry[009] 0x01c9-0x01f7: Cleo | Rani! | What do you mean?
- entry[010] 0x01f7-0x0267: Rani | You can't... | Zeus, | This woman... | Quaris... | She's Azel! | She's your daughter | Azel!
- entry[011] 0x0267-0x0284: Quaris | What!?
- entry[012] 0x0284-0x02b2: Zeus | What are you... | saying?
- entry[013] 0x02b2-0x02f1: Cleo | You can't | expect us to | believe that!
- entry[014] 0x02f1-0x0320: Rani | Azel... | Azel, I'm sorry.
- entry[015] 0x0320-0x0358: Quaris | Rani... | What did | you just say?
- entry[016] 0x0358-0x03ac: Rani | Azel, | this man is Zeus. | Your father. | He came here for you.
- entry[017] 0x03ac-0x03e0: Quaris | Zeus? | . . . | Da... | Daddy?
- entry[018] 0x03e0-0x0409: Orphen | What in the world?
- entry[019] 0x0409-0x04ab: Rani | Listen! | Azel and I were both kidnapped. | I was chosen first... | For the experiments. | But | Azel | saved me. | She took my place.
- entry[020] 0x04ab-0x04c6: Zeus | What!!
- entry[021] 0x04c6-0x0512: Rani | The experiment | didn't go right. | It made Azel old...
- entry[022] 0x0512-0x0549: Orphen | Why | didn't you tell us sooner?
- entry[023] 0x0549-0x05e3: Rani | When Azel | took my place, I ran. | I left her behind... | I was too ashamed | to tell you. | Azel | got old because | I ran away.
- entry[024] 0x05e3-0x0648: Quaris | Rani... | It's not your fault. | It was | my decision. | Don't blame yourself.
- entry[025] 0x0648-0x0664: Rani | Azel...
- entry[026] 0x0664-0x06aa: Zeus | Is it true? | Are you really | my | little Azel?
- entry[027] 0x06aa-0x06f3: Quaris | Daddy... | I, | I remember | sitting on your lap.
- entry[028] 0x06f3-0x071a: Zeus | Ohh... | Ohhhhh...
- entry[029] 0x071a-0x075d: Cleo | Wait! | So then | who is that old woman, Jado?
- entry[030] 0x075d-0x0777: Quaris | Oh!
- entry[031] 0x0777-0x0791: Zeus | Azel!
- entry[032] 0x0791-0x081c: Jado | Hehehehe... | I'll be taking back the Crystal Egg now, thank you. | I find it quite essential for my time experiments.
- entry[033] 0x081c-0x0838: Quaris | Jado!
- entry[034] 0x0838-0x085d: Zeus | What's going on?
- entry[035] 0x085d-0x08c2: Rani | The evil sorceress, the one that | ran all those experiments... | It was | Jado!
- entry[036] 0x08c2-0x08ee: Cleo | What? | That old woman!?
- entry[037] 0x08ee-0x0924: Orphen | I see... | I knew it all along.
- entry[038] 0x0924-0x095e: Rani | Jado, | give us back the Crystal Egg!
- entry[039] 0x095e-0x09a4: Jado | Don't be stupid! | The Egg | is mine! All mine!
- entry[040] 0x09a4-0x09f6: Rani | Without the Egg, | we can't cure Azel. | Orphen, get the Egg!
- entry[041] 0x09f6-0x0aba: Orphen | Old woman, if there's two things | I hate, | it's rich undertakers | and old bats with funny ideas. | Be smart, and leave the Egg right there. | You know you can't beat us.
- entry[042] 0x0aba-0x0af4: Zeus | Jado! | You've got | no where to run!
- entry[043] 0x0af4-0x0bb7: Jado | All my plans would have succeeded | if not for you meddling strangers! | You'll pay! | If I can't have | my youth back | then I'll take Chaos Island with me... | To the grave!
- entry[044] 0x0bb7-0x0be5: Cleo | Oh! | She's getting away!
- entry[045] 0x0be5-0x0c32: Orphen | I don't like the sound of that. | What's she planning?
- entry[046] 0x0c32-0x0ccb: Quaris | Jado | experimented on herself at first. | She seems to have gone | completely mad... | Oh, no! | She's | headed for the volcano!
- entry[047] 0x0ccb-0x0cf8: Cleo | Huh? | What do you mean?
- entry[048] 0x0cf8-0x0d82: Quaris | If the Crystal Egg falls into the lava, | the heat will set off the Egg and | Chaos island will sink under the sea!
- entry[049] 0x0d82-0x0da4: Orphen | Follow her!
- entry[050] 0x0da4-0x0dc2: Zeus | To the volcano!
- entry[051] 0x0dc2-0x0de1: \u201ad\u201au\u201aQ\u201aU\u201aU\ufffdi\u2018\xe4\u017d\u0152\u2013\xb3\u201a\xb5\ufffdj
