Header: pointer_table=0x04d4-0x0548, footer=0x6388, records=28, text_records=27

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x005e: Cleo | It's a dead end, Orphen.
- entry[001] 0x005e-0x00a8: Orphen | Look at that sluice gate! | x | I wonder what its for...
- entry[002] 0x00a8-0x00dc: Mar | Look. There's a switch over there.
- entry[003] 0x00dc-0x00f9: Mar | Huh?
- entry[004] 0x00f9-0x0119: Mar | ! . . . Mommy?
- entry[005] 0x0119-0x0145: Cleo | Mar! Where are you going?
- entry[006] 0x0145-0x016c: Mar | ! Get out of my way!
- entry[007] 0x016c-0x018a: Mar | I'm coming!
- entry[008] 0x018a-0x01d1: Orphen | So this is what | - | was making the music.
- entry[009] 0x01d1-0x0220: Cleo | It's some kind of music box. | \u2013 | The water must make it run.
- entry[010] 0x0220-0x0253: Mar | I thought it was | K | my mother...
- entry[011] 0x0253-0x026b: Orphen | ! !
- entry[012] 0x026b-0x028a: Orphen | Get back!
- entry[013] 0x028a-0x02a9: Mar | What's wrong?
- entry[014] 0x02a9-0x02de: Orphen | There's something in the water!
- entry[015] 0x02de-0x0304: Mar | The organ...
- entry[016] 0x0304-0x0331: Orphen | That thing's | done for.
- entry[017] 0x0331-0x0357: Orphen | Whoa! Earthquake!
- entry[018] 0x0357-0x0379: Cleo | It's a big one!
- entry[019] 0x0379-0x0392: Cleo | Aiyee!
- entry[020] 0x0392-0x03b0: Orphen | Get down!
- entry[021] 0x03b0-0x03d0: Orphen | I think...
- entry[022] 0x03d0-0x03ee: Cleo | Is it over?
- entry[023] 0x03ee-0x0414: Mar | I know this song...
- entry[024] 0x0414-0x042c: Cleo | Mar!
- entry[025] 0x042c-0x049a: Mar | I have | < | to follow the music. | \xf5 | Whereever it's coming from, that's | \xa4 | where I have to go.
- entry[026] 0x049a-0x04d3: Cleo | What are you doing? | Let's get going!

## scr/scr59.out

Header: pointer_table=0x0564-0x05cc, footer=0x4608, records=24, text_records=24

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x0085: Orphen | So these are the Endless Stairs. | n | What nutjob designed this?
- entry[001] 0x0085-0x00c9: Cleo | So | where is it? | Z | The Crystall Egg fragment?
- entry[002] 0x00c9-0x0128: Orphen | Who knows if it even exists. | \u203a | You can't trust those damned halflings.
- entry[003] 0x0128-0x0165: Mar | Look... | Over there, | something's glowing!
- entry[004] 0x0165-0x018e: Cleo | That's it! | Let's go!
- entry[005] 0x018e-0x01be: Cleo | It was around here, wasn't it?
- entry[006] 0x01be-0x01da: Mar | Maybe...
- entry[007] 0x01da-0x01f0: Mar | Ahh!
- entry[008] 0x01f0-0x0218: Orphen | What | the hell!?
- entry[009] 0x0218-0x0246: Cleo | Orphen! | See, it worked!
- entry[010] 0x0246-0x026c: Orphen | What do you mean?
- entry[011] 0x026c-0x02d1: Cleo | Look... | We're | Z | in a different time! | \xb4 | I told you I was right about the Egg.
- entry[012] 0x02d1-0x0308: Orphen | But | all it did was make it night.
- entry[013] 0x0308-0x0329: Cleo | Yeah, | But...
- entry[014] 0x0329-0x035f: Orphen | But nothing! | < | Find another one!
- entry[015] 0x035f-0x039d: Mar | Aha! | Over there! | - | Something's glowing!
- entry[016] 0x039d-0x03d6: Cleo | Okay! | It's sure to work this time!
- entry[017] 0x03d6-0x03fb: Cleo | I don't | get it...
- entry[018] 0x03fb-0x0413: Mar | Oh, no.
- entry[019] 0x0413-0x044a: Orphen | Oh | Hell! | < | This is not funny!
- entry[020] 0x044a-0x04a5: Orphen | - | I'm totally soaked. | Z | Damn it. | \xb4 | That's some time machine, Cleo!
- entry[021] 0x04a5-0x04dd: Cleo | It's not my fault! | Z | Don't blame me!
- entry[022] 0x04dd-0x0532: Orphen | Damn that | Volcan! | x | We'll never get | to winter at this rate!
- entry[023] 0x0532-0x0564: Mar | Let's try one more time, okay?

## scr/scr60.out

Header: pointer_table=0x0520-0x0580, footer=0x4330, records=23, text_records=22

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x0066: Cleo | There she is, Orphen! | Hurry!
- entry[001] 0x0066-0x0096: Orphen | Damn! | Look at her move!
- entry[002] 0x0096-0x00b7: Zeus | Yeearggh! | Jado!!
- entry[003] 0x00b7-0x00d9: Cleo | Eeyaa!
- entry[004] 0x00d9-0x00ff: Orphen | Cleo! Look out!
- entry[005] 0x00ff-0x0123: Orphen | Ouch! | Damn!
- entry[006] 0x0123-0x014f: Cleo | Orphen! | Are you okay?
- entry[007] 0x014f-0x0174: Zeus | Are you injured?
- entry[008] 0x0174-0x01ab: Orphen | I'm okay. | What about you guys?
- entry[009] 0x01ab-0x01ce: Zeus | We'll survive.
- entry[010] 0x01ce-0x01f6: Cleo | Orphen! | Hurry up!
- entry[011] 0x01f6-0x0230: Orphen | What do you expect me to do!? Swim?
- entry[012] 0x0230-0x026c: Cleo | You'll have to go around the other way!
- entry[013] 0x026c-0x02d5: Orphen | Easy for you to say! Have you seen | the balls of molten lava falling over there?
- entry[014] 0x02d5-0x032f: Cleo | That's your fault, not mine! | You're the one who was lagging behind!
- entry[015] 0x032f-0x038b: Orphen | How did she get to be so damned annoying? | All right! | I'm going around!
- entry[016] 0x038b-0x03ce: Orphen | Well that was a miserable experience.
- entry[017] 0x03ce-0x0413: Cleo | Aw, you needed a little exercise anyway, Orphen.
- entry[018] 0x0413-0x046d: Zeus | Master Orphen, | We have to catch Jado before she reaches the summit.
- entry[019] 0x046d-0x049d: Orphen | Yeah, you're right. | Let's go!
- entry[020] 0x049d-0x04cf: Cleo | It's really hot down here!
- entry[021] 0x04cf-0x051d: Cleo | It's like a sauna! | Ooh, maybe I'll lose some weight!

## scr/scr61.out

Header: pointer_table=0x0350-0x038c, footer=0x3484, records=14, text_records=13

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x005f: Cleo | This must be the place.
- entry[001] 0x005f-0x008f: Orphen | Where is that | evil hag?
- entry[002] 0x008f-0x00b1: Zeus | There she is!
- entry[003] 0x00b1-0x00e5: Jado | Curses! | They're still following me.
- entry[004] 0x00e5-0x011c: Cleo | Not again! | What do we do?
- entry[005] 0x011c-0x014f: Orphen | Damn! | We're so close, too!
- entry[006] 0x014f-0x018e: Jado | Hehehe. | You're all going to die with me!
- entry[007] 0x018e-0x01f8: Zeus | Foul hag! | You won't escape justice! | Master Orphen, | I'll find a way. | Leave it to me!
- entry[008] 0x01f8-0x024c: Orphen | We've got you now, Jado! | You won't escape this time!
- entry[009] 0x024c-0x027d: Cleo | Hand over | the Crystal Egg!
- entry[010] 0x027d-0x02ae: Zeus | Jado, | You're going to pay!
- entry[011] 0x02ae-0x0300: Jado | No, | it is you who will pay! | No one | can stop me! | Die, fools!
- entry[012] 0x0300-0x034f: Cleo | Zeus! | Knock those pillars | down so we can walk over them

## scr/scr62.out

Header: pointer_table=0x042c-0x0480, footer=0x279c, records=19, text_records=19

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x0057: Cleo | What's happening?
- entry[001] 0x0057-0x0087: Mar | Is the volcano coming to life?
- entry[002] 0x0087-0x00fa: Orphen | I don't think so. This island has been | acting up ever since that music machine was destroyed.
- entry[003] 0x00fa-0x011e: Cleo | What do you mean?
- entry[004] 0x011e-0x0188: Orphen | I'm not sure, but I think the earthquakes | are related to the music machine somehow.
- entry[005] 0x0188-0x01c8: Orphen | Great! I can't see anything. | Nobody move!
- entry[006] 0x01c8-0x0205: Cleo | I can't see my own feet, where would I go?
- entry[007] 0x0205-0x023b: Mar | I'll go on ahead. | Follow my flute!
- entry[008] 0x023b-0x0267: Mar | Hey! Cleo! Orphen!
- entry[009] 0x0267-0x02a1: Cleo | Look at all this soot! I need a shower!
- entry[010] 0x02a1-0x02d6: Orphen | At least we're all in one piece.
- entry[011] 0x02d6-0x02ed: Mar | Yeah!
- entry[012] 0x02ed-0x0316: Cleo | Hey... | Look, Orphen!
- entry[013] 0x0316-0x0339: Orphen | What is that??
- entry[014] 0x0339-0x0368: Cleo | Do you think it has showers?
- entry[015] 0x0368-0x03b4: Orphen | It's not a health spa! | Anyway, we should check it out.
- entry[016] 0x03b4-0x03d4: Orphen | Be careful.
- entry[017] 0x03d4-0x03fa: Cleo | You can do it, Mar.
- entry[018] 0x03fa-0x042c: Cleo | Come on! We don't have all day.

## scr/scr63.out

Header: pointer_table=0x10d4-0x11d4, footer=0xa898, records=63, text_records=62

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x006c: Cleo | Ohh! | The tunnel is full of monsters!
- entry[001] 0x006c-0x00c6: Orphen | Grannie here will get waxed | before we get near that music machine.
- entry[002] 0x00c6-0x0124: Jado | The seal is nearly broken. | We must fix the organ! | Every minute counts!
- entry[003] 0x0124-0x0141: Mar | Let's go.
- entry[004] 0x0141-0x01af: Orphen | Cleo and I will hold off the monsters here. | Mar, take the old lady to the pipe organ!
- entry[005] 0x01af-0x01da: Cleo | Will they be okay alone?
- entry[006] 0x01da-0x0206: Orphen | Can you handle it, Mar?
- entry[007] 0x0206-0x025f: Mar | Yeah! | Come on, Grannie. Let's go. | I won't let those bugs get you.
- entry[008] 0x025f-0x029d: Jado | Mar... | Fate works | in mysterious ways...
- entry[009] 0x029d-0x02d4: Orphen | Good job, Mar. | Way to go.
- entry[010] 0x02d4-0x0304: Mar | Thanks! | I could do it again.
- entry[011] 0x0304-0x033a: Cleo | We're not out of trouble yet. Look!
- entry[012] 0x033a-0x038f: Orphen | Unreal. | Don't they ever give up? | Cleo! Mar! Let's take 'em!
- entry[013] 0x038f-0x03da: Jado | Oh Guardian of Time! | Guide me to the True Path!
- entry[014] 0x03da-0x0404: Cleo | It worked! It's fixed!
- entry[015] 0x0404-0x0425: Jado | No, not quite.
- entry[016] 0x0425-0x043e: Cleo | What?
- entry[017] 0x043e-0x04ee: Jado | It's not doing anything. | Perhaps not even the Crystal Egg | can completely repair this machine... | But, not to fear. | I think if I do some tinkering...
- entry[018] 0x04ee-0x05f0: Jado | For generations, my family has been cursed | with the duty of protecting this island with our music. | Our task is made harder by the fact that only music played by | a young child will maintain the seal that holds the demon in check.
- entry[019] 0x05f0-0x061d: Mar | Does it have to be a girl?
- entry[020] 0x061d-0x0635: Jado | What?
- entry[021] 0x0635-0x066f: Mar | Would it work if a boy played the music?
- entry[022] 0x066f-0x0697: Cleo | Mar, what are you...
- entry[023] 0x0697-0x06ef: Jado | It is possible. There are instances when boys have | played the Song.
- entry[024] 0x06ef-0x070a: Mar | Really!?
- entry[025] 0x070a-0x07b5: Jado | But a boy can only hold the demon in check | for a short time. | And even then, it comes at the price of his life. | Everything is resting on Rani now.
- entry[026] 0x07b5-0x07c8: Mar
- entry[027] 0x07c8-0x080c: Jado | We have no need for | boy-children in our family.
- entry[028] 0x080c-0x0829: Mar | . . . . . .
- entry[029] 0x0829-0x0928: Jado | Our lives are not our own. We are tied to this | island from birth. I tried to relieve us of our fate | by constructing this self-playing pipe organ. | But it breaks down quite often, so we're still trapped | on this accursed island.
- entry[030] 0x0928-0x0957: Jado | Okay... | That should do it.
- entry[031] 0x0957-0x0983: Cleo | Really!? So Rani will...
- entry[032] 0x0983-0x09a1: Mar | It's moving!
- entry[033] 0x09a1-0x09d3: Orphen | Then everything's okay again?
- entry[034] 0x09d3-0x0a0e: Jado | Nooo! Not now! | We didn't make it time!
- entry[035] 0x0a0e-0x0a35: Orphen | What are you mean?
- entry[036] 0x0a35-0x0a9d: Jado | It is as I feared. Rani's exhaustion influenced | the Song. | The beast has awakened!
- entry[037] 0x0a9d-0x0ab9: Cleo | But how!?
- entry[038] 0x0ab9-0x0ad5: Jado | Ohhhhhh!
- entry[039] 0x0ad5-0x0af0: Orphen | Jado!
- entry[040] 0x0af0-0x0b0b: Mar | Grandma!
- entry[041] 0x0b0b-0x0b4b: Jado | Ohh! It's all over! | The island is doomed...
- entry[042] 0x0b4b-0x0b6c: Orphen | What's that?
- entry[043] 0x0b6c-0x0bd3: Jado | A passageway to the underground labyrinth has opened! | That's where the demon lies.
- entry[044] 0x0bd3-0x0bff: Orphen | Hmm. Cleo, ready to go?
- entry[045] 0x0bff-0x0c1d: Cleo | Definitely.
- entry[046] 0x0c1d-0x0c85: Jado | Now you're thinking. Get off this island as soon as you can! | Mar, you must go, too.
- entry[047] 0x0c85-0x0cb8: Cleo | No, we're going after the demon.
- entry[048] 0x0cb8-0x0cd5: Jado | W-w-what??
- entry[049] 0x0cd5-0x0d5c: Orphen | If we wanted to run away, we would have done it a long time ago. | Without a ship, we prefer to stick it out here.
- entry[050] 0x0d5c-0x0e03: Cleo | That's right. Plus it wouldn't look | very cool if we let the island sink, would it? | The only thing left to do is | to kill that silly old demon.
- entry[051] 0x0e03-0x0e62: Jado | Don't be fools! | It's not just some monster! | Your powers cannot stop it!
- entry[052] 0x0e62-0x0eca: Orphen | Who wants to grow old and die in a bed? | I'll show the demon how a sorcerer dies.
- entry[053] 0x0eca-0x0f30: Cleo | Bravo! Bravo! | You're the greatest of them all, Orphen! | You're finally a hero!
- entry[054] 0x0f30-0x0f67: Orphen | Hey! What do you mean, 'finally'!?
- entry[055] 0x0f67-0x0fb8: Jado | Mar, you must get off the island. | You can use | my sailboat.
- entry[056] 0x0fb8-0x0fe8: Mar | No, I'm not going to run away.
- entry[057] 0x0fe8-0x1002: d | Jado | Mar...
- entry[058] 0x1002-0x106c: Mar | I finally understand why | I'm on this island. | That's why... | I have to go with them!
- entry[059] 0x106c-0x109b: Jado | Oh... Gods, | Protect him...
- entry[060] 0x109b-0x10ac: Mar | Grannie!!
- entry[061] 0x10ac-0x10d1: Mar | Come one, let's go.

## scr/scr64.out

Header: pointer_table=0x1468-0x15d4, footer=0xada8, records=90, text_records=88

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x005f: Cleo | Wake up! | Get up!
- entry[001] 0x005f-0x00b5: Cleo | Come on! You lazy, | rotten, useless, | mean, grumpy old sorcerer!
- entry[002] 0x00b5-0x00d3: Orphen | Grrargh!!
- entry[003] 0x00d3-0x0105: Cleo | Wahh!! | You... | You're awake?
- entry[004] 0x0105-0x014b: Orphen | I'm awake now. | Now what was it you were saying?
- entry[005] 0x014b-0x01cb: Cleo | I was just trying to get you to wake up. | You know, there's no sign of Magnus or | those other two dimwits!
- entry[006] 0x01cb-0x020a: Orphen | Huh? | Are we at Arvanrama? | I guess not.
- entry[007] 0x020a-0x0259: Cleo | Apparently, that ship | was never going to Arvanrama at all.
- entry[008] 0x0259-0x027f: Orphen | What do you mean?
- entry[009] 0x027f-0x02f2: Cleo | Dortin said something | about it before the ship sank. | Volcan got on the wrong ship, he said.
- entry[010] 0x02f2-0x036d: Orphen | You're kidding! | That pea-brained insect! | I'll beat him into a pulp | and drop him down an anthole!
- entry[011] 0x036d-0x03c6: Cleo | Well, that's gonna be hard, | because he's missing, and so is Magnus!
- entry[012] 0x03c6-0x043a: Orphen | Then maybe the sharks have done me | the favor of finally getting rid of that amoeba-wannabe!!
- entry[013] 0x043a-0x047d: Cleo | Sounds like you're running | out of bad names...
- entry[014] 0x047d-0x04b3: Orphen | We should go find Magnus, though.
- entry[015] 0x04b3-0x04ca: Cleo | Huh?
- entry[016] 0x04ca-0x04f1: Orphen | Sounds like... | A flute.
- entry[017] 0x04f1-0x0517: Cleo | Huh?
- entry[018] 0x0517-0x0552: Cleo | Hey... | It won't open... | Ungh!
- entry[019] 0x0552-0x0593: Orphen | Maybe it's locked. | Move over, let me try.
- entry[020] 0x0593-0x05b3: Orphen & Cleo | . . . . . . .
- entry[021] 0x05b3-0x05e1: Cleo | Hmm. | Shoddy construction.
- entry[022] 0x05e1-0x0614: Orphen | Umm, | Cleo... Push, not pull.
- entry[023] 0x0614-0x062e: Cleo | Really?
- entry[024] 0x062e-0x0653: Orphen | . . . | Cleo!
- entry[025] 0x0653-0x0675: Cleo | Oops. Hahaha...
- entry[026] 0x0675-0x06d1: Orphen | Thanks to you, we can't open it | from this side. | Now what do we do!?
- entry[027] 0x06d1-0x0720: Cleo | Okay, sorry. Sheesh. | I'll go up the hill and take a look.
- entry[028] 0x0720-0x073a: Orphen | Why?
- entry[029] 0x073a-0x0774: Cleo | There might be another way in up there.
- entry[030] 0x0774-0x0798: Orphen | Hey! | Hold up!
- entry[031] 0x0798-0x07c3: Cleo | Stay there. I'll be right back!
- entry[032] 0x07c3-0x07f8: Cleo | Huh? | What just...
- entry[033] 0x07f8-0x0820: Cleo | Oh! | It's beautiful!
- entry[034] 0x0820-0x0861: Cleo | Hey Orphen! | Look at the sunset! | Orphen?
- entry[035] 0x0861-0x0884: Cleo | Where did he go?
- entry[036] 0x0884-0x08c8: Cleo | What's going on? | How come the weather keeps changing?
- entry[037] 0x08c8-0x08f7: Cleo | Hello? | Oh...
- entry[038] 0x08f7-0x092d: Mar | Ah! | You're the girl from the ship!
- entry[039] 0x092d-0x095b: Cleo | What are you doing up here?
- entry[040] 0x095b-0x0983: Cleo | That was you playing?
- entry[041] 0x0983-0x0999: Mar | Yep!
- entry[042] 0x0999-0x09d6: Cleo | You came to this island | all by yourself?
- entry[043] 0x09d6-0x0a52: Mar | Yeah... | I'm trying to find this song. | Somebody told me they heard it | while they were on this island.
- entry[044] 0x0a52-0x0a8a: Cleo | You came all this way to hear a song?
- entry[045] 0x0a8a-0x0acc: Mar | This song | is going to lead me | to my mother.
- entry[046] 0x0acc-0x0ae4: Cleo | What?
- entry[047] 0x0ae4-0x0b23: Mar | I'm... | actually | trying to find my mother.
- entry[048] 0x0b23-0x0b42: Cleo | Your mother?
- entry[049] 0x0b42-0x0b9e: Mar | We were separated when I was just a baby, | and I don't remember her face.
- entry[050] 0x0b9e-0x0bd8: Cleo | Hmm... | So how does | the song fit in?
- entry[051] 0x0bd8-0x0c4f: Mar | The only thing I remember about my | mother is this song. | She used to play it | to me all the time.
- entry[052] 0x0c4f-0x0d09: Cleo | All right, | Clearly you need my help! | I'm just not sure how I can help yet... | Anyway, | leave everything up to me! | I'm very experienced with this sort of thing!
- entry[053] 0x0d09-0x0d23: Mar | You are?
- entry[054] 0x0d23-0x0d49: Cleo | Trust me. | I'll find her!
- entry[055] 0x0d49-0x0d9c: Cleo | Aha! There you are! | Where have you been, Orphen?
- entry[056] 0x0d9c-0x0def: Orphen | What're you talking about? | I was right here the whole time!
- entry[057] 0x0def-0x0e43: Cleo | You were? | So you saw that sunset then... | Wasn't it beautiful?
- entry[058] 0x0e43-0x0e82: Orphen | Sunset? | Have you gone completely looney?
- entry[059] 0x0e82-0x0ecb: Cleo | Me? You're the one that spaced on | an entire sunset!!
- entry[060] 0x0ecb-0x0f01: Orphen | Forget it. | What's with the kid?
- entry[061] 0x0f01-0x0f48: Cleo | Oh, he was on the ship with us. | His name is, uh...
- entry[062] 0x0f48-0x0f5e: Mar | Mar.
- entry[063] 0x0f5e-0x0fa5: Cleo | This mean old sorcerer is Orphen. | Okay, let's go!
- entry[064] 0x0fa5-0x0fd5: Orphen | Go? Go where!? | Hey! Wait!
- entry[065] 0x0fd5-0x0ff4: Cleo | Hmm? | What?
- entry[066] 0x0ff4-0x1041: Orphen | What!? That kid, that's what! | We're taking him along?
- entry[067] 0x1041-0x10c0: Cleo | Of course. | Look at this place... | Can't you see he needs our help? | Or don't you care if he gets killed?
- entry[068] 0x10c0-0x10f7: Orphen | No, I, err... | You're here alone?
- entry[069] 0x10f7-0x110e: Mar | Yeah.
- entry[070] 0x110e-0x11c6: Cleo | He came here to find his mother. | He traveled all this way by himself! | Isn't he brave? | I'm going to help him find his mother. | And you're going to help me.
- entry[071] 0x11c6-0x11e7: Orphen | What! | Me?
- entry[072] 0x11e7-0x1219: Orphen | Just stay out of my way, kid.
- entry[073] 0x1219-0x123f: Mar | Don't worry, I will.
- entry[074] 0x123f-0x1270: Cleo | Hurry up, Orphen! | Let's go!
- entry[075] 0x1270-0x128e: Cleo | Hurry up, Orphen!
- entry[076] 0x128e-0x12cb: Orphen | How are we going to get up there?
- entry[077] 0x12cb-0x12fe: Cleo | Mar, think you can do it?
- entry[078] 0x12fe-0x1315: Mar | Yeah.
- entry[079] 0x1315-0x133a: Cleo | Bend down, Orphen.
- entry[080] 0x133a-0x1354: Orphen | What?
- entry[081] 0x1354-0x138d: Cleo | So I can get on your shoulders, dummy!
- entry[082] 0x138d-0x13b4: Orphen | . . . Oohhff. . .
- entry[083] 0x13b4-0x13e1: Orphen | Ungh! Hurry up, already!
- entry[084] 0x13e1-0x1402: Cleo | Stand still, Orphen!
- entry[086] 0x1404-0x1426: Orphen | What's wrong?
- entry[087] 0x1426-0x144b: Mar | We can go this way.
- entry[088] 0x144b-0x1466: Mar | Let's go.

## scr/scr66.out

Header: pointer_table=0x052c-0x0590, footer=0x7138, records=24, text_records=23

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x005a: Cleo | Hey! | I saw someone!
- entry[001] 0x005a-0x008a: Orphen | Huh? | I don't see anybody.
- entry[002] 0x008a-0x00cd: Cleo | But I saw really someone! | Z | Lucky saw them too.
- entry[003] 0x00cd-0x0126: Orphen | Probably just a big rat or something. | \xb4 | Come on, let's keep moving.
- entry[004] 0x0126-0x014d: Cleo | Someone was there...
- entry[005] 0x014d-0x018d: Orphen | Uh-oh. | The bridge is washed out.
- entry[006] 0x018d-0x01ed: Cleo | I bet | - | we could cross over if we knocked | x | that boulder into the river.
- entry[007] 0x01ed-0x0254: Orphen | Yeah, | ( | but how the hell are we supposed to | n | get over there in the first place?
- entry[008] 0x0254-0x028f: Mar | I could get there | by going through here.
- entry[009] 0x028f-0x02b4: Cleo | It's too dangerous.
- entry[010] 0x02b4-0x02fc: Mar | Don't worry. | - | I can do it. | x | Wait here for me here.
- entry[011] 0x02fc-0x032c: Mar | I can do this...
- entry[012] 0x032c-0x034f: Cleo | Way to go, | Mar!
- entry[013] 0x034f-0x0368: Mar | Hahaha.
- entry[014] 0x0368-0x03a1: Orphen | Hey, | how the heck did you do that?
- entry[015] 0x03a1-0x03d9: Cleo | What does it matter? | Z | Come on, let's go!
- entry[016] 0x03d9-0x040b: Mar | Shh! . . . | Can you hear it?
- entry[017] 0x040b-0x0426: Cleo | What?
- entry[018] 0x0426-0x044d: Orphen | From over there?
- entry[019] 0x044d-0x0495: Cleo | Huh? | - | How come we can hear it | \xa5 | from so far away?
- entry[020] 0x0495-0x04c2: Orphen | Mar, | is that the song?
- entry[021] 0x04c2-0x0503: Mar | Yeah... | Z | It's calling me... | \xb4 | I have to go.
- entry[022] 0x0503-0x052a: Cleo | Come on, | let's go.

## scr/scr68.out

Header: pointer_table=0x1000-0x10ec, footer=0xc36c, records=58, text_records=57

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x006a: Orphen | Ah, not another dead end?
- entry[001] 0x006a-0x00b4: Magnus | Master, look! | There's something strange over here.
- entry[002] 0x00b4-0x00e6: Magnus | I wonder what it could be...
- entry[003] 0x00e6-0x012c: Orphen | Hmm... | Magnus, stick your hand in there.
- entry[004] 0x012c-0x0191: Orphen | That must be a keyhole. | It probably only opens for the right kind of person.
- entry[005] 0x0191-0x01c7: Magnus | The right kind of person?
- entry[006] 0x01c7-0x0217: Orphen | Yeah, you know, a good, honest person... | Someone like me.
- entry[007] 0x0217-0x0257: Magnus | So why don't you stick your hand in there?
- entry[008] 0x0257-0x02b1: Orphen | Are you crazy!? | It might be dangerous! I can't afford to get hurt.
- entry[009] 0x02b1-0x02fa: Magnus | But you just said you were the right kind of person!
- entry[010] 0x02fa-0x032a: Sephy | Perhaps I should try.
- entry[011] 0x032a-0x034c: Orphen | Whoa!
- entry[012] 0x034c-0x0397: Magnus | It opened because Sephy has such a pure heart.
- entry[013] 0x0397-0x03de: Sephy | Well, I don't know about that... | Let's just keep going.
- entry[014] 0x03de-0x0440: Volcan | What are you doing!? | Let me go, you money-grubbing sorcerer!
- entry[015] 0x0440-0x048c: Orphen | Shut up! | You're in for a good old-fashioned beating!
- entry[016] 0x048c-0x04fb: Dortin | Please don't hurt me... | I tried to make him stop... | I'll tell you the secret!
- entry[017] 0x04fb-0x0568: Volcan | Dortin! Shut your trap! | You don't have to | tell this penniless bum anything!
- entry[018] 0x0568-0x059e: Orphen | That's enough out of you!
- entry[019] 0x059e-0x05d7: Orphen | Okay Dortin, | Let's hear it.
- entry[020] 0x05d7-0x0652: Dortin | Uh, well, | If you answer the stone face | with the word 'mirror', | a secret passage will appear...
- entry[021] 0x0652-0x069c: Orphen | Hmm... | Interesting. | Very interesting. | Thanks.
- entry[022] 0x069c-0x06e7: Dortin | Umm, | you'll give me a share of the loot now, right?
- entry[023] 0x06e7-0x0729: Orphen | Oh, of course... | Here's your down payment!
- entry[024] 0x0729-0x0765: Magnus | Where did you go off to?
- entry[025] 0x0765-0x0799: Orphen | Oh, taking care of business...
- entry[026] 0x0799-0x07d4: Magnus | Huh? | Did you figure out the answer?
- entry[027] 0x07d4-0x0806: Stone Guardian | Speak, mortal!
- entry[028] 0x0806-0x0838: Orphen | The answer is... | A mirror!
- entry[029] 0x0838-0x0870: Magnus | Master, look! | Down there!
- entry[030] 0x0870-0x0890: Orphen | Excellent!
- entry[031] 0x0890-0x08ba: Magnus | Ah, | My turn again?
- entry[032] 0x08ba-0x08eb: Orphen | You're welcome to stay here.
- entry[033] 0x08eb-0x0916: Sephy | Goodbye, Magnus.
- entry[034] 0x0916-0x094b: Magnus | Hey! | Wait for me! I'm coming!
- entry[035] 0x094b-0x0990: Magnus | What is this place? | It's spooky.
- entry[036] 0x0990-0x09b0: Orphen | Yep, pretty weird.
- entry[037] 0x09b0-0x0a30: Stone Guardian | If you would see the light of day again, | you must solve this riddle and prove your worth.
- entry[038] 0x0a30-0x0a4b: Orphen | Whoa!
- entry[039] 0x0a4b-0x0a73: Magnus | Master! What is it?
- entry[040] 0x0a73-0x0b2d: Stone Guardian | If you look upon my form, | Your own form will I take. | The entire world | fits inside me. | Yet even a child | can bear my weight. | What am I? | Answer!
- entry[041] 0x0b2d-0x0b77: Orphen | Hmm. I'm no good at riddles. | Sephy, got an ideas?
- entry[042] 0x0b77-0x0ba3: Sephy | I can't think what it could be.
- entry[043] 0x0ba3-0x0bf2: Volcan | Naa-naa, naa-naa! | Come and get me, Orphen!
- entry[044] 0x0bf2-0x0c33: Dortin | Volcan, | I wouldn't do that. He'll just...
- entry[045] 0x0c33-0x0d07: Volcan | Shaddup! | He wanted to take all the treasure, and leave me with nothing! | Me! Lord Volcan Volcano, the Masmaturian Bulldog! | And now he's stuck down there! | Serves you right, Sorcerer!
- entry[046] 0x0d07-0x0dae: Orphen | You punks | have a lot of guts showing up here! | You oversized sewer rats! | We wouldn't be in this mess | if it weren't for you two idiots!
- entry[047] 0x0dae-0x0e1d: Volcan | Cry me a river! | You pathetic excuse for a sorcerer! | Have fun down there in the maze!
- entry[048] 0x0e1d-0x0e77: Dortin | Volcan, maybe you shouldn't make him any angrier than he already is.
- entry[049] 0x0e77-0x0eb1: Orphen | Too late! | You're dead meat, Volcan!
- entry[050] 0x0eb1-0x0ee1: Magnus | \u2030\xbd\u201a\xa9\ufffdo\u201a\xc4\u201a\xab\u201a\xbb\u201a\xa4\u201a\xc5\u201a\xb7\u201a\xcb
- entry[051] 0x0ee1-0x0f18: Orphen | \u2022|\u201a\xaf\u201a\xe8\u201a\xe1\u2018\xd2\u201a\xc1\u201a\xc4\u201a\xbd\u201a\xc1\u201a\xc4\u201a\xe6\u201a\xa9\u201a\xc1\u201a\xbd\u201a\xf1\u201a\xbe\u201a\xba
- entry[052] 0x0f18-0x0f4f: Magnus | \u2018\xe5\ufffd\xe4\u2022v\u201a\xc5\u201a\xb7\u201a\xe6\ufffdB\u2018\xe5\ufffd\xe4\u2022v\u201a\xc5\u201a\xb7\u201a\xaf\u201a\xc7\ufffdE\ufffdE\ufffdE
- entry[053] 0x0f4f-0x0f70: \u201ad\u201au\u201aP\u201aQ\u201aU\ufffdi\u0192Z\u0192\u0160\u0192t\u2013\xb3\u201a\xb5\ufffdj
- entry[054] 0x0f70-0x0f98: Magnus | \u201a\xa8\u017dt\u2014l\u201a\xc1\ufffdI\ufffdI
- entry[055] 0x0f98-0x0fbf: Orphen | \u201a\xa8\u201a\xa8\ufffdA\u2013\xb3\u017d\u2013\u201a\xbe\u201a\xc1\u201a\xbd\u201a\xa9
- entry[056] 0x0fbf-0x0ffe: Orphen | \ufffdE\ufffdE\ufffdE\u201a\xb3\u201a\xc4\ufffdA\u201a\xb1\u201a\xf1\u201a\xc8\ufffd\u0160\u201a\xc5\u201a\xae\u201a\xb8\u201a\xae\u201a\xb8\u201a\xb5\u201a\xc4\u201a\xe7\u201a\xea\u201a\xcb\u201a\xa6

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x0051: \u201ad\u201au\u201aP\u201aQ\u201aW\ufffdi\u0192Z\u0192\u0160\u0192t\u2013\xb3\u201a\xb5\ufffdj
- entry[001] 0x0051-0x0076: \u201ad\u201au\u201aP\u201aQ\u201aX\ufffdi\u0192Z\u0192\u0160\u0192t\u2013\xb3\u201a\xb5\ufffdj
- entry[002] 0x0076-0x009f: Magnus | Hey! Master!
- entry[003] 0x009f-0x00d7: Orphen | Ah, you two finally caught up, eh?
- entry[004] 0x00d7-0x0129: Orphen | This could be tricky... | We better split up and go one by one.
- entry[005] 0x0129-0x0151: Magnus | Good luck, Master!
- entry[006] 0x0151-0x0179: Sephy | Be careful, Orphen.
- entry[007] 0x0179-0x01a8: Orphen | \u201a \u201a\xa2\u201a\xc2\u201a\xe7\ufffdA\u2018\xe5\ufffd\xe4\u2022v\u201a\xa9\u201a\xc8\ufffdE\ufffdE\ufffdE

## scr/scr70.out

Header: pointer_table=0x0340-0x037c, footer=0x38c4, records=14, text_records=13

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x0054: Cleo | Did it work?
- entry[001] 0x0054-0x00aa: Orphen | We are in a different time, at least. | The entrance has moved.
- entry[002] 0x00aa-0x00cc: Cleo | You're right!
- entry[003] 0x00cc-0x00fb: Zeus | Ah! | Azel's tomb is gone!
- entry[004] 0x00fb-0x013a: Orphen | Then we must be in the right time again.
- entry[005] 0x013a-0x0190: Cleo | If there's no tomb, | then your daughter | must be alive, right?
- entry[006] 0x0190-0x01cd: Zeus | Hmm... | I indeed hope that is the case.
- entry[007] 0x01cd-0x0228: Orphen | You of all people have to believe! | Anyway, let's go find her. | Come on!
- entry[008] 0x0228-0x024f: Cleo | Hey, what's that?
- entry[009] 0x024f-0x0293: Orphen | That must be the center of the island.
- entry[010] 0x0293-0x02d3: Zeus | That's where Azel is supposed to be living.
- entry[011] 0x02d3-0x030e: Orphen | We might as well go and take a look.
- entry[012] 0x030e-0x033f: Cleo | Don't worry, Zeus, we'll find her.

## scr/scr72.out

Header: pointer_table=0x08d8-0x0948, footer=0x2bf4, records=27, text_records=26

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x006d: Magnus | Whoa! | It's so beautiful...
- entry[001] 0x006d-0x00b1: Magnus | Umm, Sephy? | Can I ask you a question?
- entry[002] 0x00b1-0x00d9: Sephy | Yes... | What is it?
- entry[003] 0x00d9-0x0101: Magnus | Well... | I, uh...
- entry[004] 0x0101-0x0132: Sephy | [giggle] | Is it about Rufus?
- entry[005] 0x0132-0x0175: Magnus | Yes... | I was wondering what | he was like.
- entry[006] 0x0175-0x01b5: Sephy | Well... | Magnus, do you have a girlfriend?
- entry[007] 0x01b5-0x021d: Magnus | Me? No, uh, girls don't like me. | And, you know, I'm busy with training and stuff.
- entry[008] 0x021d-0x02b1: Sephy | [giggles] | Until I met Rufus, I didn't think | I'd ever be in love, either. | He taught me what it was to love someone.
- entry[009] 0x02b1-0x0395: Sephy | Rufus was a treasure hunter. | He traveled all over the world, looking for jewels and mysterious relics. | He started out as an archeologist, but eventually, | he quit to become a full time treasure hunter.
- entry[010] 0x0395-0x03c9: Magnus | Where did you meet him?
- entry[011] 0x03c9-0x0487: Sephy | I was raised from birth to be a performer in the Royal Palace. | I trained all day, every day. | I had no chance to make friends, or even to see the outside world.
- entry[012] 0x0487-0x0541: Sephy | When I met Rufus, all that changed. | He would tell me stories about his travels, stories about strange relics. | Rufus was a dreamer. I loved that about him.
- entry[013] 0x0541-0x05ee: Sephy | When he told his stories, | his eyes would shine, like a little boy's eyes. | I fell head over heels in love with him. | And we swore to marry...
- entry[014] 0x05ee-0x066b: Sephy | Then one day, | his letters just stopped coming. | Eventually, | I heard that he had been killed.
- entry[015] 0x066b-0x06aa: Magnus | I'm sorry. | I shouldn't have pried.
- entry[016] 0x06aa-0x0706: Sephy | No, it's all right. | I'm sorry to have burdened you | with such talk.
- entry[017] 0x0706-0x0728: Magnus | Don't worry!
- entry[018] 0x0728-0x0741: Sephy | Huh?
- entry[019] 0x0741-0x0792: Magnus | I'll make sure you get there safely! | You can count on me!
- entry[020] 0x0792-0x07c8: Sephy | Thank you, Magnus. | I trust you.
- entry[021] 0x07c8-0x07e3: Magnus | Aww...
- entry[022] 0x07e3-0x07fd: Magnus | Ah!!
- entry[023] 0x07fd-0x0869: Orphen | Sorry to barge in on you love birds.. | Sephy, are you sure we're in the right place?
- entry[024] 0x0869-0x08a0: Sephy | Yes. The temple should be just over there.
- entry[025] 0x08a0-0x08d5: Magnus | What's wrong? Let's keep going.

## scr/scr73.out

Header: pointer_table=0x0b04-0x0b84, footer=0x3bbc, records=31, text_records=30

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x00a7: Sephy | You were right, Orphen. | Before coming here, | I trained in various combat techniques.
- entry[001] 0x00a7-0x016e: Sephy | But I wasn't sure | how far I could get | on my own. I knew it would | be difficult to penetrate the island's defences. | So I used | you two, | to help me reach my objective.
- entry[002] 0x016e-0x01a2: Magnus | Used us? | You didn't use us...
- entry[003] 0x01a2-0x0278: Sephy | I saw Orphen's pendant on the ship. | I knew | it was the mark of Tower of Fangs, | And that only the most powerful sorcerers wore it. | I decided then I would use you to help me get the Egg.
- entry[004] 0x0278-0x02c2: Orphen | I'd like to know just what | your real objective was.
- entry[005] 0x02c2-0x033a: Sephy | I never got over Rufus' death. | I just couldn't put him behind me. | I tried. | I really tried...
- entry[006] 0x033a-0x0354: Magnus | . . .
- entry[007] 0x0354-0x03c2: Sephy | I would read his letters, | and cry myself to sleep at night. | Every day was like that.
- entry[008] 0x03c2-0x0465: Sephy | As I told you earlier, | Rufus was a treasure hunter. | He came here looking for a legendary treasure. | A treasure known as the Crystal Egg.
- entry[009] 0x0465-0x04fc: Sephy | In the last letter he sent | me before he died, | he said he had learned that the Crystal Egg | could bring the dead back to life.
- entry[010] 0x04fc-0x053c: Orphen | Impossible. | Sephy, | You're | not serious?
- entry[011] 0x053c-0x0589: Sephy | I lied about praying for him... | What I really wanted...
- entry[012] 0x0589-0x05e3: Magnus | Was bring your dead fiance back to life? | It's not possible, Sephy.
- entry[013] 0x05e3-0x069f: Sephy | Even the slimmest chance | was reason to hope. | That hope | is all that has kept me going. | I would do anything | for a chance | to be with him again. Anything.
- entry[014] 0x069f-0x06ff: Orphen | We should go back to the Tomb of Gaia. | The others will be waiting there for us.
- entry[015] 0x06ff-0x0757: Magnus | This is Rani. | She knows all the roads and passageways.
- entry[016] 0x0757-0x0773: Rani | Hello.
- entry[017] 0x0773-0x07a0: Orphen | Look how tiny she is!
- entry[018] 0x07a0-0x082e: Magnus | Master! Don't be rude! | Rani, this is my teacher. | He's not as bad as he sounds. | And he is a pretty good sorcerer.
- entry[019] 0x082e-0x0868: Rani | Nice to | meet you, | Mr. Magician.
- entry[020] 0x0868-0x08af: Orphen | I'm not a magician! Magicians do parlor tricks!
- entry[021] 0x08af-0x08e7: Magnus | Yeah, anyway... | This is, ah...
- entry[022] 0x08e7-0x094e: Zeus | My name is Zeus. | I'm here looking for my daughter. | She's just about your age.
- entry[023] 0x094e-0x09d4: Rani | Really? | I don't have very many friends here. | I do have one best friend, though. | We're practically sisters.
- entry[024] 0x09d4-0x0a28: Orphen | Rani, do you know how to get into the underground labyrinth?
- entry[025] 0x0a28-0x0a50: Rani | You mean the Maze?
- entry[026] 0x0a50-0x0a74: Magnus | You know it?
- entry[027] 0x0a74-0x0ac5: Rani | Yeah, I know it... | I can take you near there if you want.
- entry[028] 0x0ac5-0x0ae4: Magnus | Really?
- entry[029] 0x0ae4-0x0b02: Rani | Okay, let's go.

## scr/scr74.out

Header: pointer_table=0x1e4c-0x2018, footer=0xf614, records=114, text_records=112

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x005f: Cleo | Orphen! | Magnus!
- entry[001] 0x005f-0x007a: Orphen | Whoa!
- entry[002] 0x007a-0x0094: Magnus | Ohh!
- entry[003] 0x0094-0x0105: Cleo | Hey! | Where have you two been? | Wandering around here lost this whole time no doubt!
- entry[004] 0x0105-0x0134: Orphen | Uh, so... | You're alive?
- entry[005] 0x0134-0x01c1: Cleo | Like you care whether I'm alive! | How could you leave a frail, innocent girl like me | all alone on this weird island!
- entry[006] 0x01c1-0x020b: Magnus | But, Cleo, where | were you? | We tried to find you!
- entry[007] 0x020b-0x028c: Cleo | When I woke up, | I was near this really strange building. | I just wandered around until I found this place.
- entry[008] 0x028c-0x02be: Cleo | Who's that? (a little jealous)
- entry[009] 0x02be-0x0305: Magnus | Oh, this is Sephy. | She was on the ship with us.
- entry[010] 0x0305-0x0321: Cleo | I see...
- entry[011] 0x0321-0x0391: Sephy | It's nice to meet you. | I am greatly indebted to | Orphen and Magnus for all their help.
- entry[012] 0x0391-0x03ee: Cleo | Well, well. | Orphen actually helping out a stranger... | That's a first!
- entry[013] 0x03ee-0x0441: Magnus | Oh, Master | is only doing it because she's going to... Ouch!
- entry[014] 0x0441-0x0493: Orphen | Yeah, well, | You know me, always helping out those in need.
- entry[015] 0x0493-0x04ca: Cleo | Yeah, right. Where are we, anyway?
- entry[016] 0x04ca-0x0506: Magnus | Some place called Chaos Island.
- entry[017] 0x0506-0x0529: Cleo | How can that be?
- entry[018] 0x0529-0x057e: Orphen | It looks like those idiotic midgets | put us on the wrong boat!
- entry[019] 0x057e-0x05cd: Cleo | I knew it! | When I get my hands on them, they'll be sorry!
- entry[020] 0x05cd-0x062f: Orphen | Uh-oh, she's on the warpath! | I'd hate to be those two when she finds 'em.
- entry[021] 0x062f-0x0668: Cleo | Anyway, let's hurry up | and go home.
- entry[022] 0x0668-0x06cd: Orphen | Yeah, well, about that... | We've just got this one little errand to run first.
- entry[023] 0x06cd-0x06f6: Cleo | What!? | What errand?
- entry[024] 0x06f6-0x0745: Magnus | We're have to escort Sephy to this holy temple near here.
- entry[025] 0x0745-0x07ae: Cleo | Come on! | I'm tired, and I'm starving... | I'm don't want to go to some dumb temple.
- entry[026] 0x07ae-0x07e0: Orphen | Okay, stay here then.
- entry[027] 0x07e0-0x0859: Cleo | What!? | Are you really going to go off and leave me alone again? | Fine! | Go then! See if I care!
- entry[028] 0x0859-0x0897: Cleo | Really! | It's unbelievable!
- entry[029] 0x0897-0x08cc: Orphen | What... | What's unbelievable?
- entry[030] 0x08cc-0x097a: Cleo | I could understand if it was only Magnus. | But even you are all googly-eyed over her. | Sure, she has an okay body. | And she's pretty. | But still...
- entry[031] 0x097a-0x09b2: Orphen | Ha ha! | Feeling a little jealous?
- entry[032] 0x09b2-0x09ed: Cleo | ! | Don't be stupid! | I'm not jealous!
- entry[033] 0x09ed-0x0a01: Lucky | Rrrrrr...
- entry[034] 0x0a01-0x0a29: Cleo | Lucky? | What is it?
- entry[035] 0x0a29-0x0a43: Orphen | Huh?
- entry[036] 0x0a43-0x0a63: Cleo | Ah! | Lucky!
- entry[037] 0x0a63-0x0a88: No! | Get away, shoo!
- entry[038] 0x0a88-0x0ab7: Cleo | Hello? | Is somebody there?
- entry[039] 0x0ab7-0x0ad0: Lucky | Rrrrrr!
- entry[040] 0x0ad0-0x0af7: No! | Let go! | Let go!
- entry[041] 0x0af7-0x0b2b: Orphen | Who's there!? | Show yourself!
- entry[042] 0x0b2b-0x0b7f: Cleo | Lucky! | Let go right now! | Who | are you? | Do you live here?
- entry[043] 0x0b7f-0x0bbe: Jado | You're friends of | that woman, aren't you?
- entry[044] 0x0bbe-0x0bdf: Orphen | What woman?
- entry[045] 0x0bdf-0x0c27: Jado | It won't work! | I won't let you | get away with it!
- entry[046] 0x0c27-0x0c72: Cleo | Hold on, what do you mean? | Lucky! | Leave her alone!
- entry[047] 0x0c72-0x0c97: Cleo | Hey! Wait!
- entry[048] 0x0c97-0x0cc5: Orphen | What was that all about?
- entry[049] 0x0cc5-0x0d1b: Cleo | Something about some woman | and getting away with... | Something.
- entry[050] 0x0d1b-0x0d35: Orphen | Hmm.
- entry[051] 0x0d35-0x0d5b: Cleo | What did she mean?
- entry[052] 0x0d5b-0x0d73: Orphen | Who knows.
- entry[053] 0x0d73-0x0dc9: Cleo | Something about some woman | and getting away with... | Something.
- entry[054] 0x0dc9-0x0e40: Zeus | Ah, Master Orphen! | Will you join me? | This confounded island is | proving most challenging!
- entry[055] 0x0e40-0x0e89: Cleo | Hey Orphen... | What about your pretty little friend?
- entry[056] 0x0e89-0x0ebd: Cleo | Aren't you | forgetting somebody?
- entry[057] 0x0ebd-0x0efe: Zeus | Excellent! | Together we shall be unstoppable!
- entry[058] 0x0efe-0x0f34: Zeus | So be it. Good luck, Master Orphen.
- entry[059] 0x0f34-0x0f71: Mar | Hi. | Are you going to come with me?
- entry[060] 0x0f71-0x0fba: Cleo | Hey Orphen... | What about your pretty little friend?
- entry[061] 0x0fba-0x0fee: Cleo | Aren't you | forgetting somebody?
- entry[062] 0x0fee-0x1025: Mar | Thanks! | I was lonely all by myself.
- entry[063] 0x1025-0x1048: Mar | Okay, nevermind.
- entry[064] 0x1048-0x1077: Orphen | Hi Sephy...
- entry[065] 0x1077-0x1092: Sephy | Hello.
- entry[066] 0x1092-0x1187: Orphen | I, uh... | Look, | it's not that | I don't trust you or anything like that. | It's just that, you know, as a money-lender, | it's my responsibility to make sure that my | clients stay alive long enough to pay me back.
- entry[067] 0x1187-0x11e9: Sephy | Oh, if this is about your fee, | I assure you, I will pay you when I get home.
- entry[068] 0x11e9-0x1219: Magnus | Master! | What's going on?
- entry[069] 0x1219-0x1324: Orphen | Well, | it's not really that.It's just, you know... | We were on a sinking ship together, | and we washed ashore at the very same place... | It might be bad karma to leave you all by yourself. | I'd like to see this thing through to the end.
- entry[070] 0x1324-0x1341: Magnus | Master?
- entry[071] 0x1341-0x137b: Sephy | So you want to travel together again?
- entry[072] 0x137b-0x13b9: Orphen | Ah, | yeah, that's what it comes down to.
- entry[073] 0x13b9-0x1414: Sephy | Magnus! Orphen said | that we're going to be traveling together again.
- entry[074] 0x1414-0x14a3: Magnus | Do you mean it? | All right! | Back together again with Sephy! | And don't pretend | you aren't happy about it, either.
- entry[075] 0x14a3-0x14dd: Orphen | Hey! | I'm just in it for the money.
- entry[076] 0x14dd-0x150c: Sephy | Thank you both, very much.
- entry[077] 0x150c-0x157e: Sephy | There should be an underground corridor | over there that will take us to the Temple. | Shall we go?
- entry[078] 0x157e-0x1620: Sephy | \u201a\xa8\u201c\xf1\ufffdl\u201a\xc9\u2013{\u201c\u2013\u201a\xcc\u017d\u2013\u201a\xf0\u201a\xa8\u02dcb\u201a\xb5\u201a\xb5\u201a\xc8\u201a\xa9\u201a\xc1\u201a\xbd\u201a\xcc\u201a\xcd | \ufffd\u201a\xb5\u2013\xf3\u201a\xc8\u201a\xad\u017dv\u201a\xc1\u201a\xc4\u201a\xa8\u201a\xe8\u201a\xdc\u201a\xb7" | \u201a\xaf\u201a\xea\u201a\xc7\ufffdA\ufffd\xc5\ufffd\u2030\u201a\xa9\u201a\xe7\u2013{\u201c\u2013\u201a\xcc\u017d\u2013\u201a\xf0\u201a\xa8\u02dcb\u201a\xb5\u201a\xb5\u201a\xc4\u201a\xe0 | \ufffdM\u201a\xb6\u201a\xc4\u201a\xcd\u201a\xe0\u201a\xe7\u201a\xa6\u201a\xc8\u201a\xa2\u201a\xc6\u017dv\u201a\xc1\u201a\xc4\ufffdE\ufffdE\ufffdE
- entry[079] 0x1620-0x1645: Magnus | \u0192Z\u0192t\u0192B\u201a\xb3\u201a\xf1\ufffdE\ufffdE\ufffdE
- entry[080] 0x1645-0x1688: Orphen | \u201a\xdc\u201a \ufffdA\u201a\xc6\u201a\xc9\u201a\xa9\u201a\xad\ufffdA\u201a\xb1\u201a\xea\u201a\xa9\u201a\xe7\u201a\xc7\u201a\xa4\u201a\xb7\u201a\xe9\u201a\xa9\ufffdl\u201a\xa6\u201a\xcb\u201a\xa6\u201a\xc6\u201a\xc8
- entry[081] 0x1688-0x16b0: Sephy | \u201a \u201a\xcc\u2014\u2018\u201a\xaa\u201a\xc8\u201a\xa2\u201a\xc6\ufffdE\ufffdE\ufffdE
- entry[082] 0x16b0-0x16d3: Magnus | \u0192Z\u0192t\u0192B\u201a\xb3\u201a\xf1\ufffdI\ufffdH
- entry[083] 0x16d3-0x171d: Zeus | Ah, Master Orphen. | I hoped you would join me.
- entry[084] 0x171d-0x176a: Cleo | Oh my, are you giving up | on your pretty little friend?
- entry[085] 0x176a-0x17aa: Cleo | Shouldn't you be worrying about someone else?
- entry[086] 0x17aa-0x17db: Zeus | Very good. | shall we go then?
- entry[087] 0x17db-0x1817: Zeus | I understand... | Another time, perhaps.
- entry[088] 0x1817-0x187d: Mar | If you help me | I'm sure I'll find what | I'm looking for. | Help me, please?
- entry[089] 0x187d-0x18ca: Cleo | Oh my, are you giving up | on your pretty little friend?
- entry[090] 0x18ca-0x190a: Cleo | Shouldn't you be worrying about someone else?
- entry[091] 0x190a-0x1937: Mar | Really? You'll help? Yeah!!
- entry[092] 0x1937-0x195d: Mar | Oh, okay. Nevermind.
- entry[093] 0x195d-0x19a6: Orphen | I just wish there was some way I could help.
- entry[094] 0x19a6-0x19d8: Magnus | Master! | Sephy! | I got it!
- entry[095] 0x19d8-0x19f7: Orphen | Got what?
- entry[096] 0x19f7-0x1acc: Magnus | Remember when we first came ashore here? | At the 'Tower of Mercy' or whatever? | On the top floor of the tower, | There were some fragments from the Crystal Egg. | Do you remember, Sephy?
- entry[097] 0x1acc-0x1ae5: Sephy | Yes.
- entry[098] 0x1ae5-0x1b67: Magnus | I think we can | use those egg fragments | to jump back in time, | to a time when the Egg there was whole.
- entry[099] 0x1b67-0x1bab: Orphen | It sounds logical, but | will it really work?
- entry[100] 0x1bab-0x1c41: Magnus | I don't know... | But it's the only chance we have. | Don't forget Rani, she still needs the Egg too. | Let's give it a shot!
- entry[101] 0x1c41-0x1cc1: Orphen | Well, it's not like we have a way | off this dumb island anyway. | Can't hurt to try it. | Ready, Sephy?
- entry[102] 0x1cc1-0x1cda: Sephy | Yes!
- entry[103] 0x1cda-0x1cfb: Sephy | Magnus, I...
- entry[104] 0x1cfb-0x1d15: Magnus | Yes?
- entry[105] 0x1d15-0x1d4e: Sephy | Thank you | for being so nice to me.
- entry[106] 0x1d4e-0x1d71: Magnus | Aw, I just...
- entry[107] 0x1d71-0x1da2: Orphen | Okay! | Time to get moving!
- entry[108] 0x1da2-0x1dba: Magnus | Yes, sir!
- entry[110] 0x1dbc-0x1dec: Allow Sephy to join your group? | choices: Yes / No
- entry[111] 0x1dec-0x1e1b: Allow Zeus to join your group? | choices: Yes / No
- entry[112] 0x1e1b-0x1e49: Allow Mar to join your group? | choices: Yes / No

- entry[002] 0x0074-0x0093: Jado | Curse you!
- entry[003] 0x0093-0x00af: Cleo | Oh, no!
- entry[004] 0x00af-0x00d1: Zeus | Are you hurt?
- entry[005] 0x00d1-0x0112: Jado | Don't worry about me. | Get the Crystal Egg!
- entry[006] 0x0112-0x0138: Zeus | The Crystal Egg!?
- entry[007] 0x0138-0x0152: Quaris | Ah!
- entry[008] 0x0152-0x016c: Zeus | Stop!
- entry[009] 0x016c-0x01a1: Zeus | Bah. | She got away underground.

- entry[012] 0x01f2-0x0222: Jado | You're Azel's father, aren't you?

- entry[082] 0x0d11-0x0d8e: Orphen | \u201a\xa2\u201a\xe2\u201a\xc1\u201a\xa9\u201a\xdc\u201a\xb5\u201a\xa2\u201a\xa2\ufffdI | \u201a\xc4\u201a\xdf\u201a\xa6\u201a\xc9\u201a\xe1\u201a \u201a\xc8\ufffdA\u2018\xdd\u201a\xb5\u201a\xbd\u2039\xe0\u02c6\xc8\ufffd\xe3\u201a\xcc\u2018\xdd\u201a\xb5\u201a\xaa\u201a \u201a\xe9\u201a\xf1\u201a\xbe\ufffdI | \u201a\xbb\u201a\xcc\ufffd\xe3\ufffdA\ufffdl\u201a\xcc\u0152\xa9\u201a\xc2\u201a\xaf\u201a\xbd\u201a\xa8\u2022\xf3\u201a\xf0\u2030\xa1\u017d\xe6\u201a\xe8\u201a\xb7\u201a\xf1\u201a\xb6\u201a\xe1\u201a\xcb\u201a\xa6\ufffdI

## scr/scr76.out

Header: pointer_table=0x1b30-0x1c9c, footer=0x6d8c, records=89, text_records=89

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x0067: Cleo | Humph! | I'm not talking to you!
- entry[001] 0x0067-0x00ea: Magnus | Master, | This seems to have been a place for pilgrims | to stay when they visited the temple.
- entry[002] 0x00ea-0x0134: Orphen | Maybe. But there's something weird about this place.
- entry[003] 0x0134-0x01a4: Magnus | You're probably right. | Have you noticed that almost nobody | from that ship is on this island?
- entry[004] 0x01a4-0x0254: Magnus | Master, | Did you know that when pilgrims came to pray | at the temple, they would bring gifts for the temple? | It was to make up for their sins.
- entry[005] 0x0254-0x0293: Orphen | How do you know so much about this place?
- entry[006] 0x0293-0x02f4: Magnus | I just do. | Maybe we can find something interesting. | Let's look around.
- entry[007] 0x02f4-0x032f: Orphen | Yeah, maybe there's some money or something.
- entry[008] 0x032f-0x037d: Magnus | We're not looking for money! | We're here to help Sephy.
- entry[009] 0x037d-0x0421: Sephy | Orphen, | Thank you so much for all your help. | There's no way I can thank Magnus enough. | So, where will | you go from here?
- entry[010] 0x0421-0x0465: Orphen | Hmm... | I'm not sure. | We'll play it by ear.
- entry[011] 0x0465-0x04c2: Sephy | Well, I'll | be leaving for the temple soon. | I wish you a safe journey home.
- entry[012] 0x04c2-0x04fd: Zeus | Greetings, young sorcerer.
- entry[013] 0x04fd-0x0520: Orphen | Do I know you?
- entry[014] 0x0520-0x05de: Zeus | Ah, | forgive me. | I was so busy fighting | off those monsters in the corridor that | I neglected | to introduce myself. | I am Zeus. | And you are | Orphen, are you not?
- entry[015] 0x05de-0x05ff: Orphen | Yes. How...?
- entry[016] 0x05ff-0x0654: Zeus | Miss Cleo told me all about you. | I am indebted to her and Lucky.
- entry[017] 0x0654-0x0671: Orphen | To Cleo?
- entry[018] 0x0671-0x06c0: Zeus | Miss Cleo was very kind to me. | Would you thank her for me?
- entry[019] 0x06c0-0x06d5: Orphen | Why me?
- entry[020] 0x06d5-0x0717: Zeus | People her age can be difficult to deal with...
- entry[021] 0x0717-0x0759: Zeus | People her age can be difficult to deal with...
- entry[022] 0x0759-0x0794: Orphen | Don't worry. | I'll thank her for you.
- entry[023] 0x0794-0x0813: Zeus | Excuse me, | Master Orphen, wasn't it? | Miss Cleo was very kind to me. | Would you thank her for me?
- entry[024] 0x0813-0x082f: Orphen | Why me?
- entry[025] 0x082f-0x0871: Zeus | People her age can be difficult to deal with...
- entry[026] 0x0871-0x0901: Orphen | She's hard to take, all right. | I'm not sure it has anything to do with her age, though. | Don't worry, | I'll thank her for you.
- entry[027] 0x0901-0x0945: Zeus | That young lad... | Is he traveling alone?
- entry[028] 0x0945-0x0969: Orphen | Looks that way.
- entry[029] 0x0969-0x09ba: Zeus | My daughter would be about the same age. | Azel... | Where are you...
- entry[030] 0x09ba-0x0a43: Zeus | Master Orphen! | The young woman that you | came with... Will you be accompanying | her from here on as well?
- entry[031] 0x0a43-0x0a6a: Orphen | What's it to you?
- entry[032] 0x0a6a-0x0af6: Zeus | I only want to advise , | you to be careful. | There's more to her | than meets the eye. | Oh! | I must get started. Good luck!
- entry[033] 0x0af6-0x0b32: Mar | Hi. | You're Orphen, right?
- entry[034] 0x0b32-0x0b5d: Orphen | How do you know that?
- entry[035] 0x0b5d-0x0ba3: Mar | Cleo told me about you. | You're a sorcerer, right?
- entry[036] 0x0ba3-0x0bca: Orphen | Yep, that's right.
- entry[037] 0x0bca-0x0c17: Mar | Well, then, can you help me? | I'm trying to find my mom.
- entry[038] 0x0c17-0x0c39: Orphen | Can you pay?
- entry[039] 0x0c39-0x0c63: Mar | I don't have any money.
- entry[040] 0x0c63-0x0cd5: Orphen | Sorry, little man, | I can't make exceptions, even for kids. | Better find someone else to help you.
- entry[041] 0x0cd5-0x0ced: Mar | Oh...
- entry[042] 0x0ced-0x0d38: Mar | Um, you talked to that big guy over there, right?
- entry[043] 0x0d38-0x0d51: Orphen | Yep.
- entry[044] 0x0d51-0x0d87: Mar | Do you think he could be my father?
- entry[045] 0x0d87-0x0da3: Orphen | What!?
- entry[046] 0x0da3-0x0dbd: Mar | Uh, nevermind.
- entry[047] 0x0dbd-0x0dfe: Mar | You know that woman you were talking to?
- entry[048] 0x0dfe-0x0e19: Orphen | Yes...
- entry[049] 0x0e19-0x0e4f: Mar | Do you think she could be my mother?
- entry[050] 0x0e4f-0x0e69: Orphen | Huh?
- entry[051] 0x0e69-0x0e9a: Mar | I wish I had a pretty mother like her.
- entry[052] 0x0e9a-0x0ef9: Magnus | Well, at least | we know Cleo is alive and well. | Master?
- entry[053] 0x0ef9-0x0f44: Orphen | Yeah. | I can't believe I was ever worried about her.
- entry[054] 0x0f44-0x0f81: Magnus | So, Sephy, | Where do we go from here?
- entry[055] 0x0f81-0x0fcc: Sephy | Actually, | I think I'd like to go on alone from here.
- entry[056] 0x0fcc-0x1032: Magnus | What!? | But you | said it was too dangerous | for you to go there by yourself!
- entry[057] 0x1032-0x10f8: Sephy | Now that | you've taken me this far, I think I can make it the rest of the way myself. | The temple should be somewhere below us. | I've caused you enough trouble as it is.
- entry[058] 0x10f8-0x1169: Orphen | Well, | if that's the way you want it... | Of course, there is the small matter of my fee.
- entry[059] 0x1169-0x1186: Magnus | Master!
- entry[060] 0x1186-0x122c: Sephy | Oh, | I'm terribly sorry about this, | but I'm afraid I don't have any money with me. | I will, of course, pay you just as soon as we get home.
- entry[061] 0x122c-0x1296: Orphen | I don't normally take IOUs | in situations like this, | but I guess I can trust you.
- entry[062] 0x1296-0x12c8: Magnus | Master!! | I'm so ashamed...
- entry[063] 0x12c8-0x1329: Orphen | Hey, somebody has to put food on the table! | You want to starve to death?
- entry[064] 0x1329-0x1369: Magnus | Sephy, | are you sure you'll be all right?
- entry[065] 0x1369-0x13ad: Sephy | Yes. | Thank you both for everything. | Farewell.
- entry[066] 0x13ad-0x13cd: Magnus | Ah, Sephy!
- entry[067] 0x13cd-0x1404: Magnus | Are you just going to let her go?
- entry[068] 0x1404-0x14a5: Orphen | There's nothing I can do about it. | She doesn't want us to come with her. | Well, | I guess we better go try and make peace with Cleo.
- entry[069] 0x14a5-0x14b9: Magnus | [sigh]
- entry[070] 0x14b9-0x1530: Cleo | What's with the long face? | Is your little friend in love with Magnus or something?
- entry[071] 0x1530-0x157b: Orphen | Oh, she's in love all right... | Just not with Magnus.
- entry[072] 0x157b-0x15b0: Cleo | Ooh, really!? | Tell me, tell me!
- entry[073] 0x15b0-0x15e9: Orphen | NO! | Now go mind your own business.
- entry[074] 0x15e9-0x1608: Cleo | Fine, be that way!
- entry[075] 0x1608-0x166e: Magnus | Oh man! | We were so close... | It was right there in front of us.
- entry[076] 0x166e-0x1765: Orphen | All the wishing in the world | can't put that egg back together. | And even if it could, | you know just as well as I do | that magic can't | bring the dead back to life. | The dead are better off | left alone, in peace.
- entry[077] 0x1765-0x17c9: Magnus | So you think | Sephy should just give up? | You think she should forget him?
- entry[078] 0x17c9-0x1818: Orphen | I didn't say that! | You just have to think logically about it.
- entry[079] 0x1818-0x1862: Magnus | It seems like there should be a way... | Darnit all!
- entry[080] 0x1862-0x18ff: Sephy | Orphen... | I'm sorry I lost my composure back there. | And I'm sorry I put your life at risk for my own selfish reasons.
- entry[081] 0x18ff-0x1942: Orphen | Don't worry about it. | What will you do now?
- entry[082] 0x1942-0x1974: Sephy | I don't know... | I just don't know.
- entry[083] 0x1974-0x19c7: Zeus | Was there some kind of trouble | with that woman?
- entry[084] 0x19c7-0x19eb: Orphen | Yeah, kind of.
- entry[085] 0x19eb-0x1a6a: Zeus | Hmm. | I thought so. | Argh! | I have to go! | I found out where | my daughter is. | I have to hurry! | Good luck.
- entry[086] 0x1a6a-0x1ab7: Mar | Hi. | Have you heard this | melody anywhere?
- entry[087] 0x1ab7-0x1ad8: Orphen | 'Fraid not.
- entry[088] 0x1ad8-0x1b30: Mar | Someone told me you can hear it here | somewhere, but I can't find out where.

## scr/scr77.out

Header: pointer_table=0x080c-0x0888, footer=0x5c0c, records=30, text_records=29

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x0068: Orphen | Damn... | It was a trap.
- entry[001] 0x0068-0x009b: Jado | Hah... | You survived that, eh?
- entry[002] 0x009b-0x00dd: Orphen | I remember you... | You're that old woman...
- entry[003] 0x00dd-0x0127: Sephy | Please, I have to get out. | I don't have much time!
- entry[004] 0x0127-0x01de: Jado | Do you think I'd let a servant of Quaris | fool me with such a cowardly ruse? | Forget about it, Missy. | Now... | What have you done with my granddaughter!?
- entry[005] 0x01de-0x01f7: Sephy | Huh?
- entry[006] 0x01f7-0x021b: Magnus | Granddaughter?
- entry[007] 0x021b-0x02b0: Sephy | What are you talking about? | I don't know anything about your granddaughter. | Please, you've mistaken us for somebody else.
- entry[008] 0x02b0-0x0351: Jado | Don't play dumb with me, Missy! | You will tell me where she is, sooner or later. | I'll do whatever I have to get her back. Remember that!
- entry[009] 0x0351-0x038a: Sephy | No, wait! | Don't go! | Let us out!
- entry[010] 0x038a-0x03c8: Orphen | Sephy, | Do you know anything about her?
- entry[011] 0x03c8-0x044b: Sephy | No, not a thing... | I've never even been on this island before. | I don't know her, or her granddaughter.
- entry[012] 0x044b-0x048a: Orphen | Hmm. It doesn't sound like you're lying...
- entry[013] 0x048a-0x04e3: Magnus | Sephy would never tell a lie! | That old woman must be mistaken.
- entry[014] 0x04e3-0x054c: Orphen | What did she say? | A 'servant of Quaris'? | Have you ever heard that name before?
- entry[015] 0x054c-0x057f: Orphen | Just what the hell is going on here?
- entry[016] 0x057f-0x05ad: Magnus | Huh? | Ah!
- entry[017] 0x05ad-0x05e0: Orphen | What!? | Did a rat scare you?
- entry[018] 0x05e0-0x05ff: Magnus | No, look!
- entry[019] 0x05ff-0x0678: Magnus | That torch... | There must be a draft somewhere. | Master, I'm going to get us out of here.
- entry[020] 0x0678-0x06ca: Magnus | It's flickering... | There must be a draft somewhere.
- entry[021] 0x06ca-0x06f3: Magnus | Ahhh!
- entry[022] 0x06f3-0x0720: Orphen | An underground passage!
- entry[023] 0x0720-0x0753: Sephy | You're amazing, Magnus!
- entry[024] 0x0753-0x077e: Magnus | Hehe. | All right, let's go.
- entry[025] 0x077e-0x07b8: Magnus | Oh, Master, what are we going to do?
- entry[026] 0x07b8-0x07d6: Orphen | Get lost!
- entry[027] 0x07d6-0x07f0: Orphen | What?
- entry[028] 0x07f0-0x080a: Sephy | Hello.

## scr/scr78.out

Header: pointer_table=0x0310-0x034c, footer=0x3b8c, records=14, text_records=13

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x007d: Orphen | Looks like we're finally out of that dungeon.
- entry[001] 0x007d-0x00a9: Sephy | Thanks to you, Magnus!
- entry[002] 0x00a9-0x00dc: Magnus | Hehe. How did I do, | Master?
- entry[003] 0x00dc-0x012f: Orphen | Better... | At least you didn't embarrass yourself this time.
- entry[004] 0x012f-0x018c: Magnus | Aww, that wasn't very nice! | Why do you always have to make fun of me?
- entry[005] 0x018c-0x01c1: Orphen | Hey, I was just kidding. Sorry.
- entry[006] 0x01c1-0x01d8: Magnus | Humph...
- entry[007] 0x01d8-0x022b: Magnus | Look... | These two torches, the flames are flickering.
- entry[008] 0x022b-0x0263: Sephy | Oh! | It's a secret passage.
- entry[009] 0x0263-0x02c0: Orphen | Ha! Not too shabby. | I guess you're not totally | worthless after all!
- entry[010] 0x02c0-0x02d6: Magnus | Man...
- entry[011] 0x02d6-0x02f0: Magnus | Hey!
- entry[012] 0x02f0-0x030d: Magnus | Master!

## scr/scr79.out

Header: pointer_table=0x0658-0x06b4, footer=0x3bdc, records=22, text_records=21

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x006b: Magnus | Hey, there's Cleo! | Cleo!!
- entry[001] 0x006b-0x00a2: Cleo | Oh, you guys. | What are you doing?
- entry[002] 0x00a2-0x0115: Orphen | No, what are you doing!? | That's the old bag | from before, right? | Why are you with her?
- entry[003] 0x0115-0x0223: Cleo | Orphen! | Polite people don't say 'old bag'! | I ran into her again, and we talked. | Apparently, she thought we were somebody else. | Her granddaughter went missing here, | and she's been trying to find her ever since. | I'm helping her search.
- entry[004] 0x0223-0x026f: Orphen | That old bag, | locked us up in her little dungeon!
- entry[005] 0x026f-0x02f6: Cleo | That's impossible. | She wouldn't do anything like that. | You guys should help her look for her granddaughter!
- entry[006] 0x02f6-0x033c: Orphen | Us? Help her!? | Sorry, we've got our own plans!
- entry[007] 0x033c-0x03bd: Cleo | I'm risking my life here! | This creepy place has monsters everywhere. | What would happen if one found me?
- entry[008] 0x03bd-0x03f7: Orphen | The monster would run away crying...
- entry[009] 0x03f7-0x0419: Cleo | What was that?
- entry[010] 0x0419-0x043b: Orphen | Uh, nothing.
- entry[011] 0x043b-0x0486: Cleo | Well, if you find her, bring her here. | Thanks, guys.
- entry[012] 0x0486-0x04ce: Orphen | Hey! | We're not done talking yet! | Cleo! | Damn.
- entry[013] 0x04ce-0x0500: Magnus | Do you think she'll be okay?
- entry[014] 0x0500-0x0534: Orphen | Who cares! | Anyway, let's get going.
- entry[015] 0x0534-0x0556: Orphen | Whoa!
- entry[016] 0x0556-0x0590: Orphen | Hmm. This could get a little hairy.
- entry[017] 0x0590-0x05d9: Orphen | I'll go on ahead. | You two catch up when you can.
- entry[018] 0x05d9-0x05fe: Magnus | Master!!
- entry[019] 0x05fe-0x0625: Orphen | Ah, you two okay?
- entry[020] 0x0625-0x0655: Orphen | Don't let your guard down.

## scr/scr82.out

Header: pointer_table=0x1a80-0x1be0, footer=0x7864, records=86, text_records=83

- entry[001] 0x0035-0x0078: Magnus | This is where the map was pointing to...
- entry[002] 0x0078-0x00a0: Cleo | Look, | over there...
- entry[003] 0x00a0-0x00dc: Orphen | Ah, | we're directly below | the Tomb.
- entry[004] 0x00dc-0x0127: Cleo | So this is right | under where we were standing before?
- entry[005] 0x0127-0x0150: Magnus | Master! | Look out!
- entry[006] 0x0150-0x0166: Orphen | Uh-oh.
- entry[008] 0x016f-0x0192: Magnus | Master!
- entry[009] 0x0192-0x01af: Cleo | Orphen!
- entry[010] 0x01af-0x01dc: Orphen | What... | What happened?
- entry[011] 0x01dc-0x0226: Magnus | You touched that thing, | and everything went white.
- entry[012] 0x0226-0x02ae: Cleo | The next thing we knew, | we were standing here, | and that | stone came falling down. | Then you appeared, Orphen.
- entry[013] 0x02ae-0x0304: Orphen | So it was all an illusion, | cooked up by that thing? | Damn it!
- entry[014] 0x0304-0x033b: Magnus | M-m- | master! | Look | at that!
- entry[015] 0x033b-0x037a: Orphen | They're nothing but puppets on a string.
- entry[016] 0x037a-0x0397: Cleo | Puppets?
- entry[017] 0x0397-0x03e3: Orphen | Isn't that right, | Rani? | I know | you're out there!
- entry[018] 0x03e3-0x0446: Rani | You are correct. | These people | were once | normal people, with normal lives.
- entry[019] 0x0446-0x046c: Magnus | W-w-who | are you?
- entry[020] 0x046c-0x0545: Rani | Once they touched a Dragon Eye, | they took on new lives and | new personalities, | and travelled here. | They are the cast of the play known as Chaos Island. | In other words, they are actors.
- entry[021] 0x0545-0x0570: Orphen | What is a | Dragon Eye?
- entry[022] 0x0570-0x066d: Rani | It plants the false memories that Gaia creates | into the mind of a person chosen to be an actor. | It records all the emotions they experience as | they wander through the imaginary lives Gaia creates for them | here on this island.
- entry[023] 0x066d-0x068a: Orphen | Emotions?
- entry[024] 0x068a-0x076a: Rani | Yes. | Gaia sustains | itself with the emotions | of humans. | Their anger, fear, grief, and joy. | The emotions the Dragon Eye records | are transferred to Gaia so that it can nourish itself with them.
- entry[025] 0x076a-0x0840: Orphen | Let me see if I got this right... | It takes ordinary people, | puts them in make believe, tear-jerker stories, | all so it can gorge itself on their emotions? | That is seriously messed up.
- entry[026] 0x0840-0x0874: Magnus | Why would it do | such a thing?
- entry[027] 0x0874-0x08d6: Rani | I cannot say. | It was the will of the Ancient Ones, the ones that built Gaia.
- entry[028] 0x08d6-0x091c: Orphen | Built? | You mean this monstrosity is a machine?
- entry[029] 0x091c-0x0988: Rani | To me, it is the source of orders, | nothing more. | I cannot speculate | on its reasons.
- entry[030] 0x0988-0x09f6: Rani | For eons - Eternity - | we have brought new cast members to this island. | But | now...
- entry[031] 0x09f6-0x0a62: Rani | I brought | you here because | I want you | to put the Dragon Eyes into Gaia yourselves.
- entry[032] 0x0a62-0x0a82: Magnus | Us? Why us?
- entry[033] 0x0a82-0x0ab5: Cleo | No! | No way! | That's too weird!
- entry[034] 0x0ab5-0x0b41: Rani | Normally, | each of the three souls sleeping here | would complete its mission | by inserting their Dragon Eye into Gaia.
- entry[035] 0x0b41-0x0b6d: Cleo | Then what happens to them?
- entry[036] 0x0b6d-0x0bdd: Rani | After their mission is completed, | cast members remain here, | to sleep... for all eternity.
- entry[037] 0x0bdd-0x0c14: Cleo | That's just like | being dead, right?
- entry[038] 0x0c14-0x0c7b: Orphen | So if we want to save them, | we have to put the stones into the machine ourselves?
- entry[039] 0x0c7b-0x0d06: Rani | You, too, | are caught in Gaia's web. | If you don't free yourselves, | you will wander this island | for all eternity.
- entry[040] 0x0d06-0x0d24: Magnus | Master...
- entry[041] 0x0d24-0x0d45: Cleo | What do we do?
- entry[042] 0x0d45-0x0d7e: Orphen | Damn. | Looks like we have no choice.
- entry[043] 0x0d7e-0x0d9d: Cleo | O-o- | orphen?
- entry[044] 0x0d9d-0x0ded: Gaia | Gwaaaaa! | Ido... | Unit. | What is... | the meaning of this?
- entry[045] 0x0ded-0x0e0c: Rani | . . . . . . .
- entry[046] 0x0e0c-0x0eb8: Gaia | The memories in the Dragon Eyes | do not correspond to the ones who installed them. | The ones who inserted the stones | are not the ones chosen by Gaia.
- entry[047] 0x0eb8-0x0f10: If the memories are not coordinated, | Our mind will... | There will be...
- entry[048] 0x0f10-0x0f80: Rani | A logic error will occur. The errors will cascade. | Eventually, your system | will overload.
- entry[049] 0x0f80-0x0fb9: Gaia | Ido Unit, | you are acting illogically.
- entry[050] 0x0fb9-0x0fe5: Rani | Yes. | I probably | am...
- entry[051] 0x0fe5-0x0fff: Gaia | Explain.
- entry[052] 0x0fff-0x1100: Rani | I'm tired of watching you play with innocent people like pawns. | You put them through your | horrible little dramas, | over and over again, | until | their hearts | are left barren and empty of emotion. | Then you | leave them to rot.
- entry[053] 0x1100-0x1173: Rani | My existence is meaningless. | Day after day, | I do as I'm ordered. | I can't take it any more.
- entry[054] 0x1173-0x11ee: Gaia | Fool! | Ido | Unit! | Remove | the Dragon Eyes immediately! | Gwo-gwoo-gwooo-gwoooooo. | Immediately....
- entry[055] 0x11ee-0x1287: Rani | I cannot comply with that order. | Gaia, | you must be destroyed. | You cannot be allowed to | destroy the lives of any more humans!
- entry[056] 0x1287-0x12d9: Gaia | Ido... | U-... | nit... | Re-... | move... | the... | Gwaaaaaaa!
- entry[057] 0x12d9-0x12f6: Magnus | Look out!
- entry[058] 0x12f6-0x1324: Orphen | I think we | got it angry.
- entry[059] 0x1324-0x1357: Rani | Destroy Gaia! | Set me free, Orphen!
- entry[061] 0x1360-0x137e: Orphen | You okay?
- entry[062] 0x137e-0x13a6: Cleo | Yeah... | I guess so.
- entry[063] 0x13a6-0x13da: Magnus | It was all one big... | machine?
- entry[064] 0x13da-0x1445: Rani | Now I can finally | be free... | Orphen, take out the Dragon Eyes. | Then Gaia will...
- entry[065] 0x1445-0x146e: Gaia | That | was | foolish...
- entry[066] 0x146e-0x149c: Orphen | Woah! | It's still alive!
- entry[067] 0x149c-0x14f6: Gaia | Without the master, | the slave has no | future. | You have doomed us.
- entry[068] 0x14f6-0x1518: Rani | Ahh... | Aiihhh!
- entry[069] 0x1518-0x1539: Orphen | What's wrong?
- entry[070] 0x1539-0x1566: Quaris | My body! | My body is...
- entry[071] 0x1566-0x15b9: Jado | Oh... | I understand now. | I will die | together with | Gaia.
- entry[072] 0x15b9-0x15de: Magnus | What's happening?
- entry[073] 0x15de-0x169a: Ido | I was created | solely to be a puppet | for Gaia to | control. | When my role ends, | I die and am created again for the next role. | Over and over, for all eternity.
- entry[074] 0x169a-0x16c5: I wanted to | stop the cycle.
- entry[075] 0x16c5-0x1725: I was stupid. | I thought I could live without | Gaia, but it seems I was wrong.
- entry[076] 0x1725-0x1739: Orphen?
- entry[077] 0x1739-0x1757: Orphen | I'm here.
- entry[078] 0x1757-0x17fb: Ido | I'm sorry for | everything. | You couldn't give me freedom, | but at least, | you have released me from | an eternity of sadness. | Thank you.
- entry[079] 0x17fb-0x1853: I would have liked to see | your world. The real world. | Just once...
- entry[080] 0x1853-0x18bb: I wanted to talk | to people... | in a real world... | Not a make believe world...
- entry[081] 0x18bb-0x18dc: Magnus | She's gone...
- entry[082] 0x18dc-0x192d: Cleo | Poor creature... | All she wanted was to be free. | How sad...
- entry[083] 0x192d-0x19e3: Orphen | She must have been a part of the Gaia machine. | All those human tragedies she witnessed... | At some point, | she must have learned how to feel emotion herself.
- entry[084] 0x19e3-0x1a38: Magnus | Who do you suppose | would build a machine | like Gaia? And why?
- entry[085] 0x1a38-0x1a80: Orphen | I don't know. | I don't want to know. | Let's go home.

## scr/scr83.out

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x0050: Mar | \u201a\xc8\u201a\xf1\u201a\xc9\u201a\xe0\u0152\xa9\u201a\xa6\u201a\xc8\u201a\xa2\ufffdE\ufffdE\ufffdE
- entry[001] 0x0050-0x006c: Orphen | \u201a\xa8\u201a\xa2\ufffdA\u201a\xcd\u201a\xae\u201a\xea\u201a\xf1\u201a\xc8\u201a\xe6
- entry[002] 0x006c-0x00c5: Cleo | \u0192I\ufffd[\u0192t\u0192F\u0192\u201c\u201a\xb1\u201a\xbb\ufffdA\u2018S\ufffdg\ufffd^\u201a\xc1\ufffd\u2022\u201a\xc5\ufffdA\u02c6\xc5\u2013\xe9\u201a\xcc\u0192J\u0192\u2030\u0192X\u201a\xb6\u201a\xe1\u201a\xc8\u201a\xa2 | \u201a\xc1\u201a\xc4\u201a\xe4\u201a\xa4\u201a\xa9\ufffdA\u02c6\xc5\u2013\xe9\u201a\xcc\u0192I\ufffd[\u0192t\u0192F\u0192\u201c\u201a\xcb
- entry[003] 0x00c5-0x00e3: Orphen | \u201a\xa4\u201a\xe9\u201a\xb9\u201a\xa6\u201a\xc8\ufffdI\u201a\xa2\u201a\xad\u201a\xbc\ufffdI
- entry[004] 0x00e3-0x0125: Orphen | This could get tricky... | I'll go on ahead.
- entry[005] 0x0125-0x014a: Mar | Be careful, Orphen.
- entry[006] 0x014a-0x0176: Cleo | Orphen, don't lost, okay?
- entry[007] 0x0176-0x0191: Mar | Let's go.

## scr/scr84.out

Header: pointer_table=0x0288-0x02b0, footer=0x1d2c, records=9, text_records=8

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x0066: Orphen | Hey, Magnus. Any luck?
- entry[001] 0x0066-0x00b1: Magnus | Hi, Master. | Actually, | we seem to to be stuck here.
- entry[002] 0x00b1-0x00f9: Sephy | We got in easily enough, but now we can't get out...
- entry[003] 0x00f9-0x01a1: Magnus | Have you noticed that the cages are all linked? | The big chains connect | one cage to another. | Watch what happens when you get in one.
- entry[004] 0x01a1-0x01df: Magnus | Did you see that other cage move?
- entry[005] 0x01df-0x023e: Sephy | Orphen, I think | I saw a passageway in another room, | beneath one of the cages.
- entry[006] 0x023e-0x0267: Magnus | Be careful, Master.
- entry[007] 0x0267-0x0285: Sephy | Orphen...

## scr/scr85.out

Header: pointer_table=0x12fc-0x1410, footer=0x6554, records=67, text_records=67

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x0055: Aeeeiiii!
- entry[001] 0x0055-0x007a: Sephy | What was that?
- entry[002] 0x007a-0x009e: Orphen | I don't know.
- entry[003] 0x009e-0x00bc: Magnus | Sephy!
- entry[004] 0x00bc-0x00fc: Sephy | It's all right. | Don't be afraid.
- entry[005] 0x00fc-0x0114: Rani | Look out!
- entry[006] 0x0114-0x0140: Magnus | Sephy!
- entry[007] 0x0140-0x015e: Orphen | Sephy!
- entry[008] 0x015e-0x0187: Sephy | Are you all right?
- entry[009] 0x0187-0x01a7: Rani | Thank you.
- entry[010] 0x01a7-0x01ed: Sephy | Hello. Are you looking for your | grandmother?
- entry[011] 0x01ed-0x0217: Rani | You know my grandma?
- entry[012] 0x0217-0x0266: Magnus | So this is the girl the | old woman was talking about?
- entry[013] 0x0266-0x028c: Rani | My name is Rani.
- entry[014] 0x028c-0x02c2: Orphen | What do you know about Quaris?
- entry[015] 0x02c2-0x031c: Rani | She's a mean, nasty lady. | She used me in some kind of experiment.
- entry[016] 0x031c-0x033f: Orphen | Experiment?
- entry[017] 0x033f-0x03c0: Rani | Yes, but it didn't work. | She made it so I get older faster than other people. | The experiment hurt me.
- entry[018] 0x03c0-0x03ef: Magnus | But... You're so young?
- entry[019] 0x03ef-0x041b: Rani | I'm | really only ten.
- entry[020] 0x041b-0x043f: Magnus | You're ten!?
- entry[021] 0x043f-0x0497: Orphen | Hmm... | She definitely looks older than 10. Look at that body.
- entry[022] 0x0497-0x04b7: Magnus | Master!!
- entry[023] 0x04b7-0x0534: Rani | Since I got sick, | my body grows every day. | I think... I think I'm going to | die of old age soon.
- entry[024] 0x0534-0x055b: Sephy | That's horrible.
- entry[025] 0x055b-0x05b4: Rani | I just wanted to see | Grandma Jado one more time | before I died.
- entry[026] 0x05b4-0x05f5: Orphen | So you escaped from Quaris and came here?
- entry[027] 0x05f5-0x0617: Cleo | Orphen!! | Magnus!!
- entry[028] 0x0617-0x063b: Magnus | Cleo!
- entry[029] 0x063b-0x0661: Rani | Oh... | Grandma!
- entry[030] 0x0661-0x068f: Jado | Rani? Is that you, Rani?
- entry[031] 0x068f-0x06f4: Jado | Look how big you've gotten since I last saw you. | Children grow up so fast...
- entry[032] 0x06f4-0x0753: Orphen | Huh? | Are you crazy, you senile old bat? | Nobody grows up that fast!
- entry[033] 0x0753-0x0788: Cleo | Orphen! How can you be so rude?
- entry[034] 0x0788-0x07cd: Jado | What, what happened? | What did she do to you?
- entry[035] 0x07cd-0x0809: Rani | Quaris did experiments on me, Grandma.
- entry[036] 0x0809-0x0896: Jado | Oh, no. How could she? | That evil witch! | Ohh... | Even if it costs me my life, | I'll find a cure for you, Rani!
- entry[037] 0x0896-0x08b4: Rani | Grandma!
- entry[038] 0x08b4-0x0908: Magnus | So there's a cure that will put her | body back to normal?
- entry[039] 0x0908-0x0968: Jado | I know what Quaris is trying to do. | She's trying to find eternal youth.
- entry[040] 0x0968-0x098c: Cleo | Eternal youth?
- entry[041] 0x098c-0x0a0d: Jado | I'm sorry I treated you so roughly, Missy. | I thought... It's just that you | look like Quaris, in a way.
- entry[042] 0x0a0d-0x0a29: Sephy | I do?
- entry[043] 0x0a29-0x0b01: Jado | Quaris is quite a beautiful woman. | On the surface, at least. | Her greatest fear is getting old and wrinkly. | She wants to make time stop for her, | so her beauty will be preserved forever.
- entry[044] 0x0b01-0x0b6f: Orphen | Ah... | That would explain why | the experiment caused rapid aging | when it failed.
- entry[045] 0x0b6f-0x0c04: Jado | Somewhere on this island, | there is an object known as the Crystal Egg. | The Crystal Egg can manipulate | the flow of time.
- entry[046] 0x0c04-0x0c2d: Sephy | The Crystal | Egg!
- entry[047] 0x0c2d-0x0c59: Magnus | Sephy, what's wrong?
- entry[048] 0x0c59-0x0c81: Sephy | I, uh... Nothing.
- entry[049] 0x0c81-0x0cdc: Orphen | What about all those fragments of the Egg that you find everywhere?
- entry[050] 0x0cdc-0x0d38: Jado | No, | you need the complete egg to | fully harness its awesome power.
- entry[051] 0x0d38-0x0d8d: Cleo | So, if we had an unbroken egg, | could we cure Rani's illness?
- entry[052] 0x0d8d-0x0e27: Jado | Yes, but Quaris has searched for the Crystal Egg | for years, and hasn't found it. | Still, it has to be somewhere on this island.
- entry[053] 0x0e27-0x0ea0: Sephy | I know where the Crystal Egg is. | It's in the underground temple. | We'll get that egg for you!
- entry[054] 0x0ea0-0x0ee4: Orphen | Hey! | I don't recall volunteering for that!
- entry[055] 0x0ee4-0x0f58: Sephy | I'll go alone if I have to. | This girl is running out of life | while we sit here talking!
- entry[056] 0x0f58-0x0fd6: Magnus | Come on Master, let's go. | Sephy is going to need our help. | You want a shot at Quaris, don't you?
- entry[057] 0x0fd6-0x1040: Cleo | Jado, let's go back. I'm sure | this mean-looking sorcerer will do the right thing.
- entry[058] 0x1040-0x108a: Jado | Thank you for helping an old woman, | Master Orphen.
- entry[059] 0x108a-0x10b8: Orphen | Huh!? | But I'm not...
- entry[060] 0x10b8-0x1147: Cleo | Don't worry, Jado. | He may act like a jerk, | but he's actually pretty good as sorcerers go. | Come on, Rani, let's go.
- entry[061] 0x1147-0x118b: Orphen | Wait! | I never said I would do it! | Cleo!
- entry[062] 0x118b-0x11af: Orphen | Huh? | What?
- entry[063] 0x11af-0x11f3: Orphen | Okay, okay! | I'll help you find it. Sheesh!
- entry[064] 0x11f3-0x1215: Magnus | All right!
- entry[065] 0x1215-0x1286: Sephy | Thank you, Orphen. | I knew you would do it. | Deep down, you're really a nice person.
- entry[066] 0x1286-0x12fc: Orphen | Yeah, you're right, | I'm well known all for my love and compassion. | Okay, let's go get that egg!

## scr/scr86.out

Header: pointer_table=0x0144-0x0160, footer=0x314c, records=6, text_records=5

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x0096: Orphen | This could get tricky... | I'll go | on ahead. | You two, | catch up later.
- entry[001] 0x0096-0x00bc: Magnus | Master!!
- entry[002] 0x00bc-0x00e5: Zeus | Well met, | Master Orphen.
- entry[003] 0x00e5-0x0116: Zeus | Good luck, | Master Orphen.
- entry[004] 0x0116-0x0143: Magnus | Be careful, | Master!

## scr/scr87.out

Header: pointer_table=0x01ac-0x01d4, footer=0x2994, records=9, text_records=8

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x004f: Orphen | Whoa!
- entry[001] 0x004f-0x0071: Magnus | Be careful!
- entry[002] 0x0071-0x00cf: Zeus | There must be switch somewhere to control the iron balls. | You two, wait here.
- entry[003] 0x00cf-0x0103: Orphen | Hey, not bad for an old man!
- entry[004] 0x0103-0x012b: Orphen | I owe you, Zeus!
- entry[005] 0x012b-0x015d: Zeus | I have to stop those iron balls.
- entry[006] 0x015d-0x0187: Magnus | Good luck, | Zeus!
- entry[007] 0x0187-0x01aa: Orphen | Be careful!

## scr/scr88.out

Header: pointer_table=0x045c-0x04b8, footer=0x299c, records=21, text_records=21

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x0056: Magnus | Good luck, | Zeus!
- entry[001] 0x0056-0x0072: Orphen | Be careful!
- entry[002] 0x0072-0x009a: Orphen | Not again!
- entry[003] 0x009a-0x00c2: Zeus | You two, wait here.
- entry[004] 0x00c2-0x00f2: I have to stop those iron balls.
- entry[005] 0x00f2-0x013b: There must be switch somewhere to control the iron balls.
- entry[006] 0x013b-0x0164: Magnus | Good luck, | Zeus!
- entry[007] 0x0164-0x0180: Orphen | Be careful!
- entry[008] 0x0180-0x01bc: Orphen | Hey, | not bad for an old man!
- entry[009] 0x01bc-0x01ff: Orphen | There | sure are | a lot of traps down here.
- entry[010] 0x01ff-0x023d: Magnus | Hey, | Master, | who's that over there?
- entry[011] 0x023d-0x025f: Orphen | Huh? | Hey!
- entry[012] 0x025f-0x0295: Zeus | Ungh. | It's Quaris! | Wait here.
- entry[013] 0x0295-0x02d3: Quaris | You!! | What are you | doing down here?
- entry[014] 0x02d3-0x0303: Zeus | Witch! | We've got you now!
- entry[015] 0x0303-0x0360: Quaris | Tell your master Jado that I'll never | give her the Crystal Egg back!
- entry[016] 0x0360-0x038f: Orphen | What? | She's not our...
- entry[017] 0x038f-0x03c6: Quaris | Oh stop it! | You can't fool me!
- entry[018] 0x03c6-0x03fb: Zeus | Stand and fight, coward! | Stop!
- entry[019] 0x03fb-0x0437: Orphen | No! | Don't chase her! | It's a trap!
- entry[020] 0x0437-0x045c: Ahhhh! Ohhh! Damn!
