- entry[000] 0x002c-0x005d: Orphen | \u201a\xc7\u201a\xa4\u201a\xb5\u201a\xbd\u201a\xe7\u201c\xfc\u201a\xea\u201a\xe9\u201a\xf1\u201a\xbe\ufffdH
- entry[001] 0x005d-0x007e: Sephy | \u017d\u0178\u201a\xcd\u201a\xb1\u201a\xb1\ufffdE\ufffdE\ufffdE
- entry[002] 0x007e-0x0096: Orphen | \u201a \ufffdA\u201a\xa8\u201a\xa2\ufffdI
- entry[003] 0x0096-0x00ce: Orphen | Ah. | We finally made it out.
- entry[004] 0x00ce-0x0121: Magnus | Where... | Master, look! | That building! It's made of gold!
- entry[005] 0x0121-0x0157: Orphen | So that's the underground temple?
- entry[006] 0x0157-0x0197: Sephy | Yes. | The Crystal Egg should be inside it.
- entry[007] 0x0197-0x01d7: Magnus | Okay, | Let's find the Egg and hurry back.
- entry[008] 0x01d7-0x01f2: Orphen | . . .
- entry[009] 0x01f2-0x0225: Magnus | Master? | Is something wrong?
- entry[010] 0x0225-0x0258: Orphen | Huh? | No, it's nothing. | Let's go.
- entry[011] 0x0258-0x02b1: Magnus | Hmm... | This is the only statue we've seen that has a head.
- entry[012] 0x02b1-0x02d9: Magnus | Look! A staircase!
- entry[013] 0x02d9-0x02fe: Sephy | \u0192}\u0192W\u0192N\u2014l\ufffdA\u201a\xb7\u201a\xb2\u201a\xa2\ufffdI
- entry[014] 0x02fe-0x031c: Magnus | \u201a\xd6\u201a\xd6\u201a\xd6\u201a\xd6\u201a\xd6
- entry[015] 0x031c-0x0348: Orphen | \u201a\xa8\u201a\xe7\ufffdA\u201a\xc9\u201a\xe2\u201a\xc2\u201a\xa2\u201a\xc4\u201a\xc8\u201a\xa2\u201a\xc5\ufffds\u201a\xad\u201a\xbc\ufffdI
- entry[016] 0x0348-0x037b: Magnus | \u201a\xb1\u201a\xb1\u201a\xc9\u201a\xe0\ufffd\xce\u2018\u0153\u201a\xaa\u201a \u201a\xe8\u201a\xdc\u201a\xb7\u201a\xe6
- entry[017] 0x037b-0x03a0: Sephy | \u201a\xdc\u201a\xbd\u0160K\u2019i\u201a\xaa\ufffdo\u201a\xc4\u201a\xab\u201a\xdc\u201a\xb5\u201a\xbd\u201a\xed
- entry[018] 0x03a0-0x03dd: Magnus | \u201a\xa8\u017dt\u2014l\ufffdA\u201a\xb1\u201a\xb1\u201a\xcd\u201a\xb3\u201a\xc1\u201a\xab\u201a\xe0\u2019\xca\u201a\xe8\u201a\xdc\u201a\xb5\u201a\xbd\u201a\xe6
- entry[019] 0x03dd-0x0421: Orphen | \u201a\xc7\u201a\xa4\u201a\xc8\u201a\xc1\u201a\xc4\u201a\xe9\u201a\xf1\u201a\xbe\ufffdH\ufffd@\u201a\xc7\u201a\xb1\u201a\xc9\u201a\xe0\u201c\xfc\u201a\xe8\u0152\xfb\u201a\xaa\u0152\xa9\u201a \u201a\xbd\u201a\xe7\u201a\xcb\u201a\xa6
- entry[020] 0x0421-0x0450: Magnus | What are you doing, Sephy?
- entry[021] 0x0450-0x04b1: Sephy | I think we're supposed to push this wall inwards. | Wait here, I'll be right back.
- entry[022] 0x04b1-0x04ed: Sephy | We have to go around to the other side.
- entry[023] 0x04ed-0x0512: Orphen | \u201a\xb3\u201a \ufffdA\u2039}\u201a\xb2\u201a\xa4\u201a\xba\ufffdI
- entry[024] 0x0512-0x0536: Magnus | Sephy, wait up!
- entry[025] 0x0536-0x0564: Orphen | You run like | a gazelle.
- entry[026] 0x0564-0x0591: Sephy | \u0192t\u0192t\ufffdA\u201a\xb5\u201a\xc1\u201a\xa9\u201a\xe8\u201a\xc2\u201a\xa2\u201a\xc4\u201a\xab\u201a\xc4\u2030\xba\u201a\xb3\u201a\xa2\u201a\xcb
- entry[027] 0x0591-0x05be: Magnus | \u201a\xcd\u201a \ufffdA\u201a\xcd\u201a \ufffdA\ufffdE\ufffdE\ufffdE
- entry[028] 0x05be-0x05e2: Orphen | \u201c\xfc\u201a\xe8\u0152\xfb\u201a\xcd\u201a\xc7\u201a\xb1\u201a\xbe\ufffdH
- entry[029] 0x05e2-0x0601: Sephy | \u201a\xe0\u201a\xa4\ufffdA\u2039\xdf\u201a\xa2\u201a\xcd\u201a\xb8\u201a\xc5\u201a\xb7
- entry[030] 0x0601-0x063f: Orphen | This must be the treasure chamber.
- entry[031] 0x063f-0x0668: Magnus | We finally made it.
- entry[032] 0x0668-0x06a6: Sephy | The Crystal Egg is within these walls...
- entry[033] 0x06a6-0x06d7: Orphen | Yeah, but how do we get in?
- entry[034] 0x06d7-0x06f0: Orphen | Huh?
- entry[035] 0x06f0-0x0722: Magnus | Sephy, | what are you doing?
- entry[036] 0x0722-0x07a5: Sephy | I entrust my soul to the Gods. | \xb4 | That which is eternal, is transient. | \u2020 | That which is transient, is eternal.
- entry[037] 0x07a5-0x07c8: Orphen | What was that?
- entry[038] 0x07c8-0x07f3: Magnus | Cool! What did you do?
- entry[039] 0x07f3-0x080b: Magnus | Ah! Sephy!
- entry[040] 0x080b-0x082e: Orphen | What happened?
- entry[041] 0x082e-0x085d: Magnus | Is everything okay, Sephy?
- entry[042] 0x085d-0x0872: Sephy | Orphen...

## scr/scr183.out

Header: pointer_table=0x0258-0x09fc, footer=0x2778, records=7, text_records=7

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x009a: Orphen | Okay, co-operative attacks. We're both attacking the same enemy. | Watch closely.
- entry[001] 0x009a-0x00b8: Magnus | Yes, sir!
- entry[002] 0x00b8-0x0153: Orphen | Sometimes you'll see a monster in this condition after a teammate's attack. | If you see this, let fly with a projectile magic attack.
- entry[003] 0x0153-0x016f: Magnus | Got it.
- entry[004] 0x016f-0x01a2: Orphen | Okay, this time it's for real.
- entry[005] 0x01a2-0x022a: Orphen | Nice job. | This technique can be very effective, | so if the opportunity comes up, take advantage of it.
- entry[006] 0x022a-0x0258: Magnus | I understand. Thank you, Master.

## scr/scr188.out

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x0068: Magnus | All right! | Let's do it!
- entry[001] 0x0068-0x009e: Orphen | Hey, don't get cocky. Be careful!
- entry[002] 0x009e-0x00bb: Sephy | Tee hee.
- entry[003] 0x00bb-0x00d7: Orphen | Uh-oh!

## scr/scr189.out

Header: pointer_table=0x0b58-0x0c04, footer=0x4354, records=42, text_records=41

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x0077: Cleo | Why does everything | have to be so confusing?
- entry[001] 0x0077-0x009d: Zeus | Ah!? | That's...
- entry[002] 0x009d-0x00c0: Orphen | What is it?
- entry[003] 0x00c0-0x00ef: Cleo | Hey! | What is it, Zeus?
- entry[004] 0x00ef-0x0129: Cleo | [pants] | What? | What did you see?
- entry[005] 0x0129-0x0177: Zeus | I thought... | I thought I saw my daughter. | I'm sorry.
- entry[006] 0x0177-0x01d6: Orphen | Are you feeling okay, old man? | Maybe it was an illusion of some kind.
- entry[007] 0x01d6-0x0201: Zeus | An illusion? | Maybe.
- entry[008] 0x0201-0x0234: Orphen | What is this place, anyway?
- entry[009] 0x0234-0x0271: Cleo | Is that a grave? | Strange place for a tomb.
- entry[010] 0x0271-0x0292: Zeus | Azel!
- entry[011] 0x0292-0x02b5: Orphen | Zeus! Wait!
- entry[012] 0x02b5-0x02dc: Orphen | Where'd she go?
- entry[013] 0x02dc-0x0302: Cleo | Was that a ghost?
- entry[014] 0x0302-0x0334: Zeus | Azel! | No... | Not that...
- entry[015] 0x0334-0x0372: Cleo | So... | This tomb, it's your daughter's?
- entry[016] 0x0372-0x039d: Zeus | I don't know. | But...
- entry[017] 0x039d-0x03ea: Orphen | There's something in the tomb. | Do you recognize it?
- entry[018] 0x03ea-0x0421: Zeus | Hmm... | I can't tell from here.
- entry[019] 0x0421-0x044c: Cleo | What do we do, Orphen?
- entry[020] 0x044c-0x0495: Orphen | We need to check that out. | It's the only clue we have.
- entry[021] 0x0495-0x04bd: Zeus | What's this?
- entry[022] 0x04bd-0x04fe: Orphen | A pendant | and a | fragment of some kind.
- entry[023] 0x04fe-0x0563: Cleo | Is this a letter? | It's addressed | 'To Father'. | This must be | for Zeus...
- entry[024] 0x0563-0x0580: Zeus | For me?
- entry[025] 0x0580-0x05b0: Zeus | This... | It's from Azel...
- entry[026] 0x05b0-0x0625: Orphen | That doesn't make sense. | She would have had to know | in advance that you were coming here.
- entry[027] 0x0625-0x066c: Zeus | She wrote: | 'I pray that you will come someday.'
- entry[028] 0x066c-0x0697: Orphen | So what's this then?
- entry[029] 0x0697-0x06f8: Zeus | Ah! That fragment, Master Orphen, | appears to be part of | the Crystal Egg.
- entry[030] 0x06f8-0x075e: Orphen | What? | That egg that was in the book? | The one that lets you travel in time?
- entry[031] 0x075e-0x0827: Zeus | It seems that by rubbing the inside of | the shell, you can cross space and time. | My daughter must have sensed something | would happen, and left these items here for me to find.
- entry[032] 0x0827-0x089f: Cleo | Then that figure back there | must have been your daughter's spirit. | She wanted to lead us here.
- entry[033] 0x089f-0x08bb: Zeus | Azel...
- entry[034] 0x08bb-0x0948: Orphen | None of that matters. | The important thing is that now | we have a way to go back to our time. | I'm ready, let's go!
- entry[035] 0x0948-0x09c3: Cleo | Orphen! It does too matter! | How can you be so insensitive? | No wonder people think you're a creep!
- entry[036] 0x09c3-0x0a23: Orphen | Like I care what people think! | All I'm saying is that we gotta go back.
- entry[037] 0x0a23-0x0ac9: Zeus | Master Orphen is right. | The first thing we should do is | use the egg fragment my daughter left here, | and return to our own time. | Are you ready?
- entry[038] 0x0ac9-0x0aff: Cleo | This place gives me the creeps...
- entry[039] 0x0aff-0x0b2d: Cleo | Look at all these crypts.
- entry[040] 0x0b2d-0x0b57: Cleo | This place is creepy.

## scr/scr190.out

Header: pointer_table=0x0bbc-0x0c5c, footer=0x4464, records=38, text_records=36

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x0046: Orphen | .........
- entry[001] 0x0046-0x0083: Cleo | Are you sure | you don't miss her, Orphen?
- entry[002] 0x0083-0x00ab: Orphen | Yeah, | you wish...
- entry[003] 0x00ab-0x0125: Sephy | I've decided | to remain here. | His memory will live on here. | There is nothing for me at home now.
- entry[004] 0x0125-0x01ac: Magnus | Why would someone that beautiful | want to spend | her whole life on an island like that? | I just don't get it.
- entry[005] 0x01ac-0x0241: Cleo | That | is because | you're just a little boy. | She really loved him. | If staying there makes her happy, that's all that matters.
- entry[006] 0x0241-0x0266: Magnus | If you say so...
- entry[007] 0x0266-0x02b0: Cleo | Well I do say so! | You're just | too young understand.
- entry[008] 0x02b0-0x02ef: Magnus | Hey! I'm only three years younger than you!
- entry[009] 0x02ef-0x0349: Cleo | Everybody knows girls mature | faster than boys. | You're the living proof!
- entry[011] 0x0352-0x03c4: Orphen | Well, I'm glad that's over. | What a mess that was! | How do I get involved in these things?
- entry[012] 0x03c4-0x0424: Cleo | You know, | helping other people out every | once in a while won't kill you.
- entry[013] 0x0424-0x0492: Orphen | Hey, I'm not the selfish one around here! | You're the | one that | only cares about...
- entry[014] 0x0492-0x04fe: Volcan | Orphen! | Where do you | get off trying | to sail without my regal self | like that!?
- entry[015] 0x04fe-0x0525: Magnus | How did | they...
- entry[016] 0x0525-0x0583: Orphen | I thought we left you on that dumb island. | How did you get on the ship?
- entry[017] 0x0583-0x05bc: Volcan | You can't get rid of me that easily!
- entry[018] 0x05bc-0x060c: Dortin | Actually, we swam from shore. | My arms | are killing me...
- entry[019] 0x060c-0x0634: Magnus | What!? | You swam!?
- entry[020] 0x0634-0x067a: Cleo | If only you were as | smart as you are stubborn...
- entry[021] 0x067a-0x06dc: Orphen | Damn. | I should have asked Zeus to | chop you up into little halfling bits.
- entry[022] 0x06dc-0x0762: Zeus | Master Orphen, | thanks to you, | I was able to | find my daughter. | I thank you from | the bottom of my heart.
- entry[023] 0x0762-0x0797: Orphen | No, no, | don't get mushy on me.
- entry[024] 0x0797-0x07d3: Zeus | There is something I | want you to have.
- entry[025] 0x07d3-0x07ec: Orphen | Eh?
- entry[026] 0x07ec-0x088c: Zeus | This is the Golden Arrow. | It has been in my family for generations. | It will bring you and your companions good luck. | Please accept it.
- entry[027] 0x088c-0x08e6: Orphen | Okay, | come on now. | I can't take something | like that from you.
- entry[028] 0x08e6-0x094c: Zeus | Please, | accept it as an momento | of my eternal gratitude | for all your help.
- entry[029] 0x094c-0x0994: Orphen | Hmm. | All right then, | I'll take it. | Thanks, Zeus.
- entry[031] 0x099d-0x09f7: Cleo | Do you think | Mar was happy, at the end? | Meeting his mother and all?
- entry[032] 0x09f7-0x0a4d: Orphen | I suppose | he's the only one that | could answer that question.
- entry[033] 0x0a4d-0x0a87: Cleo | Well I think he was happy. | Don't you?
- entry[034] 0x0a87-0x0abd: Magnus | Oh, my. | Cleo, | are you crying?
- entry[035] 0x0abd-0x0b32: Cleo | Why would I be crying!? | If it weren't for Mar, | you'd be | at the bottom | of the ocean, twerp!
- entry[036] 0x0b32-0x0b57: Magnus | What | did I do?
- entry[037] 0x0b57-0x0bbc: Orphen | Dumb kid. | Just had to go and get himself killed | saving the rest of us. Damn it!

## scr/scr194.out

Header: pointer_table=0x0748-0x07d4, footer=0x4cdc, records=34, text_records=33

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x004f: \u201ad\u201au\u201aP\u201aP\u201aQ\ufffdi\u201al\u201ad\u201ar\u201ar\u201a\xc8\u201a\xb5\ufffdj
- entry[001] 0x004f-0x007e: Magnus | W-w-what happened?
- entry[002] 0x007e-0x00ae: Orphen | How the hell should I know!
- entry[003] 0x00ae-0x013a: Sephy | You must have stepped on a fragment of the Crystal Egg. | There appear to be pieces of it all over this island.
- entry[004] 0x013a-0x01c2: Orphen | Oh great! So, what, we jumped to a different time period again? | Can't we reverse the jump or something?
- entry[005] 0x01c2-0x01fd: Sephy | There's nothing we can do here.
- entry[006] 0x01fd-0x0222: Magnus | Brrr. I'm cold!
- entry[007] 0x0222-0x028b: Orphen | In any case, if we sit around here, we'll freeze to death. | We better keep going.
- entry[008] 0x028b-0x02a7: Sephy | Agreed.
- entry[009] 0x02a7-0x02c8: Magnus | Ahh!
- entry[010] 0x02c8-0x02ee: Orphen | What a goofball.
- entry[011] 0x02ee-0x0314: Magnus | Master!!
- entry[012] 0x0314-0x033c: Sephy | I'm cold... So cold.
- entry[013] 0x033c-0x0379: Magnus | Sephy, are you okay? | I'll help you...
- entry[014] 0x0379-0x03c5: Magnus | Mean old Master... | Never wants to help anyone else...
- entry[015] 0x03c5-0x03f1: Magnus | You feel very happy...
- entry[016] 0x03f1-0x0417: Magnus | You very warm...
- entry[017] 0x0417-0x0445: Orphen | Huh? No! Don't do that!
- entry[018] 0x0445-0x045f: Sephy | Oh...
- entry[019] 0x045f-0x047a: Orphen | No...
- entry[020] 0x047a-0x0496: Magnus | Ahhhh!
- entry[021] 0x0496-0x04af: Sephy | Ughh!
- entry[022] 0x04af-0x04ed: Orphen | Oh, you just had to do it, didn't you?
- entry[023] 0x04ed-0x0523: Magnus | But, Master, I just wanted to...
- entry[024] 0x0523-0x057f: Orphen | No buts! | Disobey me again, and you'll be looking for another teacher!
- entry[025] 0x057f-0x05c0: Sephy | Ohh! | Orphen! There's something in the ice!
- entry[026] 0x05c0-0x05e1: Orphen | What the...
- entry[027] 0x05e1-0x0621: Magnus | What's going on? Shouldn't we keep moving?
- entry[028] 0x0621-0x0645: Magnus | Master!!
- entry[029] 0x0645-0x0679: Orphen | Ah, you finally caught up, eh?
- entry[030] 0x0679-0x06de: Orphen | Okay, let's split up. I'll go on ahead. You two, catch up with me later.
- entry[031] 0x06de-0x0717: Magnus | Don't worry about me, Master.
- entry[032] 0x0717-0x0746: Sephy | Orphen, be careful.

## scr/scr197.out

Header: pointer_table=0x03ac-0x03f0, footer=0x470c, records=15, text_records=15

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x0079: Magnus | Only one more floor to go. I hope it's here.
- entry[001] 0x0079-0x00a8: Magnus | Aha! | This must be it.
- entry[002] 0x00a8-0x00eb: Magnus | But which one? | Okay... | I'll turn 'em all off!
- entry[003] 0x00eb-0x0118: Magnus | I wonder...
- entry[004] 0x0118-0x012f: Magnus | Ahhh!!
- entry[005] 0x012f-0x0168: Sephy | \u0192L\u0192\u0192\u0192b\ufffdI\u2030\xbd\u201a\xc5\u201a\xb7\ufffdH\u2039C\u017d\ufffd\u201a\xbf\u02c6\xab\u201a\xa2\ufffdI
- entry[006] 0x0168-0x01c0: Orphen | \u201a\xad\u201a\xbb\u201a\xc1\ufffdA\u017d\u0178\u201a\xa9\u201a\xe7\u017d\u0178\u201a\xd6\ufffdo\u201a\xc4\u201a\xab\u201a\xe2\u201a\xaa\u201a\xc1\u201a\xc4\ufffdB | \u0192C\u0192\u2030\u0192C\u0192\u2030\u201a\xb7\u201a\xe9\u201a\xba\ufffdB\u2030\xb4\u201a\xaa\u0192J\u0192^\u201a\xc2\u201a\xaf\u201a\xc4\u201a\xe2\u201a\xe9\ufffdI
- entry[007] 0x01c0-0x0205: Sephy | Magnus!? | Where | could he have gone?
- entry[008] 0x0205-0x0262: Orphen | It looks like he came this far... | Maybe he was eaten by a monster.
- entry[009] 0x0262-0x028c: Sephy | No! | Don't say it!
- entry[010] 0x028c-0x0323: Orphen | Just a joke. | He may not be tough, but he is my apprentice. | He wouldn't let some critter kill him. | At least, I hope not.
- entry[011] 0x0323-0x034b: Sephy | I hope he's okay.
- entry[012] 0x034b-0x0377: Orphen | Sephy, over here. Come on.
- entry[013] 0x0377-0x0393: Sephy | Orphen.
- entry[014] 0x0393-0x03ac: Orphen | Huh?

## scr/scr199.out

Header: pointer_table=0x0330-0x038c, footer=0x1fa4, records=22, text_records=17

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x0054: Orphen
- entry[001] 0x0054-0x007b: Sephy
- entry[002] 0x007b-0x00a1: Zeus
- entry[003] 0x00a1-0x00c6: Mar
- entry[005] 0x00cf-0x010a: Orphen | Hmm... What are we supposed to do here?
- entry[006] 0x010a-0x013c: Sephy | Orphen, let me handle this one.
- entry[007] 0x013c-0x0160: Orphen | It's all yours, Sephy.
- entry[009] 0x0169-0x0186: Sephy | We're in!
- entry[010] 0x0186-0x01a5: Magnus | It opened!
- entry[011] 0x01a5-0x01d8: Orphen | Hey, not bad for a circus performer!
- entry[013] 0x01e1-0x0219: Orphen | Okay, Zeus, it's your turn to shine.
- entry[014] 0x0219-0x0245: Orphen | Think you can handle it?
- entry[015] 0x0245-0x0260: Zeus | Leave it to me!
- entry[017] 0x0269-0x02a3: Orphen | Mar, think you can find a way through?
- entry[018] 0x02a3-0x02db: Mar | Maybe. Wait here, I'll go check it out.
- entry[019] 0x02db-0x0309: Orphen | I'm counting on you, Mar.
- entry[020] 0x0309-0x032d: Orphen | We're counting on you!

## scr/scr200.out

Header: pointer_table=0x0210-0x024c, footer=0x1334, records=14, text_records=10

- entry[001] 0x0036-0x0070: Orphen | Hmm... How are we supposed to get out?
- entry[002] 0x0070-0x00a2: Sephy | Orphen, let me handle this one.
- entry[003] 0x00a2-0x00c1: Orphen | Go for it, Sephy.
- entry[005] 0x00ca-0x0102: Orphen | \u201a\xb1\u201a\xb1\u201a\xcd\u201a \u201a\xf1\u201a\xbd\u201a\xcc\u0152\xa9\u201a\xb9\ufffd\xea\u201a\xbe\ufffdI\u201a\xe6\u201a\xeb\u201a\xb5\u201a\xad\u201a\xc8\ufffdI
- entry[006] 0x0102-0x0128: Orphen | \u0192[\u0192C\u0192A\u0192X\ufffdA\u2014\u0160\u201a\xde\u201a\xba\ufffdI
- entry[007] 0x0128-0x0140: \u0192[\u0192C\u0192A\u0192X | \ufffd\xb3\u201a\xc1\u201a\xbd\ufffdI
- entry[009] 0x0149-0x0183: Orphen | Mar, think you can find a way through?
- entry[010] 0x0183-0x01b8: Mar | Don't worry. I can do it. Wait here.
- entry[011] 0x01b8-0x01eb: Orphen | Okay, show us what you can do.
- entry[012] 0x01eb-0x020d: Orphen | I'm counting on you!

## scr/scr204.out

Header: pointer_table=0x094c-0x09cc, footer=0x3064, records=31, text_records=30

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x005e: Cleo | What is | this place?
- entry[001] 0x005e-0x009f: Orphen | Looks like a reading room | of some kind.
- entry[002] 0x009f-0x00bf: Cleo | What kind?
- entry[003] 0x00bf-0x012e: Orphen | How the hell should I know? | One thing's for sure... | This is no | ordinary island.
- entry[004] 0x012e-0x015b: Zeus | What does the book say?
- entry[005] 0x015b-0x01a7: Orphen | Zeus, | have you ever | heard of the 'Crystal Egg'?
- entry[006] 0x01a7-0x01dd: Zeus | The Crystal Egg? | No, | never.
- entry[007] 0x01dd-0x0229: Cleo | Orphen, | what is it? | That crystalline egg thingie?
- entry[008] 0x0229-0x02b6: Orphen | Apparently, there is a Crystal Egg | somewhere on this island. | If you have it, | you can control the flow of time.
- entry[009] 0x02b6-0x02d9: Cleo | Eh? | Really?
- entry[010] 0x02d9-0x030d: Orphen | That's what it | says. . .
- entry[011] 0x030d-0x0354: Zeus | Hmm. | Why | would anyone create such an object?
- entry[012] 0x0354-0x03ae: Orphen | Who knows? | This book is ancient. | That's all I could make out.
- entry[013] 0x03ae-0x0434: Cleo | Well, | that's interesting, but how do we get out of here? | You know, we ARE surrounded | by bookshelves. . .
- entry[014] 0x0434-0x047e: Orphen | Well you're sharp today aren't you. (sarcastically)
- entry[015] 0x047e-0x04d9: Zeus | Hmm. | I think we can move these | bookcases if we push hard enough.
- entry[016] 0x04d9-0x0510: Cleo | He's right! | Okay! | I'll help!
- entry[017] 0x0510-0x0541: Orphen | Hey, hey! | Don't go overboard!
- entry[018] 0x0541-0x05b9: Cleo | Look at all these books... | Politics, economics, physics... | But not one romance novel!
- entry[019] 0x05b9-0x0646: Orphen | 'In order to learn about the human existence, | Gaia observed'... | What is this crap? | And who the hell is Gaia?
- entry[020] 0x0646-0x06b5: Cleo | Orphen. | Just because you've never heard of something | doesn't mean it doesn't exist!
- entry[021] 0x06b5-0x06e1: Orphen | Ah, shut up! | Man...
- entry[022] 0x06e1-0x0733: Cleo | Hey, | isn't there anything | in there about this building?
- entry[023] 0x0733-0x07b4: Zeus | This place | is called the Tower of Wisdom. | There seems | to be a passage from here leading underground.
- entry[024] 0x07b4-0x07db: Cleo | Where does it go?
- entry[025] 0x07db-0x084e: Zeus | Hmm. | I can't tell, | but it looks like there is a maze | running underneath the island.
- entry[026] 0x084e-0x08b2: Orphen | I don't know why, | but there's definitely something strange going on here.
- entry[027] 0x08b2-0x08ed: Cleo | Let's go | check out the underground!
- entry[028] 0x08ed-0x0928: Zeus | Wait here. I will make sure it's safe.
- entry[029] 0x0928-0x094b: Cleo | Be careful, | Zeus!

## scr/scr205.out

Header: pointer_table=0x1ac0-0x1c50, footer=0xe8bc, records=99, text_records=97

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x0075: Zeus | What? | Old Woman, | you know | my daughter?
- entry[001] 0x0075-0x00ba: Jado | Yes. Azel and her mother | spoke of you often.
- entry[002] 0x00ba-0x00eb: Zeus | Where is my | daughter now?
- entry[003] 0x00eb-0x0144: Jado | She | was kidnapped by that woman | I was struggling with just now.
- entry[004] 0x0144-0x0185: Orphen | Kidnapped? | Why would she be kidnapped?
- entry[005] 0x0185-0x0207: Jado | My name is Jado. | Quaris, | that's the witch's name, | took Azel away. | In the name of science, she said.
- entry[006] 0x0207-0x0277: Zeus | That witch! | I'll follow her to the ends of the earth | if I have to. I'll get Azel back.
- entry[007] 0x0277-0x02ed: Jado | Quaris | has sealed off the underground area. | It's impossible for anyone but | her to get in.
- entry[008] 0x02ed-0x0348: Cleo | Can't we get to the underground | passages from | the Tower of Wisdom?
- entry[009] 0x0348-0x03d0: Orphen | In the future, | maybe. | But now | we're back in the present. | I doubt we'll | be able to get in | from there.
- entry[010] 0x03d0-0x0432: Cleo | Oh, yeah. We were in the future. | Why does this have to be so complicated!
- entry[011] 0x0432-0x0464: Orphen | We need | to find another way in.
- entry[012] 0x0464-0x04a2: Magnus | Hello! | You were on the ship!
- entry[013] 0x04a2-0x0508: Zeus | Ah, | I remember you as well my little friend. | I'm heartened to see you safe.
- entry[014] 0x0508-0x0558: Magnus | Well, | I'm glad we're both okay. | I'm | called Magnus.
- entry[015] 0x0558-0x0574: Orphen | Hey!
- entry[016] 0x0574-0x05a9: Magnus | Ouch! | Oh! | Master. Hello.
- entry[017] 0x05a9-0x0678: Orphen | 'Hello'? That's it!? | You haven't seen your beloved teacher | since your ship sank, | and all you have to say is, | 'Hello'? | You could | at least pretend to be have been worried.
- entry[018] 0x0678-0x071f: Magnus | Hahaha... | You're too tough to be killed, | Master. | That's why I wasn't worried about you. | Well, | anyway, | I'm glad | everyone is okay.
- entry[019] 0x071f-0x075a: Orphen | He didn't | miss me at all, did he?
- entry[020] 0x075a-0x0774: Magnus | Hahaha...
- entry[021] 0x0774-0x0823: Sephy | Orphen, I must begin my journey soon. | Won't you please come with me? | I can't imagine anything as exciting | as traveling with someone like you.
- entry[022] 0x0823-0x085c: Sephy | Won't you please come with me?
- entry[023] 0x085c-0x0887: Cleo | Hey! What about Zeus?
- entry[024] 0x0887-0x08bc: Cleo | Aren't you forgetting somebody?
- entry[025] 0x08bc-0x08eb: Sephy | Oh, thank you so much.
- entry[026] 0x08eb-0x0969: Sephy | I understand. | I hope you'll forgive | me for being so forward. | I hope I didn't upset your friend.
- entry[027] 0x0969-0x098f: Sephy | I understand.
- entry[028] 0x098f-0x09cc: Mar | Hi. | Are you going to come with me?
- entry[029] 0x09cc-0x09ea: Mar | Hello.
- entry[030] 0x09ea-0x0a29: Mar | Thanks! | I was lonely all by myself.
- entry[031] 0x0a29-0x0a54: Mar | Okay, never mind.
- entry[032] 0x0a54-0x0a7e: Zeus | Ah, Master Orphen.
- entry[033] 0x0a7e-0x0ad5: Orphen | I can't stand all this waiting around. | Let's move out.
- entry[034] 0x0ad5-0x0b30: Zeus | Yes. | But | where do we go? | If we only | had a clue of some kind...
- entry[035] 0x0b30-0x0b4f: Magnus | Master!
- entry[036] 0x0b4f-0x0b84: Orphen | Huh? | What happened to Cleo?
- entry[037] 0x0b84-0x0c38: Magnus | She's totally | bummed out about something. | She told me what you're trying to do. | Cleo | wasn't really up to it. | I thought I'd better take her place.
- entry[038] 0x0c38-0x0c72: Orphen | Whatever. It's all the same to me.
- entry[039] 0x0c72-0x0ccd: Magnus | Oh yeah! | I almost forgot. | I found someone who knows the island.
- entry[040] 0x0ccd-0x0ce9: Orphen | Who?
- entry[041] 0x0ce9-0x0d06: Magnus | Come on over
- entry[042] 0x0d06-0x0d37: Zeus | I wish it were otherwise...
- entry[043] 0x0d37-0x0d79: Magnus | Hmm... | I wonder where Cleo went.
- entry[044] 0x0d79-0x0d96: Magnus | Ouch!
- entry[045] 0x0d96-0x0db8: Orphen | What the!?
- entry[046] 0x0db8-0x0de3: Orphen | Oh, it's just Cleo.
- entry[047] 0x0de3-0x0e15: Magnus | Cleo! | Why'd you do that!?
- entry[048] 0x0e15-0x0e89: Cleo | Why? | Because you deserve it, that's why! | Orphen! | Magnus! | Why did you leave me behind!?
- entry[049] 0x0e89-0x0f01: Orphen | Well, ah... | You looked tired out, and Magnus | said he | would go in your place. Right, Magnus?
- entry[050] 0x0f01-0x0f67: Magnus | Huh? | Oh, yeah. That's right, Cleo. | You looked like you could use a rest...
- entry[051] 0x0f67-0x0fb5: Cleo | A likely story, indeed! | So, anyway, did you find Azel?
- entry[052] 0x0fb5-0x1018: Zeus | No... We almost got our hands on Quaris, | but she got away at the last second.
- entry[053] 0x1018-0x1044: Cleo | She got away... Again?
- entry[054] 0x1044-0x1079: Magnus | We were so close this time...
- entry[055] 0x1079-0x111b: Orphen | What we need to do now is | sit down and plan our next move. | Zeus, | if you want to get Azel back, | you have to stay focused... | Hey!
- entry[056] 0x111b-0x1136: Zeus | Yeaaaarrgh!
- entry[057] 0x1136-0x1171: Zeus | Ohh | Azel... | My little Azel...
- entry[058] 0x1171-0x11e5: Orphen | You can't lose hope now, | Zeus. Your daughter needs you. | Now let's go find her.
- entry[059] 0x11e5-0x121c: Zeus | You'll come with me? | Thank you.
- entry[060] 0x121c-0x128a: Orphen | Don't worry about it. | We have to find Quaris. | She's our only connection to Azel.
- entry[061] 0x128a-0x12c9: Cleo | Orphen! | Trying to leave me behind again!?
- entry[062] 0x12c9-0x12f8: Orphen | Oh, great, it's Cleo...
- entry[063] 0x12f8-0x137f: Cleo | Disappointed? | Magnus is no good at anything. | You guys are going to need me, | I know how to get things done!
- entry[064] 0x137f-0x13e2: Orphen | Whatever. It's all the same to me. | Don't get in my way, that's all I ask!
- entry[065] 0x13e2-0x1447: Cleo | This is Cleo you are talking to. Trust me! | Zeus, everything's going to be fine.
- entry[066] 0x1447-0x1469: Zeus | I thank you.
- entry[067] 0x1469-0x149f: Orphen | Trust her? I'm not that crazy.
- entry[068] 0x149f-0x14c2: Rani | Mr. Magician!
- entry[069] 0x14c2-0x14ee: Orphen | What? Not you again.
- entry[070] 0x14ee-0x152c: Cleo | Magician? She means you, Orphen? Hahaha!
- entry[071] 0x152c-0x1576: Orphen | Shut up! | I thought I told you not to call me that!
- entry[072] 0x1576-0x15af: Rani | Oops. Sorry. | Did you find Quaris?
- entry[073] 0x15af-0x15de: Orphen | Yeah, but she got away.
- entry[074] 0x15de-0x15fa: Rani | Oh...
- entry[075] 0x15fa-0x1638: Zeus | We're going back to the Tower of Wisdom.
- entry[076] 0x1638-0x165f: Rani | I'll go with you.
- entry[077] 0x165f-0x1697: Zeus | We can find our own way this time.
- entry[078] 0x1697-0x16d5: Rani | It's okay, I have to go by there anyway.
- entry[079] 0x16d5-0x1711: Orphen | Hey, we're not going on some picnic!
- entry[080] 0x1711-0x173f: Rani | I won't get in your way.
- entry[081] 0x173f-0x1799: Zeus | I can't sit here waiting any longer. | We must go find Quaris... Now!
- entry[082] 0x1799-0x17cb: Cleo | He's right, Orphen. Come on!
- entry[083] 0x17cb-0x17f9: Orphen | All right already. Off we go.
- entry[084] 0x17f9-0x184c: Sephy | I've been waiting for you, | Orphen. | I knew you'd come.
- entry[085] 0x184c-0x1880: Sephy | I've been waiting for you.
- entry[086] 0x1880-0x18ba: Sephy | Oh, thank you! You won't regret it.
- entry[087] 0x18ba-0x18dd: Sephy | Oh, I see...
- entry[088] 0x18dd-0x1943: Mar | If you help me | I'm sure I'll find what | I'm looking for. | Help me, please?
- entry[089] 0x1943-0x196b: Mar | Help me, please?
- entry[090] 0x196b-0x19a5: Magnus | Master, | Shouldn't we help Zeus?
- entry[091] 0x19a5-0x19c6: Magnus | Master!!
- entry[092] 0x19c6-0x19fd: Mar | Really? You'll help? | Yeah!!
- entry[093] 0x19fd-0x1a31: Mar | Oh, | okay. | Never mind.
- entry[095] 0x1a33-0x1a62: Allow Sephy to join your group? | choices: Yes / No
- entry[096] 0x1a62-0x1a90: Allow Zeus to join your group? | choices: Yes / No
- entry[097] 0x1a90-0x1abd: Allow Mar to join your group? | choices: Yes / No

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x0077: Cleo | Poor Zeus. | What are we going to do, | Orphen?
- entry[001] 0x0077-0x00c4: Orphen | Until we find that woman, | there's nothing we can do.
- entry[002] 0x00c4-0x012e: Cleo | I guess you're right... | I can't believe | we just let her escape. | It's our fault.
- entry[003] 0x012e-0x016d: Orphen | That doesn't sound like the Cleo I know.
- entry[004] 0x016d-0x01a0: Cleo | Well, | it's true. We blew it.
- entry[005] 0x01a0-0x01c4: Cleo | [sighs] | Poor Zeus.
- entry[006] 0x01c4-0x01ec: Cleo | [sighs] | Poor Zeus.
- entry[007] 0x01ec-0x0242: Magnus | Did you know | there are three towers on this island?
- entry[008] 0x0242-0x0280: Orphen | Well, I've been to the Tower of Wisdom.
- entry[009] 0x0280-0x02c2: Magnus | Really? | I've gone to the Tower of Mercy.
- entry[010] 0x02c2-0x02fb: Orphen | Then | that only leaves one more.
- entry[011] 0x02fb-0x032b: Magnus | Yeah, the Tower of Sound.
- entry[012] 0x032b-0x0358: Orphen | You sure | know a lot.
- entry[013] 0x0358-0x0395: Magnus | Hehe! | I know everything about this island!
- entry[014] 0x0395-0x03d3: Magnus | Hehe! | I know all about this island!
- entry[015] 0x03d3-0x0426: Magnus | Did you know | there are three towers on this island?
- entry[016] 0x0426-0x0463: Orphen | And you've been to the Tower of Mercy.
- entry[017] 0x0463-0x049e: Magnus | Me? | I've never been there, Master.
- entry[018] 0x049e-0x04da: Orphen | \u0192Z\u0192t\u0192B\u201a\xcc\u017d\u2013\u201a\xc6\u201a\xa2\u201a\xa2 | \u201a\xb5\u201a\xe7\u201a\xce\u201a\xc1\u201a\xad\u201a\xea\u201a\xe9\u201a\xc8\u201a\xe6

- entry[029] 0x0718-0x075e: Magnus | \u0192z\u0192\u201c\u0192g\u201a\xc5\u201a\xb7\u201a\xa9 | \u0192N\u0192\u0160\ufffd[\u0192I\u0192E\u201a\xcd\u2019m\u201a\xcc\u201c\u0192\u201a\xbe\u201a\xaf\u201a\xbe\u201a\xc1\u201a\xc4\ufffdE\ufffdE\ufffdE
- entry[030] 0x075e-0x07a8: Orphen | \u201a\xc8\u201a\xc9\u0152\xbe\u201a\xc1\u201a\xc4\u201a\xf1\u201a\xbe\ufffdI | \u0192}\ufffd[\u201a\xe2\u0192Z\u0192t\u0192B\u201a\xc6\u02c6\xea\ufffd\ufffd\u201a\xc9\ufffds\u201a\xc1\u201a\xbd\u201a\xeb\u201a\xa4\u201a\xaa\ufffdI
- entry[031] 0x07a8-0x07ea: Magnus | \u201a\xc8\u201a\xf1\u201a\xc5\u201a\xb7\u201a\xa9\ufffdH | \u201a\xda\u201a\xad\u2019B\u02c6\xc8\u0160O\u201a\xc9\u2019N\u201a\xaa\u201a\xa2\u201a\xe9\u201a\xf1\u201a\xc5\u201a\xb7\u201a\xa9\ufffdI
- entry[032] 0x07ea-0x083b: Orphen | \u201a\xb1\u201a\xcc\u201c\u2021\ufffdA\u2022\xcf\u201a\xbe\u201a\xc8\ufffdE\ufffdE\ufffdE | \u2030\xbd\u201a\xa9\u2018\xe5\u201a\xab\u201a\xc8\u201d\xe9\u2013\xa7\u201a\xaa | \u2030B\u201a\xb3\u201a\xea\u201a\xc4\u201a\xa2\u201a\xe9\u201a\xa9\u201a\xe0\u201a\xb5\u201a\xea\u201a\xc8\u201a\xa2
- entry[033] 0x083b-0x088a: Magnus | \u201a \ufffdA\u201a\xa8\u017dt\u2014l | \u201a\xb1\u201a\xcc\u201c\u2021\u201a\xcc\ufffdl\u201a\xcd\u0192z\u0192\u201c\u0192g\u0160F\u201a\xd0\u201a\xcb\u201a\xad\u201a\xea\u017d\xd2\u201a\xc5\u201a\xb7\u201a\xcb
- entry[034] 0x088a-0x08ab: Orphen | \u201a\xc8\u201a\xf1\u201a\xc5\u201a\xbe\ufffdH
- entry[035] 0x08ab-0x0916: Magnus | \u017d\u0153\u201d\xdf\u201a\xcc\u201c\u0192\u201a\xc6\u201a\xa2\u201a\xa2\u201a\xc2\u201a\xc2 | \ufffd\u2030\u2013\xe2\u201c\xb9\u2039\xef\u201a\xbe\u201a\xe7\u201a\xaf\u201a\xc5\u2013\xb3\u017d\u0153\u201d\xdf\u201a\xc5\u201a\xb7\u201a\xb5 | \u2019\xbe\u2013\xd9\u201a\xcc\u201c\u0192\u201a\xcd\u2030\xb9\u0160y\u201a\xbe\u201a\xe7\u201a\xaf\u201a\xc5\u201a\xb7\u201a\xb5\ufffdE\ufffdE\ufffdE
- entry[036] 0x0916-0x0949: Orphen | \u2019m\u201a\xcc\u201c\u0192\u201a\xcd\u02c6\xe1\u201a\xa4\u201a\xba\ufffdI\ufffd@\u2013{\u201a\xbe\u201a\xe7\u201a\xaf\u201a\xbe
- entry[037] 0x0949-0x0970: Magnus | \u201a \u201a\u0178\u0160m\u201a\xa9\u201a\xc9\ufffdE\ufffdE\ufffdE\u0192E\ufffd[\u0192\u201c
- entry[038] 0x0970-0x099b: Magnus | \u201a \u201a\u0178\u0160m\u201a\xa9\u201a\xc9\ufffdE\ufffdE\ufffdE\u0192E\ufffd[\u0192\u201c
- entry[039] 0x099b-0x09e3: Orphen | Oh, hello. | You made it off the ship okay?
- entry[040] 0x09e3-0x0a65: Sephy | Yes, | thanks to Magnus. | We never had a proper introduction... | I'm called Sephy. | You're Orphen, right?
- entry[041] 0x0a65-0x0abf: Orphen | Yep. Magnus told you about me, then? | You two came here together?
- entry[042] 0x0abf-0x0af3: Sephy | Yes. You have taught him well.
- entry[043] 0x0af3-0x0b6e: Sephy | I don't think I would have made | it here without Magnus' help. | I don't know how to ever thank him.
- entry[044] 0x0b6e-0x0bc4: Orphen | Don't worry about it. He loves | playing the knight in shining armor.
- entry[045] 0x0bc4-0x0c0b: Orphen | Ah! Sephy! You made it off the ship okay?
- entry[046] 0x0c0b-0x0c4d: Sephy | Yes. | I'm happy to see you're safe, Orphen.
- entry[047] 0x0c4d-0x0c82: Orphen | Did you come here with Magnus?
- entry[048] 0x0c82-0x0cb6: Sephy | Yes. You have taught him well.
- entry[049] 0x0cb6-0x0d31: Sephy | I don't think I would have made | it here without Magnus' help. | I don't know how to ever thank him.
- entry[050] 0x0d31-0x0d87: Orphen | Don't worry about it. He loves | playing the knight in shining armor.
- entry[051] 0x0d87-0x0dc7: Sephy | Is that tall gentleman a mercenary?
- entry[052] 0x0dc7-0x0dea: Orphen | Yeah, he is.
- entry[053] 0x0dea-0x0e22: Sephy | I'd feel so much safer if you helped me.
- entry[054] 0x0e22-0x0e66: Sephy | Is that young boy traveling by himself?
- entry[055] 0x0e66-0x0e8b: Orphen | Looks like it.
- entry[056] 0x0e8b-0x0eb8: Sephy | Oh... He must be very lonely.
- entry[057] 0x0eb8-0x0f2a: Sephy | I don't think I would have made | it here without Magnus' help. | I can't thank him enough.
- entry[058] 0x0f2a-0x0f57: Mar | I hate this place.
- entry[059] 0x0f57-0x0f72: Orphen | Why?
- entry[060] 0x0f72-0x101c: Mar | It's depressing. | The orphanage I was in | was like this... | So lonely. | At least | I have my flute, though. | I don't feel sad when I'm playing it.
- entry[061] 0x101c-0x1053: Orphen | So | you're an orphan, too, huh?
- entry[062] 0x1053-0x1077: Mar | Huh? | You, too?
- entry[063] 0x1077-0x108d: Orphen | Yeah.
- entry[064] 0x108d-0x10d7: Mar | You know | that big guy you were just talking to?
- entry[065] 0x10d7-0x10f2: Orphen | Yes?
- entry[066] 0x10f2-0x1125: Mar | You think maybe he's my father?
- entry[067] 0x1125-0x1141: Orphen | What?
- entry[068] 0x1141-0x1163: Mar | Nothing. Never mind.
- entry[069] 0x1163-0x11a6: Mar | You know | that woman you were talking to?
- entry[070] 0x11a6-0x11c3: Orphen | Yes...
- entry[071] 0x11c3-0x11fa: Mar | Do youthink she could be my mother?
- entry[072] 0x11fa-0x1215: Orphen | Huh?
- entry[073] 0x1215-0x1249: Mar | I wish I had a pretty mother like her.
- entry[074] 0x1249-0x1289: Mar | That girl | you were just talking to...
- entry[075] 0x1289-0x12a4: Orphen | Yes?
- entry[076] 0x12a4-0x12dc: Mar | Do you think she could be my sister?
- entry[077] 0x12dc-0x1300: Orphen | Are you nuts?
- entry[078] 0x1300-0x1330: Mar | You're right. I was just hoping...
- entry[079] 0x1330-0x1384: Zeus | There must be some way to get to Quaris. | There has be!
- entry[080] 0x1384-0x13dd: Orphen | There's probably more than | one entrance to the underground area.
- entry[081] 0x13dd-0x1418: Zeus | What? Really? | Where, Master Orphen!?
- entry[082] 0x1418-0x14d7: Orphen | It's just a hunch. | That book in the first tower said there | were passages underground linking the entire island. | That means there has to be more than one entrance.
- entry[083] 0x14d7-0x150f: Zeus | Indeed. | There must other ways in.
- entry[084] 0x150f-0x1558: Orphen | We've still got a chance. | So don't give up hope.
- entry[085] 0x1558-0x1586: Zeus | Thank you, Master Orphen.
- entry[086] 0x1586-0x15c1: Orphen | Thank me when we find your daughter.
- entry[087] 0x15c1-0x15e5: Zeus | Ah, good point.
- entry[088] 0x15e5-0x1631: Orphen | The thing is, I was hoping there might be a reward...
- entry[089] 0x1631-0x1679: Zeus | Ah, I understand. | I will go prepare for our expedition.
- entry[090] 0x1679-0x1718: Zeus | Master Orphen, I can't just sit here | while my daughter is being tortured | by that evil witch! | I'm going after her, alone if need be!
- entry[091] 0x1718-0x1778: Sephy | What's wrong with that mercenary? | He looks so angry... He frightens me.
- entry[092] 0x1778-0x17f4: Cleo | What about Zeus!? | Have you forgotten him? | Shame on you! | You can't be relied upon at all, can you?
- entry[093] 0x17f4-0x1814: Cleo | Shame on you!
- entry[094] 0x1814-0x18d1: Magnus | We were so close to catching her... | Master, what do you | tell someone at a time like this? | I'd like to say something to Zeus... | But I don't know what to say.
- entry[095] 0x18d1-0x1909: Magnus | We were so close to catching her...
- entry[096] 0x1909-0x195a: Mar | What happened? | Everyone seems upset about something.
- entry[097] 0x195a-0x198f: Orphen | Yeah. | Don't worry about it.
- entry[098] 0x198f-0x19e1: Mar | I'm okay. | I've got my trusty flute! | It will tell me where to go.
- entry[099] 0x19e1-0x19f9: Mar | Hello.

## scr/scr207.out

Header: pointer_table=0x2370-0x25a4, footer=0x163e4, records=140, text_records=138

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x005d: Magnus | Master! Cleo!
- entry[001] 0x005d-0x0077: Cleo | Magnus!
- entry[002] 0x0077-0x00c6: Orphen | Ah, I see you're not dead. | That means you must be alive.
- entry[003] 0x00c6-0x00ea: Cleo | Was that a joke?
- entry[004] 0x00ea-0x0120: Magnus | I looked everywhere for you guys!
- entry[005] 0x0120-0x015e: Volcan | Hey! Slimy sorcerer! You're not dead yet?
- entry[006] 0x015e-0x018b: Cleo | You guys made it out okay?
- entry[007] 0x018b-0x0208: Dortin | Yeah, luckily, we were able to meet up on shore. | All by himself, | I don't know what would happen...
- entry[008] 0x0208-0x0234: Cleo | I can see what you mean.
- entry[009] 0x0234-0x0276: Orphen | Hahaha! | I've been waiting for you, Volcan.
- entry[010] 0x0276-0x02be: Volcan | Hahahaha... So you finally | realized you need me!
- entry[011] 0x02be-0x0304: Orphen | Yeah, I need you to get me into messes like this!
- entry[012] 0x0304-0x0336: Magnus | Hey... Wasn't he on the ship?
- entry[013] 0x0336-0x0355: Cleo | This is Mar.
- entry[014] 0x0355-0x036d: Mar | Hello.
- entry[015] 0x036d-0x03ba: Magnus | Ah, okay. So that's why you | want to go to that tower?
- entry[016] 0x03ba-0x03e3: Cleo | Now do you understand?
- entry[017] 0x03e3-0x044f: Magnus | Yeah... | But to get to that tower from here, you | need a boat, and we don't have one.
- entry[018] 0x044f-0x0467: Mar | But...
- entry[019] 0x0467-0x04bd: Magnus | However, it does freeze over in winter. | You could walk across.
- entry[020] 0x04bd-0x04ef: Mar | But I can't wait until winter...
- entry[021] 0x04ef-0x0513: Cleo | So what do we do?
- entry[022] 0x0513-0x056c: Cleo | Orphen, don't you have some kind | of spell to make winter come early?
- entry[023] 0x056c-0x05c2: Orphen | You can't mess with the seasons. | That would screw up everything.
- entry[024] 0x05c2-0x05e2: Volcan | Bahahaha...
- entry[025] 0x05e2-0x0630: Orphen | Damn! Don't do that! | You're supposed to be knocked out.
- entry[026] 0x0630-0x066c: Volcan | I know a way to make winter come early.
- entry[027] 0x066c-0x06a0: Dortin | Ah! | You mean the Crystal Egg!
- entry[028] 0x06a0-0x06d7: Orphen | Egg? | What are you talking about?
- entry[029] 0x06d7-0x07c0: Volcan | Hear and be amazed! | There is a fabulous Crystal Egg on Chaos Island! | This egg lets you jump to whatever time you wish! | As adventurers, it is our sworn duty | to find this glorious treasure! | Bahahaha!
- entry[030] 0x07c0-0x07fd: Dortin | Since when did you become an adventurer?
- entry[031] 0x07fd-0x0834: Orphen | So where is this amazing treasure?
- entry[032] 0x0834-0x08d1: Dortin | We didn't find the Egg, but we saw | a piece of it near the Endless Stairs. | At least I think it was part of the Egg. | It was glowing.
- entry[033] 0x08d1-0x0919: Cleo | It glows? | I saw something earlier that was glowing.
- entry[034] 0x0919-0x0949: Orphen | What are you talking about?
- entry[035] 0x0949-0x09cd: Cleo | When I climbed up the cliffs after we | first came ashore, | I accidentally touched something that was glowing.
- entry[036] 0x09cd-0x0a05: Magnus | Did you notice any changes in time?
- entry[037] 0x0a05-0x0a37: Cleo | Now that I think about it, yes.
- entry[038] 0x0a37-0x0a8b: Magnus | So if we find a piece, | maybe we can turn summer into winter.
- entry[039] 0x0a8b-0x0aa4: Mar | Really?
- entry[040] 0x0aa4-0x0ada: Cleo | Yes! Then we can get to that tower!
- entry[041] 0x0ada-0x0b16: Orphen | Forget it! You can't believe those two!
- entry[042] 0x0b16-0x0b8b: Cleo | Maybe not... But if there's even a small | chance it will work, we have to try! | Come on, Mar!
- entry[043] 0x0b8b-0x0ba1: Mar | Yea!
- entry[044] 0x0ba1-0x0bc4: Orphen | Great. Why me?
- entry[045] 0x0bc4-0x0c00: Orphen | Dortin, the Endless Stairs... That way?
- entry[046] 0x0c00-0x0c19: Dortin | Yes.
- entry[047] 0x0c19-0x0c80: Volcan | Hey! That's my loot! You can't take it! | I'll Hula Dance your sorry butt to Hell!
- entry[048] 0x0c80-0x0c9a: Volcan | Ouch!
- entry[049] 0x0c9a-0x0cd3: Orphen | Magnus, help me hold this punk down.
- entry[050] 0x0cd3-0x0cf9: Magnus | What? | Uh, okay.
- entry[051] 0x0cf9-0x0d12: Volcan | Ugh!
- entry[052] 0x0d12-0x0d3a: Magnus | Hey... Master!? | Ohh.....
- entry[053] 0x0d3a-0x0db9: Orphen | Now we have to find those infernal halflings and | persuade them to tell us how to use this thing.
- entry[054] 0x0db9-0x0e67: Sephy | Orphen, I must start my journey soon. | Won't you please come with me? | I can't imagine anything as exciting | as traveling with someone like you.
- entry[055] 0x0e67-0x0e95: Magnus | Master! | What about Mar?
- entry[056] 0x0e95-0x0ec2: Magnus | Master! | You promised!
- entry[057] 0x0ec2-0x0eec: Sephy | Oh, thank you so much.
- entry[058] 0x0eec-0x0f54: Sephy | I hope you'll forgive | me for being so forward. | I hope I didn't upset your friend.
- entry[059] 0x0f54-0x0faa: Zeus | Will you join me? | This island is proving most challenging!
- entry[060] 0x0faa-0x0fdb: Zeus | Good! We shall be unstoppable!
- entry[061] 0x0fdb-0x1011: Zeus | So be it. Good luck, Master Orphen.
- entry[062] 0x1011-0x1054: Orphen | Now if we only knew how to use the Egg.
- entry[063] 0x1054-0x1078: Cleo | Orphen, any luck?
- entry[064] 0x1078-0x109e: Orphen | Huh? | With what?
- entry[065] 0x109e-0x10cb: Cleo | How to use the egg, Silly.
- entry[066] 0x10cb-0x112b: Orphen | Yeah, well... | We need those halflings... | Ah! I got it! | Watch this...
- entry[067] 0x112b-0x1150: Volcan | Yaaaaaaaaaaaaah!
- entry[068] 0x1150-0x116f: Orphen | Aha! Punk!
- entry[069] 0x116f-0x11ac: Volcan | Ugh! | Who are you!? | What do you want?
- entry[070] 0x11ac-0x1213: Orphen | What? Are you blind | as well as dumb? | We've been standing here the whole time!
- entry[071] 0x1213-0x124a: Orphen | Hey... Don't you want to help him?
- entry[072] 0x124a-0x1266: Dortin | Umm...
- entry[073] 0x1266-0x12ce: Volcan | Hey! | Let me up, you retarded loan shark! | I'll beat you to death with a feather!
- entry[074] 0x12ce-0x12fc: Orphen | Gee, that sounds painful.
- entry[075] 0x12fc-0x131f: Dortin | Yeah, well...
- entry[076] 0x131f-0x1374: Orphen | Nevermind that. What do you know | about using the Crystal Egg?
- entry[077] 0x1374-0x1464: Volcan | Bahahaha! | Bahahaha! | Well done, Orphen! | I, Lord Volcan Volcano, the Masmaturian | Bulldog, knew you would get the Crystal Egg and | present it to my royal self. You have | proven your loyalty. | Now hand it over!
- entry[078] 0x1464-0x14c9: Orphen | You idiot! | What are you babbling about? | Why the hell would I give it to you?
- entry[079] 0x14c9-0x1558: Volcan | You great greedy pig of a sorcerer! | You leave me no choice! I will have to take it by force! | Dortin! | We're leaving!
- entry[080] 0x1558-0x1592: Orphen | Your wacko brother is talking to you.
- entry[081] 0x1592-0x15c8: Dortin | Sorry... | He's always like that.
- entry[082] 0x15c8-0x15e5: Volcan | Dortin!
- entry[083] 0x15e5-0x161f: Orphen | So Dortin, do you know how to use it?
- entry[084] 0x161f-0x1684: Dortin | Well, umm. | Actually, the only thing we know about it | is that it's valuable.
- entry[085] 0x1684-0x16bb: Orphen | Oh, well... | I should have known.
- entry[086] 0x16bb-0x1714: Volcan | Hey! | Don't try to ignore me, you peon! | I'm Lord Volcan Volcano!
- entry[087] 0x1714-0x173d: Orphen | Hey! | knock it off!
- entry[088] 0x173d-0x175c: Orphen | What the!?
- entry[089] 0x175c-0x1788: Volcan | W-w-what's | happening?
- entry[090] 0x1788-0x17a2: Cleo | Orphen!
- entry[091] 0x17a2-0x17bc: Mar | Ahhh!
- entry[092] 0x17bc-0x180c: Cleo | Doesn't it feel like the tremors are getting stronger?
- entry[093] 0x180c-0x18a1: Orphen | Yeah, bad news. I don't know what this | evil spirit is, but if we don't get that | music machine fixed, we'll be swimming home!
- entry[094] 0x18a1-0x18f9: Cleo | But we don't even know how to fix the | music machine. What do we do?
- entry[095] 0x18f9-0x1933: Mar | Couldn't we use the Crystal Egg somehow?
- entry[096] 0x1933-0x1970: Cleo | Yeah, good idea! | I forgot about the egg.
- entry[097] 0x1970-0x19d0: Orphen | Only one problem... | We don't know the first thing | about how to use it.
- entry[098] 0x19d0-0x19fb: Cleo | But we can ask somebody!
- entry[099] 0x19fb-0x1a39: Orphen | Hah... | Well, I guess it's worth a shot.
- entry[100] 0x1a39-0x1a88: Sephy | I'm so lonely... | Won't you come with me, Orphen?
- entry[101] 0x1a88-0x1ad0: Magnus | Master, have you forgotten? | We've got a job to do.
- entry[102] 0x1ad0-0x1af9: Magnus | What are you doing!?
- entry[103] 0x1af9-0x1b30: Sephy | Oh, thank you! You won't regret it.
- entry[104] 0x1b30-0x1b50: Sephy | Oh, I see...
- entry[105] 0x1b50-0x1bbc: Zeus | Ah, Master Orphen. | It is time for me to leave the island. Will you accompany me?
- entry[106] 0x1bbc-0x1be1: Zeus | Shall we go, then?
- entry[107] 0x1be1-0x1c1c: Zeus | I understand... | Another time, perhaps.
- entry[108] 0x1c1c-0x1c43: Mar | How did it go?
- entry[109] 0x1c43-0x1c78: Orphen | Not good. Nobody knows anything.
- entry[110] 0x1c78-0x1c9f: Orphen | Find out anything?
- entry[111] 0x1c9f-0x1cd7: Cleo | Nothing! | Not a thing. How'd you do?
- entry[112] 0x1cd7-0x1cf6: Orphen | No better.
- entry[113] 0x1cf6-0x1d0e: Cleo | Oh...
- entry[114] 0x1d0e-0x1d48: Cleo | Look! Isn't that the old lady? | Hello!
- entry[115] 0x1d48-0x1da5: Jado | Eh? You people are still here? | Didn't I warn you to get off this island?
- entry[116] 0x1da5-0x1e28: Orphen | Lady, we'd love to get off this rock, but we can't leave just yet. | Are your daughter and granddaughter okay?
- entry[117] 0x1e28-0x1e48: Jado | You met them?
- entry[118] 0x1e48-0x1e62: Orphen | Yeah.
- entry[119] 0x1e62-0x1ee0: Jado | My granddaughter | is worn out from playing so much. | I'm on my way now to try and fix the music machine.
- entry[120] 0x1ee0-0x1f1e: Mar | Is she okay? | Is she going to be all right?
- entry[121] 0x1f1e-0x1f48: Jado | Yes, she's resting now.
- entry[122] 0x1f48-0x1f9f: Orphen | Look, uh, we were thinking | we could help fix the music machine.
- entry[123] 0x1f9f-0x2004: Jado | Thank you, but you can't help me. | I'm not even sure I can put it back together.
- entry[124] 0x2004-0x206d: Cleo | Oh, well. | Maybe we should just bash it | with the Crystal Egg and see what happens.
- entry[125] 0x206d-0x2093: Orphen | Be serious, Cleo.
- entry[126] 0x2093-0x20f3: Jado | What? | What did she say? The Crystal Egg? | You have found the Crystal Egg?
- entry[127] 0x20f3-0x211b: Orphen | Huh? Yeah, here...
- entry[128] 0x211b-0x2194: Jado | Ohhh! | The legendary Crystal Egg! | It really exists... | With this, we can fix the music machine!
- entry[129] 0x2194-0x21d4: Cleo | Huh? | So, you know how | to use that thing?
- entry[130] 0x21d4-0x2238: Jado | It is described in the old legends. | I'm the only one left that knows them now.
- entry[131] 0x2238-0x2279: Orphen | Looks like we're back in business. Let's go!
- entry[132] 0x2279-0x229a: Jado | We must hurry.
- entry[134] 0x229c-0x22cc: Allow Sephy to join your group? | choices: Yes / No
- entry[135] 0x22cc-0x22fb: Allow Zeus to join your group? | choices: Yes / No
- entry[136] 0x22fb-0x2329: Allow Mar to join your group? | choices: Yes / No
- entry[137] 0x2329-0x2344: Mar | Let's go.
- entry[138] 0x2344-0x236d: Orphen | Wait here for a sec.

## scr/scr208.out

Header: pointer_table=0x14a4-0x15bc, footer=0x4b7c, records=69, text_records=68

- entry[000] 0x002c-0x0058: Magnus | Master! | How did it go?
- entry[001] 0x0058-0x008c: Orphen | Nevermind that. Where's Volcan?
- entry[002] 0x008c-0x00ca: Magnus | Huh? | He and Dortin took off after you...
- entry[003] 0x00ca-0x0124: Orphen | Unreal. | Those idiots! | x | They always disappear at the wrong time.
- entry[004] 0x0124-0x017e: Cleo | Come on! | - | That's enough slacking! | \xb4 | Let's go find Volcan and Dortin!
- entry[005] 0x017e-0x01d9: Cleo | Come on! | - | That's enough slacking! | \xb4 | Let's go find Volcan and Dortin!
- entry[006] 0x01d9-0x021c: Orphen | I'm slacking? | d | And just what are you doing?
- entry[007] 0x021c-0x02ab: Cleo | Me? I'm on break. | B R E A K. Break! | You can be such a jerk sometimes. | Who do you think got you this far anyway? Me!
- entry[008] 0x02ab-0x02c6: Orphen | What!?
- entry[009] 0x02c6-0x0308: Orphen | Oh, | hello. | You made it off the ship okay?
- entry[010] 0x0308-0x0398: Sephy | Yes, | < | thanks to Magnus. | \xc8 | We never | had a proper introduction... | x | I'm | \xb4 | called Sephy. | You're | < | Orphen, | x | right?
- entry[011] 0x0398-0x03f3: Orphen | Yep. | < | Magnus told you about me, then? | \xdc | You two came here together?
- entry[012] 0x03f3-0x0426: Sephy | Yes. | You have taught him well.
- entry[013] 0x0426-0x04a2: Sephy | I don't think I would have made | it here without Magnus' help. | I don't know how to ever thank him.
- entry[014] 0x04a2-0x051f: Sephy | I don't think I would have made | it here without Magnus' help. | I don't know how to ever thank him.
- entry[015] 0x051f-0x0579: Orphen | Don't worry about it. He loves | playing the knight in shining armor.
- entry[016] 0x0579-0x05be: Orphen | Ah! | Sephy! | F | You made it off the ship okay?
- entry[017] 0x05be-0x05fe: Sephy | Yes. | I'm happy to see you're safe, Orphen.
- entry[018] 0x05fe-0x0630: Orphen | Did you come here with Magnus?
- entry[019] 0x0630-0x0669: Sephy | Is that tall gentleman | a mercenary?
- entry[020] 0x0669-0x068b: Orphen | Yeah, | he is.
- entry[021] 0x068b-0x06cb: Sephy | I'd feel so much safer | d | if you helped me.
- entry[022] 0x06cb-0x0707: Sephy | Is that young boy | traveling by himself?
- entry[023] 0x0707-0x0729: Orphen | Looks like it.
- entry[024] 0x0729-0x075c: Sephy | Oh... | He must be very lonely.
- entry[025] 0x075c-0x078e: Zeus | Greetings. You were on the ship.
- entry[026] 0x078e-0x07b6: Orphen | You were the one...
- entry[027] 0x07b6-0x0847: Zeus | Yes, | that was me. | Those monsters made | it a bit difficult for me to introduce | myself. | I am called Zeus. | And you are?
- entry[028] 0x0847-0x0882: Orphen | Orphen. I see you survived your fight.
- entry[029] 0x0882-0x08e8: Zeus | Hahaha! It was a short fight, my friend. | But I fear there is more fighting ahead.
- entry[030] 0x08e8-0x091c: Zeus | This island is a dangerous place.
- entry[031] 0x091c-0x0951: Orphen | Yeah, we already found that out.
- entry[032] 0x0951-0x0989: Zeus | Greetings. Master Orphen, was it not?
- entry[033] 0x0989-0x09c2: Orphen | Yes. I see you made it off the ship.
- entry[034] 0x09c2-0x09fd: Zeus | That young lad... Is he traveling alone?
- entry[035] 0x09fd-0x0a21: Orphen | Looks that way.
- entry[036] 0x0a21-0x0a7f: Zeus | My daughter would be about the same age. | Azel... Where are you, daughter?
- entry[037] 0x0a7f-0x0ac7: Zeus | Master Orphen! | You are acquainted with that woman?
- entry[038] 0x0ac7-0x0aed: Orphen | What's it to you?
- entry[039] 0x0aed-0x0b75: Zeus | I would be cautious, | if I were you. | There's more to her | than meets the eye. | Uh-oh. | I must hurry. Good luck!
- entry[040] 0x0b75-0x0c17: Magnus | Master, I heard something interesting. | Apparently, this island has so many caverns | under it, it's practically hollow! Weird, huh?
- entry[041] 0x0c17-0x0c34: Orphen | Really?
- entry[042] 0x0c34-0x0c92: Magnus | Yeah! And there's more mazes and secret | passages than you can dream of!
- entry[043] 0x0c92-0x0cba: Orphen | Sounds interesting.
- entry[044] 0x0cba-0x0d1a: Magnus | If you get lost, you never come back. | I bet there's monsters down there.
- entry[045] 0x0d1a-0x0d75: Orphen | Or, it could be Paradise. Once you find | it, you don't want to leave.
- entry[046] 0x0d75-0x0d9c: Magnus | Really? Paradise!?
- entry[047] 0x0d9c-0x0deb: Orphen | No, I'm kidding! | There's no such thing as Paradise, kid.
- entry[048] 0x0deb-0x0e1d: Magnus | Aw, don't tease me like that.
- entry[049] 0x0e1d-0x0e51: Orphen | Can I ask you something?
- entry[050] 0x0e51-0x0e68: Mar | What?
- entry[051] 0x0e68-0x0ec8: Orphen | That music, you're sure it's your mom? | Couldn't it just be a common song?
- entry[052] 0x0ec8-0x0f5f: Mar | I don't know. | I can't explain it, but I can feel it. | Every since I got here, somebody, | no, | something has been calling to me.
- entry[053] 0x0f5f-0x0f9e: Orphen | Do you know how to use Crystal Egg?
- entry[054] 0x0f9e-0x0ff2: Magnus | Cleo asked me the same thing. | I don't know anything about it.
- entry[055] 0x0ff2-0x107a: Orphen | Oh, well. | Magnus, listen to me: | This island might sink under the sea. | You need to be ready to escape, okay?
- entry[056] 0x107a-0x10d0: Magnus | What!? What are you talking about? | The island is going to sink?
- entry[057] 0x10d0-0x112d: Orphen | Calm down! | We're gonna do what we can to | stop that from happening.
- entry[058] 0x112d-0x1166: Magnus | Master, is it going to be all right?
- entry[059] 0x1166-0x119f: Magnus | Master, is it going to be all right?
- entry[060] 0x119f-0x11be: Orphen | I hope so.
- entry[061] 0x11be-0x1201: Orphen | You know anything about the Crysal Egg?
- entry[062] 0x1201-0x1248: Sephy | A little boy just asked me that | question, but...
- entry[063] 0x1248-0x12c1: Orphen | You don't know, eh? | He told you about the danger, right? | Get off this island as soon as you can.
- entry[064] 0x12c1-0x1330: Sephy | Yes, I'm planning to leave soon. | I guess I came all this way for nothing. | Oh, Rufus...
- entry[065] 0x1330-0x1398: Zeus | Miss Cleo asked me the same question. | Unfortunately, I've never heard of it.
- entry[066] 0x1398-0x1416: Orphen | Oh, well. | Oh, yeah... | You probably already know this, but this | island might be in serious trouble.
- entry[067] 0x1416-0x14a1: Zeus | I was planning leaving the island | soon in any case. | It seems that the daughter I was | searching for was never here.

## scr/scr211.out

Header: pointer_table=0x033c-0x0378, footer=0x1fbc, records=14, text_records=12

- entry[001] 0x0035-0x004d: Magnus | Huh?
- entry[002] 0x004d-0x0078: Cleo | Orphen, | what happened?
- entry[003] 0x0078-0x0101: Orphen | Something weird | is going on. | I'm starting to hate being moved around | from place to place without being asked.
- entry[004] 0x0101-0x0139: Cleo | What are | we doing | here, | Orphen?
- entry[005] 0x0139-0x017d: Orphen | It seems that we have been | trapped all along.
- entry[006] 0x017d-0x01c5: Magnus | Trapped!? | What have we been trapped by, | Master?
- entry[007] 0x01c5-0x01ed: Cleo | What | is that thing?
- entry[008] 0x01ed-0x022a: Magnus | It looks | like a map | of Chaos Island.
- entry[009] 0x022a-0x026b: Cleo | I think it's showing the position of something.
- entry[010] 0x026b-0x028e: Orphen | Gaia probably.
- entry[011] 0x028e-0x02a8: Magnus | What?
- entry[012] 0x02a8-0x033b: Orphen | The person or thing | that has us in its web... | It must be at this location. | All we can do is | go there and see what we find.

## scr/scr212.out

Header: pointer_table=0x05cc-0x0624, footer=0x2944, records=20, text_records=18

- entry[001] 0x0035-0x00df: I've been waiting | for you, Orphen. | I am | imprisoned by the chains | of Eternity. | You | have not | completed | your mission | yet...
- entry[002] 0x00df-0x011f: I will | keep on | waiting | for you | Orphen.
- entry[003] 0x011f-0x0145: You are my only hope. | Save me.
- entry[005] 0x014d-0x0185: Orphen | What the!? | Who | are they?
- entry[006] 0x0185-0x01c4: Rani | Three souls who have completed their mission.
- entry[007] 0x01c4-0x01fc: Orphen | What do you mean? | What | are you?
- entry[008] 0x01fc-0x0271: Rani | You received something | from the three that sleep before you now. | The key to unlock the truth.
- entry[009] 0x0271-0x0296: Orphen | Unlock the truth?
- entry[010] 0x0296-0x02f5: Rani | Orphen, | Your final mission for me is to free | us from Eternity, forever.
- entry[011] 0x02f5-0x0329: Orphen | Hold on! | I don't understand...
- entry[012] 0x0329-0x0387: Rani | Have you not | sensed that you, too, | are trapped by the hand of Eternity?
- entry[013] 0x0387-0x03a0: Orphen | What?
- entry[014] 0x03a0-0x03ff: Rani | Why is it that you know the story | of each of those that sleeps before you?
- entry[015] 0x03ff-0x04d0: Orphen | !! | You're right... | I met each of them on the ship, and went | on a weird little | adventure with them. | As soon as one | adventure ended, | there I was, | back on the ship again...
- entry[016] 0x04d0-0x0529: Orphen | Son of a... | Hey! | What the hell kind of a | scam is this, anyway??
- entry[017] 0x0529-0x058f: Rani | Orphen, | I beg you! | Find | the... | truth... | You must... | destroy... | Gaia.
- entry[018] 0x058f-0x05b8: Orphen | Gaia? | What's Gaia?
- entry[019] 0x05b8-0x05cc: Orphen | Wait!

## scr/scr213.out

Header: pointer_table=0x1e9c-0x20b4, footer=0xa5bc, records=132, text_records=128

- entry[001] 0x0034-0x005d: Orphen | Cleo, | We're back.
- entry[002] 0x005d-0x0093: Magnus | Cleo, | what's wrong | with Rani?
- entry[003] 0x0093-0x00d3: Cleo | Her condition | grew worse all of a sudden...
- entry[004] 0x00d3-0x00ed: Orphen | What!?
- entry[005] 0x00ed-0x010a: Orphen | Poor kid.
- entry[006] 0x010a-0x015d: Jado | Oh... | Rani! | Talk to me, Rani. | Smile like you did before.
- entry[007] 0x015d-0x0194: Cleo | Hey! | Did you find the Crystal Egg?
- entry[008] 0x0194-0x01b4: Magnus | Oh, | yeah.
- entry[009] 0x01b4-0x0219: Magnus | Grandma Jado! | Everything's gonna be okay. | We found the Crystal Egg. | Here!
- entry[010] 0x0219-0x0259: Jado | Oh! | I - I - I thought it was just a legend.
- entry[011] 0x0259-0x02af: Orphen | Lady, | that's the real deal. | Now you can cure the little girl.
- entry[012] 0x02af-0x02e1: Jado | Oh, | you're right. | But, ah...
- entry[013] 0x02e1-0x02fe: Orphen | But what?
- entry[014] 0x02fe-0x0332: Orphen | Oh, | wait. | Don't | tell me...
- entry[015] 0x0332-0x0372: Magnus | You don't know how | to use the Crystal Egg?
- entry[016] 0x0372-0x0447: Jado | No, | I'm sorry. | The legend of the Crystal Egg | has been passed down in my family for generations. | But I've never actually seen it before. | It should have the power to save her, though.
- entry[017] 0x0447-0x045d: Cleo | Huh?
- entry[018] 0x045d-0x048b: Orphen | You | gotta be kidding me!
- entry[019] 0x048b-0x04c7: Magnus | We went through all that... For nothing?
- entry[020] 0x04c7-0x0561: Jado | I can't | help her now. | It's the will of the Gods. | At least | we'll be together in the next life. | We haven't got | long to wait.
- entry[021] 0x0561-0x058e: Cleo | What are you talking about?
- entry[022] 0x058e-0x05d1: Rufus | I can break the spell | Quaris has cast on her.
- entry[023] 0x05d1-0x05f7: Cleo | Huh? | Who's | that?
- entry[024] 0x05f7-0x063f: Magnus | Rufus. | Quaris experimented | on Rufus | as well.
- entry[025] 0x063f-0x067f: Jado | Oh... | You have the same | sickness as Rani?
- entry[026] 0x067f-0x06a0: Sephy | Are you sure?
- entry[027] 0x06a0-0x072a: Rufus | Yes. | I know more about the | Crystal Egg than anyone alive. | But | I must start immediately. There isn't much time.
- entry[028] 0x072a-0x075a: Rufus | Everyone, | please step back.
- entry[029] 0x075a-0x0776: Sephy | Rufus...
- entry[030] 0x0776-0x07b5: Rufus | Sephy, | thank you. | I'm glad | you came.
- entry[031] 0x07b5-0x07d3: Sephy | !? | Rufus?
- entry[032] 0x07d3-0x081a: Rufus | Oh Guardian of Time! | Guide me to the True Path!!
- entry[033] 0x081a-0x083e: Magnus | Look! | The Egg!
- entry[034] 0x083e-0x0890: Orphen | What the? | Magic? | No! | It's something... | different...
- entry[036] 0x0899-0x08b8: Jado | Ohh! | Rani!
- entry[037] 0x08b8-0x08d4: Cleo | He did it!
- entry[038] 0x08d4-0x08f6: Rani | Am I... | cured?
- entry[039] 0x08f6-0x0948: Jado | These young people | found the Crystal Egg and saved your life.
- entry[040] 0x0948-0x0963: Sephy | Rufus!!!
- entry[041] 0x0963-0x0986: Sephy | Why? Why Rufus?
- entry[042] 0x0986-0x09eb: Rufus | Sephy... | Thank you so much | for coming. | It's okay... | It's my time to go.
- entry[043] 0x09eb-0x0a1f: Sephy | No... | Don't say that, Rufus...
- entry[044] 0x0a1f-0x0a3d: Magnus | Master...
- entry[045] 0x0a3d-0x0a6d: Orphen | We must let them be, Magnus.
- entry[046] 0x0a6d-0x0b21: Sephy | Rufus. | You | knew, didn't you? | You knew the Crystal Egg | could only be used once before it shattered. | You always did | have to have things your way...
- entry[047] 0x0b21-0x0bca: Rufus | You understand me too well. | I never thought I'd get to see you again. | I will die a happy man. | I just wish you didn't have to see me like this.
- entry[048] 0x0bca-0x0c71: Sephy | Oh, | Rufus... | Your eyes sparkle as brightly now | as they did when you were young. | I'll never | forget how you | used to tell me stories .
- entry[049] 0x0c71-0x0d12: Rufus | I went | all over the world, trying to find treasure. | Now, I've | finally found a real treasure. | Sephy, | you were my one true treasure.
- entry[050] 0x0d12-0x0d2d: Sephy | Rufus...
- entry[051] 0x0d2d-0x0d60: Rufus | Sephy... | I love...
- entry[052] 0x0d60-0x0d8c: Sephy | Rufus! | Rufus!! | [sobs]
- entry[053] 0x0d8c-0x0dd9: Rani | I don't understand. | Why didn't he save himself?
- entry[055] 0x0de2-0x0dfd: Magnus | Master!
- entry[056] 0x0dfd-0x0e55: Orphen | Jado is dead. | She was an evil old witch right up to the end, too.
- entry[057] 0x0e55-0x0e6c: Zeus | Azel!
- entry[058] 0x0e6c-0x0e8e: Rani | Do you have it!?
- entry[059] 0x0e8e-0x0eba: Cleo | We brought it. | Here...
- entry[060] 0x0eba-0x0ed4: Rani | The Egg!
- entry[061] 0x0ed4-0x0f05: Zeus | Can you... | Can you save her?
- entry[062] 0x0f05-0x0f8d: Rani | I remember the words Jado chanted before. | I think they must be | the keywords you need to activate the Crystal Egg.
- entry[063] 0x0f8d-0x0fbf: Orphen | You think? | You're not sure?
- entry[064] 0x0fbf-0x0fff: Rani | Not really. | I can't promise it will work...
- entry[065] 0x0fff-0x10b4: Zeus | It it | be the will of the Gods to take my daughter, | then so be it. | You will have our | thanks for trying. | No one will blame you. | I beg you, | please try.
- entry[066] 0x10b4-0x10f3: Rani | I understand. | Zeus... | I'll do | my best.
- entry[067] 0x10f3-0x110f: Zeus | Thank you.
- entry[068] 0x110f-0x1138: Rani | Everyone, | step back.
- entry[069] 0x1138-0x1198: Crystal Egg! | Please! | Free Azel from | the time spell! | Kellu | Rahpat | Gabar!
- entry[070] 0x1198-0x11c0: Cleo | Nothing's happening.
- entry[071] 0x11c0-0x11df: Rani | But, but...
- entry[072] 0x11df-0x11f8: Rani | Ahhh!
- entry[073] 0x11f8-0x121e: Magnus | Look at the Egg!
- entry[074] 0x121e-0x1243: Orphen | She activated it!
- entry[075] 0x1243-0x125a: Rani | Ah...
- entry[076] 0x125a-0x1296: Orphen | So this is the power of the Crystal Egg?
- entry[077] 0x1296-0x12af: Zeus | Azel...
- entry[078] 0x12af-0x12e2: Rani | It worked! | Azel, are you okay?
- entry[079] 0x12e2-0x1312: Azel | I think so. | What happened?
- entry[080] 0x1312-0x1369: Rani | These people | helped cure you. | You're going to be all right now.
- entry[081] 0x1369-0x1382: Azel | Ah...
- entry[082] 0x1382-0x13a1: Zeus | Err, | ah...
- entry[083] 0x13a1-0x13e3: Cleo | What are you waiting for? | This is your chance!
- entry[084] 0x13e3-0x1436: Zeus | Yes, | of course. | It's | just that | I don't know what to say.
- entry[085] 0x1436-0x1455: Cleo | Come | here.
- entry[086] 0x1455-0x1482: Zeus | Eh!? | I... | couldn't....
- entry[087] 0x1482-0x14a7: Cleo | Right, | good luck!
- entry[088] 0x14a7-0x14bd: Azel | . . . . . . .
- entry[089] 0x14bd-0x14ec: Zeus | . . . . . . . | Ah - | Azel.
- entry[090] 0x14ec-0x150f: Azel | . . . | . | Yes?
- entry[091] 0x150f-0x1535: Cleo | He's gonna blow it.
- entry[092] 0x1535-0x1584: Zeus | I want... | That is... | I'm sorry if I | caused you pain.
- entry[093] 0x1584-0x15a1: Azel | It's okay.
- entry[094] 0x15a1-0x1606: Zeus | . . . | Azel... | What I want to say is | that I... | I... | love you very much.
- entry[095] 0x1606-0x164c: Orphen | Uh-ha. | You told him to say that, | didn't you?
- entry[096] 0x164c-0x1690: Cleo | Isn't it great!? | Father and daughter reunited!
- entry[097] 0x1690-0x16b5: Orphen | Ugh. | Spare me.
- entry[098] 0x16b5-0x16fa: Magnus | It's the most | beautiful thing I've ever seen!
- entry[099] 0x16fa-0x1726: Orphen | You two | make me sick!
- entry[100] 0x1726-0x17f9: Zeus | Well, | I guess | there's not much | use in me trying to explain | how I feel about my own child. | Azel, | Just seeing you alive | and well has | made this | foolish old man | very happy.
- entry[101] 0x17f9-0x1812: Azel | Daddy!
- entry[102] 0x1812-0x1843: Daddy! | I missed you! | ... Daddy!
- entry[103] 0x1843-0x1856: Zeus | Azel...
- entry[105] 0x185f-0x188e: Mar | I did it. | Now she'll be...
- entry[106] 0x188e-0x18b9: Cleo | Hang on! | Oh, | Mar...
- entry[107] 0x18b9-0x191e: Mar | It's okay. | This is what I came here for. | Thank you | for everything, | Cleo...
- entry[108] 0x191e-0x1949: Cleo | Please | don't thank me.
- entry[109] 0x1949-0x1961: Quaris | Mar!
- entry[110] 0x1961-0x197c: Mar | Mother...
- entry[111] 0x197c-0x19c2: Quaris | I'm sorry. | I'm so sorry. | Can you forgive me?
- entry[112] 0x19c2-0x19fc: Mar | Mother... | I finally | found | you.
- entry[113] 0x19fc-0x1a6a: Quaris | I did it so you, | at least, wouldn't have to | share our cursed destiny. | Oh, my baby...
- entry[114] 0x1a6a-0x1a82: Rani | Mar...
- entry[115] 0x1a82-0x1aae: Mar | You're my sister, | right?
- entry[116] 0x1aae-0x1afa: Rani | What you did has proved that | more than any words could.
- entry[117] 0x1afa-0x1b36: Mar | I didn't want you | to suffer | any more.
- entry[118] 0x1b36-0x1b52: Rani | Oh Mar...
- entry[119] 0x1b52-0x1c44: Jado | I was the one that said you should be given up | for adoption. | Quaris didn't want to, of course, but | I forced her to do it. | I'm sorry Mar! | I know it must have | been hard to be without | a mother. | Forgive me!
- entry[120] 0x1c44-0x1c91: Mar | It's okay, | Grandma. | I'm glad I | came back. | Orphen.
- entry[121] 0x1c91-0x1ca8: Orphen | Eh?
- entry[122] 0x1ca8-0x1d14: Orphen | I don't believe in | all that fancy destiny talk, kid. | You're going to fine, damn it!
- entry[123] 0x1d14-0x1d40: Mar | Please, | take this for me.
- entry[124] 0x1d40-0x1d5f: Orphen | Your flute?
- entry[125] 0x1d5f-0x1da2: Mar | I think | it wants to be with you now, | Orphen.
- entry[126] 0x1da2-0x1de0: Orphen | Don't be stupid! | You love that flute!!
- entry[127] 0x1de0-0x1df9: Quaris | Mar!!
- entry[128] 0x1df9-0x1e11: Orphen | Ahh!
- entry[129] 0x1e11-0x1e29: Cleo | Mar...
- entry[130] 0x1e29-0x1e4e: Jado | Oh-oh-oh-oh [weeps]
- entry[131] 0x1e4e-0x1e9c: Rani | Mar! | No! | Not now! You can't leave me alone! | It's not fair!
